Abstract: Ask a language model the same question about the same document twenty times, and it sometimes returns two different answers. We built Probity, a benchmark of 60 tasks and 470 items from real venture-financing filings, to measure how often this happens. Then we audited our own corpus and found a defect any excerpt-built benchmark can carry: items whose evidence is missing from the window of text the model is shown. The audit flags 36 items and separates two failures a single flag would conflate: evidence genuinely absent from the window and answers that must be computed from numbers the window does supply. Flagged items change their answers far more often, wobbling at 0.255 against 0.087 on the 427 clean items, and excluding them cuts apparent cross-model agreement by about a fifth. Before testing whether the missing evidence explains the instability, we registered a prediction: re-cut each window to hold its evidence, and instability should fall below a set threshold. It failed: the repair moved wobble by 0.058, with an interval containing zero. We report the association as correlational. Almost all measurements sit where instability cannot show, which bounds what a corpus built for accuracy can say about stability. We release the corpus, all 112,800 raw responses, and the audit as a runnable check for any document benchmark.

</details>


### [133] [SLATE: Are AI-Generated Slides Educationally Effective? A Benchmark for Language Teaching Quality and Learner Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06212)
*Jingzhuo Wu,Jiajun Zhang,Liu Yi,Leqi Zheng,Yuheng Jing,Xinyuan Zhou,Quan yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SLATE，首个通过教学效果和学习者知识获取来评估AI生成语言教学幻灯片的基准，揭示内容质量与教学有效性之间的脱节，呼吁生成式教学系统构建范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs生成的语言教学幻灯片在视觉上日益精美，但实际教学效果与视觉质量之间存在严重不匹配；现有评估多关注表面质量而非学习者实际知识获取，缺乏能够隔离"先验知识泄漏"并真正衡量教学有效性的基准。

Method: SLATE将来自低网络存在感语言的奥林匹克语言学谜题转化为90个标准化教学单元（含1133个可评估项），配以结构化课程大纲和匹配的近迁移/远迁移测试集。采用前测-后测设计消除预训练知识泄漏，利用VLMs作为可扩展的学习者代理，并以三系统人类试点提供方向性支持。

Result: 内容效度与学习增益仅呈弱关联，而教学设计（pedagogical design）与学习增益呈强正相关；多数系统在近迁移与远迁移准确率之间存在显著差距，甚至前沿模型也可能产生负学习增益。

Conclusion: SLATE揭示了工件质量与教学有效性之间的分离现象，暗示当前生成式教学系统的构建、评估和部署方式需要范式层面的转变，应将教学有效性置于视觉精度之上。

Abstract: LLMs have achieved remarkable capabilities in generating language teaching slides. However, a critical mismatch persists between visual polish and actual instructional effectiveness. To address this gap, we introduce SLATE (Slide-based Learning Assessment for Teaching Effectiveness), the first benchmark that evaluates AI-generated language teaching slides through instructional effectiveness and learner knowledge acquisition. SLATE transforms linguistics olympiad puzzles from low-resource languages with negligible web presence into 90 standardized instructional units comprising 1,133 assessable items, paired with a structured course outline and matched near- and far-transfer test sets. This pretest-posttest design eliminates pretrained knowledge leakage, ensuring gains reflect learning rather than prior recall. Using VLMs as scalable learner proxies and directionally supported by a three-system human pilot, our results show that content validity exhibits a weak association with learning gain, while pedagogical design exhibits a robust positive association. Moreover, most systems show a significant gap between near- and far-transfer accuracy, and even frontier models can produce negative learning gains. SLATE reveals a dissociation between artifact quality and instructional effectiveness, calling for a paradigm shift in how generative teaching systems are built, evaluated, and deployed.

</details>


### [134] [Beyond the Flag: Clinical Framing Closes the Moderation Gap in Suicide Risk Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06263)
*Shreyas Krishnan,Gun Ahn,Jungjin Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出，现有的内容审核API本质是二分类标志工具而非临床风险分级测量工具，而平台对被动困扰与主动规划（含手段可用）的回应义务截然不同，法规（如加州SB 243）已将此差异化回应上升为合规要求。作者发布了516条r/SuicideWatch帖子的基准数据集，由持证精神科医生按基于Columbia-Suicide Severity Rating Scale的四级有序量表标注，并在七种有序感知指标下评估了审核API、提示LLM和监督基线。主要发现：厂商审核API在区分高低风险上表现良好（高危F1 0.860）但严重度测量差（宏观F1 0.395），系统性高估最严重类别；临床指导的零样本提示弥补大部分差距（宏观F1 0.562），关键杠杆是专家撰写的框架而非微调、额外推理或朴素的multi-agent聚合；推理的价值取决于语体：在冗长嘈杂的Reddit帖子上有害，在简短的精神科医生撰写的陈述上有帮助。作者主张分级严重度（而非二元标志）才是相称的注意义务所需，并开源评估框架以支持该测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台在内容审核中的责任不止于检测违规内容，还须根据临床风险严重程度提供差异化回应。已有的审核API设计目标是返回威胁性分类的二元标志，无法胜任临床分级严重度的测量需求。新兴法规正在将这种分级义务转化为合规要求，但缺乏针对"已部署安全信号是否能恢复临床意义的严重度"的系统基准评估。

Method: 方法包含三个方面：(1) 构建基准数据集：516条r/SuicideWatch帖子，由持证精神科医生按四级别有序严重度量表（Indicator, Ideation, Behavior, Attempt）标注，框架源于Columbia Suicide Severity Rating Scale；(2) 定义七种面向有序标签的评估指标；(3) 系统对比三类模型——厂商审核API、提示LLM、监督基线——以评估其恢复临床严重度层级的能力，并消融分析框架来源、推理、微调及multi-agent聚合等因素的影响。

Result: 三个核心发现：(1) 厂商审核API擅长区分低与高严重度（高危F1为0.860），但严重度分级测量不佳（宏观F1仅0.395），且系统性过度预测最高严重度等级；(2) 临床框架指导下的零样本提示可将宏观F1从0.395提升至0.562，接近弥补大部分性能差距，其中专家撰写的提示框架是核心有效的杠杆，而微调、追加推理或朴素的multi-agent聚合均不能带来同等提升；(3) 额外推理的效果取决于内容语体——在冗长嘈杂的Reddit帖子上有害，而在简短、临床医生撰写的陈述上有益。

Conclusion: 论文主张相称的注意义务（proportionate duty of care）需要的不是二元风险标志而是分级严重度测量，当前的厂商审核API在该维度上存在显著缺陷。作者发布基准数据集与评估框架，以支持并推动"已部署安全信号能否恢复临床意义严重度"这一测量目标的研究与合规实践。

Abstract: Moderation APIs are built to flag policy-violating content, not to measure graded clinical risk. But a platform's duty does not end at detection: the response owed to passive distress differs sharply from the response owed to active planning with means access, and emerging regulation (e.g., California Senate Bill 243) is turning that distinction into a compliance requirement. We therefore ask how well deployed safety signals recover clinically meaningful severity. We release a benchmark of 516 r/SuicideWatch posts rated by a licensed psychiatrist on a four-level ordinal schema (Indicator, Ideation, Behavior, Attempt) grounded in the Columbia Suicide Severity Rating Scale, and evaluate moderation APIs, prompted LLMs, and supervised baselines under seven ordinal-aware metrics. Three findings. Vendor moderation APIs separate low- from high-severity posts well (0.860 high-risk F1) but measure severity poorly (0.395 macro F1), systematically over-predicting the most severe category. Clinically grounded zero-shot prompting recovers much of that gap (0.562 macro F1), and expert-authored framing (not fine-tuning, added reasoning, or naive multi-agent aggregation) is the effective lever. The value of reasoning depends on register: it hurts on long, noisy Reddit posts and helps on short, clinician-authored statements. We argue graded severity, not a binary flag, is what a proportionate duty of care requires, and release our evaluation framework to support that measurement.

</details>


### [135] [Correction as Annotation: Bootstrapping a Dependency Parser for Documentary Medieval Latin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06266)
*Gabriel H. Pizzorno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对中世纪拉丁语文献在NLP工具上表现不佳的问题，提出了一种通过"模型预标注-专家纠正-再训练"的迭代式领域内数据生成方法。该方法在马尔赛登记的160份中世纪清单上，仅用33小时和1,804句标注就将UDPOS准确率从0.80提升至0.98、LA分数从0.48提升至0.92，超越了所有基线，且训练数据用量比最大基线少97%。还提出了标注者工作量作为无需金标准的停止准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现存的五种拉丁语树库模型在中世纪文献（马尔赛1258-1446年间的160份财产清单）上表现不佳，最佳LA分数仅0.62、形态学感知分数仅0.24，且性能与语料体裁和年代接近程度均无相关性。中世纪文献的NLP处理需求未能得到满足。

Method: 采用九轮迭代式主动学习（但无主动学习选择策略）：每轮使用当前模型对200个句子进行预标注，由领域专家纠正标注，纠正后的数据加入训练集训练下一轮模型。批次独立于模型状态采样，不进行主动学习选择。标注者负工作量的下降（从54%的token降至14-18%的平台期）作为停止准则。

Result: 33小时标注投入、1,804句训练数据：UDPOS准确率从0.80升至0.98，LA分数从0.48升至0.92，超越所有基线；训练数据量比最大基线少97%。标注者工作量从54%的token显著下降至14-18%的平台期。

Conclusion: 该迭代式领域内标注方法可有效克服中世纪特定类型文献的NLP适配难题，97%更少的数据即可达成最优性能。标注者工作量可作为无需独立金标准的实用停止准则，为缺乏高质量标注数据的低资源历史语言领域提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Medieval documentary sources remain inadequately served by existing natural language processing tools. None of the five readily available Latin treebank models attains usable performance on a collection of 160 inventories compiled in Marseille between 1258 and 1446. The best labelled attachment score is 0.62 and the best morphology-aware score is 0.24. Performance does not correlate with either genre or period proximity. To address this shortfall, in-domain training data was generated as a by-product of using these inadequate models. In each of nine iterations, a model pre-annotated 200 sentences; an expert corrected the annotations; and the corrected sentences were used to train the subsequent model, with batches sampled independently of model state, without active-learning selection. Thirty-three hours of annotation effort over 1,804 sentences increased universal part-of-speech accuracy from 0.80 to 0.98 and labelled attachment from 0.48 to 0.92, outperforming all baselines on the reported metrics while using 97% less training data than the largest one of them. Annotator effort declined from 54% of tokens to a plateau of 14-18%, an operational progress metric that requires no separate gold standard and can serve as a stopping criterion.

</details>


### [136] [Steering Geometry: Validating Human Value Geometry in LLM Steering Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06289)
*Mohammad Mahdi Abootorabi,Armin Saghafian,Ali Bazshoushtari,Hamid Rezaei,EunJeong Hwang,Vered Shwartz,Parvin Mousavi,Purang Abolmaesumi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM激活steering向量的潜在几何结构是否反映了人类价值观与道德的理论结构（基于Schwartz基本人类价值理论），引入26K样本、覆盖20种人类价值的基准，对比分布驱动与行为中心两类steering方法，发现分布驱动方法能恢复与理论预测一致的价值拓扑结构（Spearman ρ高达0.51），而行为中心方法虽性能相当但与理论几何相关性低；几何保真度随模型规模提升但在指令微调后下降，且更好的几何对齐能带来更符合人类直觉的跨价值迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常在孤立行为上验证steering方法，不清楚steering向量是否编码了连贯的语义结构，抑或只是利用了行为特有的捷径。作者希望探究steering向量的潜在几何是否反映理论规定的人类价值观与道德结构，从而加深对steering机制的理解。

Method: 以Schwartz基本人类价值理论为主要细粒度框架，构建覆盖20种人类价值的26K样本基准；分析分布驱动方法（CAA、SphericalSteer、ODESteer）与行为中心方法（COLD-Steer、BiPO），在多种模型族与规模上评估其steering性能及与理论价值几何的一致性。

Result: 分布驱动方法能恢复与理论预测一致的人类价值拓扑结构（Spearman ρ高达0.51，p<10^-13）；行为中心方法steering性能相当但与预期价值几何相关性很低；几何保真度随模型规模提升但在指令微调后下降；几何对齐更好的方法在跨价值迁移中更符合人类一致性——正确steering某价值观会上扬兼容价值并抑制对立价值。

Conclusion: 分布驱动steering方法在潜在空间中编码了与人类价值理论相一致的语义结构，而行为中心方法缺乏这种结构；模型规模与训练方式影响几何保真度；几何对齐程度与跨价值迁移的人类一致性密切相关，为steering方法在价值观控制场景下的适用性提供了理论指导。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in alignment-sensitive contexts, activation steering has emerged as a lightweight, inference-time alternative to fine-tuning methods (e.g., RLHF, DPO) for behavioral control. However, existing work typically validates steering on isolated behaviors, leaving it unclear whether steering vectors encode coherent semantic structure or merely exploit behavior-specific shortcuts. We investigate whether the latent geometry of LLM steering vectors reflects theory-specified structure in human values and morality. Using Schwartz's Theory of Basic Human Values as our primary fine-grained framework, we introduce a 26K-sample benchmark covering 20 human values and analyze distribution-driven methods (e.g., CAA, SphericalSteer, ODESteer) and behavior-centric approaches (e.g., COLD-Steer, BiPO) across diverse model families and sizes. We find that distribution-driven methods recover human value topologies aligned with theoretical predictions (Spearman $ρ$ up to 0.51, $p < 10^{-13}$). In contrast, behavior-centric methods achieve comparable steering performance but show little correlation with the expected value geometry. Geometric fidelity improves with model scale but drops after instruction tuning. Finally, better geometric alignment also leads to more human-consistent transfer across values: steering one value correctly lifts compatible values and suppresses opposing ones. Code and data are available at: https://github.com/DeepRCL/Steering_Geometry.

</details>


### [137] [Reliability, validity, and diagnostic evidence for multi-model LLM short-answer scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06315)
*Chunyi Zhao,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究使用996份SciEntsBank简答题回答，评估了GPT、DeepSeek和Qwen三种大语言模型在五个OCG-PRES维度（概念覆盖、关系准确性、推理完整性、矛盾控制、领域相关性）上的重复多轮评分。结果显示各模型重复运行信度高（ICC从.977到.992），GPT在官方标签对齐方面表现最佳（AUC=.909），OCG-PRES评分优于所有非LLM基线，支持将LLM作为评分辅助工具而非人评替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于教育评分，但单一的模型和单次运行评估无法为大规模教育测评提供充分证据。简答题评分需要关于信度、效度、评分严格度、诊断价值和失败案例的多方面证据，而现有研究缺乏对这种系统性的、重复多模型评估的探讨。

Method: 采用OCG-PRES引导的LLM评分框架，对996份SciEntsBank回答进行评分。三种模型（GPT、DeepSeek、Qwen）分别在三次独立运行中对每个回答按五个OCG-PRES维度评分（概念覆盖、关系准确性、推理完整性、矛盾控制、领域相关性）。评分结果与官方二分类和五分类标签及非LLM基线（回答长度、Jaccard关键词重叠、TF-IDF余弦相似度和结合传统逻辑回归模型）进行比较分析。

Result: 各模型重复运行信度均高：GPT的ICC(3,k)=.977，DeepSeek=.992，Qwen=.981。DeepSeek跨运行最稳定。GPT在官方标签对齐上表现最佳（AUC=.909）；Qwen评分更严格，在固定阈值=3.0规则下精度更高但召回率较低。OCG-PRES分数在五个官方类别中呈现预期诊断模式，在AUC和F1上均优于所有非LLM基线。

Conclusion: 重复多模型OCG-PRES评分能为LLM辅助简答题评分提供信度、效度和诊断性证据。研究结果支持在谨慎和循证基础上将LLM用作评分支持工具，而非取代人类评判。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used or proposed for educational scoring, but single-model and single-run evaluations provide limited evidence for assessment use. Short-answer scoring requires evidence about reliability, validity, severity, diagnostic value, and failure cases. This study evaluated repeated multi-model OCG-PRES guided LLM scoring for short-answer assessment. The analysis used 996 SciEntsBank responses. GPT, DeepSeek, and Qianwen each scored every response across three independent runs using five OCG-PRES dimensions: concept coverage, relation accuracy, reasoning completeness, contradiction control, and domain relevance. Scores were evaluated against official binary and five-category labels and compared with non-LLM baselines based on answer length, Jaccard keyword overlap, TF-IDF cosine similarity, and a combined traditional logistic model. Repeated-run reliability was high for all models, with ICC(3,k) = .977 for GPT, .992 for DeepSeek, and .981 for Qianwen. DeepSeek was the most stable across runs. GPT showed the strongest official-label alignment by AUC (.909), while Qianwen was stricter, with higher precision but lower recall under the fixed threshold = 3.0 rule. OCG-PRES scores followed expected diagnostic patterns across five official categories and outperformed all non-LLM baselines in AUC and F1. Repeated multi-model OCG-PRES scoring provides reliability, validity, and diagnostic evidence for LLM-assisted short-answer scoring. The findings support cautious, evidence-based use as a scoring support tool rather than a replacement for human judgement.

</details>


### [138] [A Ticket from Marginals to Joints: Coupled-Noise Distillation for One-Step Block Generation in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06324)
*Lin Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CONDOR（Coupled-Noise Distillation for One-Step Readout），一种通过耦合噪声蒸馏将扩散语言模型的多步去噪压缩为单步前向生成的方法。其核心思路是让噪声条件教师模型与单步学生模型在相同噪声场下协同训练，使学生能够在一个前向传播中提交一整块连贯的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型每一步只生成一个token，扩散语言模型虽然可以分步生成一整块token但需要多次前向传播。作者探究能否在单个前向传播中提交一整块token。然而现有噪声条件掩码去噪模型的训练方式（如winner-take-all或重要性加权使多个噪声场竞争）给噪声仅提供了粗略控制，其携带的信息量随竞争场数对数增长，且一步输出在多模型规模下很少保持连贯性。

Method: 作者提出CONDOR：首先训练一个噪声条件教师模型（使用随机数量的掩码位置并结合winner-take-all策略）；随后学生模型一步提出一整块token并保留所选token，然后教师在相同噪声场下通过多步迭代补全其他位置，学生从这一补全结果中学习；此外加入了一个无噪声的掩码语言模型项（基于真实文本）来锚定学生模型，防止其偏离ground truth。

Result: 在TinyStories数据集上的人类评估显示，CONDOR在一步生成合法性的指标上取得了大幅提升，同时不同噪声场仍能产生不同的输出块，且每块仅需一次前向传播。

Conclusion: CONDOR成功实现了通过单次前向传播提交一整块连贯token的目标，克服了传统噪声竞争训练方式下噪声仅能提供粗略控制、无法保证一步输出连贯性的局限，同时保留了对不同噪声场的多样性响应能力。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models commit one token per forward pass; diffusion language models commit a block of tokens over several steps. We ask whether a block can be committed in a single forward pass. We study this with a noise-conditioned masked denoiser: a data-independent Gaussian noise field is added to the mask embeddings so that, in principle, each sampled field selects one joint mode of the block. The established way of training such a model is to sample several fields per example and let them compete for the data, by winner-take-all or importance weighting. This gives the noise only coarse control: in our experiments, the information it carries grows roughly with the logarithm of the number of competing fields, and one-step outputs remain rarely coherent across the model sizes tested. We propose CONDOR (Coupled-Noise Distillation for One-Step Readout). A noise-conditioned teacher is trained with a random number of masked positions and winner-take-all. A student proposes a one-step block, retains selected tokens, and learns from the block obtained when the teacher refills the other positions in several steps under the same noise field; a noise-free masked-LM term on the ground truth anchors the student. Human evaluation on TinyStories shows a large gain in one-step legality while different noise fields still yield different blocks, at one forward pass per block.

</details>


### [139] [Cross-Lingual Representation Alignment by Token-Level Optimal Transport in a Language-Agnostic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06381)
*Taisei Yamamoto,Ryoma Kumon,Danushka Bollegala,Hitomi Yanaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CAROT方法，通过最优传输在语言无关空间中实现LLM表示的跨语言对齐，同时分离并保留语言特定信息；在推理时与训练时对齐目标上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨语言对齐（CLA）方法忽视了表征中编码的语言特定信息，且仅考虑句子级对齐，导致性能次优和输入输出语言不匹配问题。

Method: CAROT包含两步：(1) 识别LLM内部状态中的语言特定表征；(2) 通过最优传输（Optimal Transport）在token级对齐跨语言的语言无关表征，同时显式保留语言特定表征。

Result: 推理时steering实验中，CAROT计算的对齐目标将多语言性能最高提升11.2个准确率点，同时保持输入输出语言一致性；作为训练目标时，在18个评估设置（3模型×3任务×ID/OOD语言）中11个设置优于现有CLA方法。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了有效CLA对齐目标应具备的特征，为LLM的跨语言表示对齐提供了新见解。

Abstract: Cross-lingual alignment (CLA) aims to align the representations of large language models (LLMs) across languages, enabling cross-lingual transfer to improve multilingual capabilities. Previous CLA methods often ignore language-specific information encoded in representations and only consider sentence-level alignment, which may lead to suboptimal performance and input-output language mismatch. We propose CAROT (Cross-Lingual Alignment of Representations in a Language-Agnostic Space via Optimal Transport), which consists of two steps: identifying language-specific representations in LLMs' internal states and aligning language-agnostic representations across languages at the token level by optimal transport, while explicitly preserving language-specific representations. Inference-time steering experiments show that the representations computed by CAROT are effective alignment targets, improving multilingual performance by up to 11.2 points in accuracy while maintaining input-output language consistency. We further use the representations obtained by CAROT as training targets, internalizing the aligned representations. The trained models outperform existing CLA methods in 11 of 18 evaluation settings (3 models $\times$ 3 tasks $\times$ ID/OOD languages). Our work provides insights into what constitutes effective alignment targets for CLA in LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/ynklab/CAROT

</details>


### [140] [Hierarchical Wasserstein Merging for Multi-Domain Multi-Task Learning: From Specialists to a Generalist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06406)
*Ming Cheng,Jiaying Gong,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出层次化Wasserstein合并（HWM）框架，用Wasserstein重心构建任务级和全局重心来建模表征分布几何结构，实现多域多任务学习中领域-任务专有模型的合并或通用模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多域多任务学习（MD-MTL）旨在构建跨异构领域和任务的单一通用模型，但联合训练在分布偏移下常遭受干扰；现有模型合并方法多操作于模型参数层面，忽视了跨域跨任务的表征分布几何结构。

Method: 将每个领域-任务专有模型建模为共享支撑上隐表征的分布；构建任务级Wasserstein重心以捕获任务内领域变化，构建全局重心以捕获跨任务结构；支持两种模式：由Wasserstein派生权重实现免训练的专有模型聚合，或通过混合Wasserstein对齐损失进行基于训练的通用模型学习。

Result: 在四个NLP任务×每个任务四个领域上的实验表明，HWM在MD-MTL场景中实现了优越的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 表征层面的分布几何建模（Wasserstein重心）为MD-MTL提供了优于纯参数级合并的通用模型构建途径，兼具免训练聚合与可训练通用模型学习两种灵活模式。

Abstract: Multi-domain multi-task learning (MD-MTL) aims to build a single generalist model that performs well across heterogeneous domains and tasks. However, joint training often suffers from interference under distribution shifts. Existing model merging methods mostly operate on model parameters while overlooking the geometric structure of latent representation distributions across domains and tasks. To address these limitations, we propose Hierarchical Wasserstein Merging (HWM), a representation-level framework that models each domain-task specialist as a distribution of hidden representations on a shared support. HWM constructs task-level and global Wasserstein barycenters to capture within-task domain variation and cross-task structure, enabling either training-free specialist aggregation by Wasserstein-derived weights or training-based generalist learning through a hybrid Wasserstein alignment loss. Experiments on four NLP tasks across four domains per task show that HWM achieves superior effectiveness and generalization capability in MD-MTL settings.

</details>


### [141] [Visual Search Augmented Chain-of-Thought Reasoning for Attribute Value Extraction from Product Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06410)
*Tong Wu,Ming Cheng,Jiazhen Hu,Jiaying Gong,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ViS-CoT，一种无需训练、即插即用的流水线方法，通过视觉聚类、视觉搜索和交织思维链推理，增强视频大语言模型在电商视频到文本属性值抽取任务中的性能，在VideoAVE数据集上平均提升17.91个百分点micro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉属性值抽取方法依赖静态产品图像，无法捕获时序线索、多角度视图和细粒度视觉细节；直接将视频VLM应用于产品AVE缺乏领域知识，而微调需要大量高质量数据和计算资源。

Method: ViS-CoT包含三个核心模块：(1) 视觉聚类用于识别代表性视频帧；(2) 视觉搜索检索语义相似的产品知识以丰富属性线索；(3) 交织思维链推理模块，通过由字幕生成和自动语音识别产生的视觉对齐辅助文本，迭代优化推理过程。

Result: 在VideoAVE数据集的14个产品类别上进行广泛实验，ViS-CoT持续增强多个最先进的视频VLM，平均micro-F1提升17.91个百分点。

Conclusion: ViS-CoT作为一种无需训练、即插即用的流水线，可轻松应用于任何开源视频VLM，有效解决视频到文本AVE任务，弥合了静态图像方法与视频VLM之间在电商场景下的性能差距。

Abstract: Existing approaches to visual attribute value extraction (AVE) primarily rely on static product images, failing to capture temporal cues, multi-angle views and fine-grained visual details. Directly applying video vision-language models (VLMs) to product AVE results in limited performance due to the lack of domain knowledge, and fine-tuning them requires extensive high-quality data and substantial computational resources. Thus, we propose visual search augmented chain-of-thought reasoning (ViS-CoT), a training-free, plug-and-play pipeline that can be easily applied to any open-source video VLM for video-to-text AVE in e-Commerce. Specifically, ViS-CoT employs visual clustering to identify representative frames, followed by visual search to retrieve semantically similar product knowledge that can enrich attribute cues. Next, an interleaved CoT reasoning module iteratively refines reasoning through visually-aligned auxiliary texts derived from captioning and automatic speech recognition. Finally, the integrated information guides the model toward accurate and fine-grained attribute predictions. Extensive experiments across 14 product categories on the VideoAVE dataset show that ViS-CoT consistently enhances multiple state-of-the-art video VLMs, achieving an average improvement of 17.91 percentage points in micro-F1.

</details>


### [142] [InsightChain: Optimized Chain-of-Insight Analytics for LLM-driven Data Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06438)
*Hanya Sun,Chen Zhang,Sheng Liang,Yongyue Zhang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出InsightChain，一个四阶段（Explore-Focus-Test-Present）的可视化提示流水线，模拟专家分析师的迭代推理过程；同时提出VG-COPRO，一种视觉引导的自动提示优化方法，用于联合优化多阶段可执行流水线；并引入新的评估指标Insight Progression Metric (IPM)。实验表明InsightChain在公共数据集上优于基线，VG-COPRO在域内和跨域场景均取得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的数据可视化方法通常将可视化生成视为从用户查询到图形或代码的单步映射，忽略了专家分析师进行数据探索时的迭代分析推理过程，导致生成的可视化缺乏深度洞察。

Method: 提出InsightChain四阶段流水线（Explore-Focus-Test-Present），模拟专家分析流程；提出VG-COPRO视觉引导自动提示优化方法以联合优化多阶段可执行流水线；引入IPM评估指标，结合四个文本维度与一个视觉维度进行综合评价。

Result: 通过100条链的人类试点和覆盖十个领域的300条链的基于智能体的扩展评估验证了IPM的有效性；公共数据集上的实验表明InsightChain持续优于竞争性提示基线；现有APO方法在此多阶段任务上无法获得一致的收益，而VG-COPRO在域内及跨域场景均提升了性能。

Conclusion: InsightChain通过模拟专家分析推理过程显著提升了LLM驱动的可视化生成质量；针对多阶段任务的视觉引导自动提示优化（VG-COPRO）比传统APO方法更有效；本文同时填补了复杂数据可视化评估的空白。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for automated data visualization, yet existing approaches often frame visualization generation as a single-step mapping from user query to figure or code, overlooking the iterative analytical reasoning process of expert analysts. We present InsightChain, a four-stage visualization prompting pipeline (Explore--Focus--Test--Present) that emulates expert analytical workflows, together with VG-COPRO, a vision-guided automatic prompt optimization (APO) method adapted to jointly optimize such multi-stage, executable pipelines. To address the evaluation gap for complex data visualization, we introduce the Insight Progression Metric (IPM), a rubric combining four text-based dimensions with a vision-based dimension. We assess IPM through a 100-chain human pilot and an expanded 300-chain agent-based evaluation spanning all ten domains. Experiments on public datasets show that InsightChain consistently outperforms competing prompting baselines. Existing APO methods fail to yield consistent gains on this multi-stage task, whereas VG-COPRO improves performance in both in-domain and cross-domain settings.

</details>


### [143] [Decomposing LLM-Judge Uncertainty to Target Expert Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06444)
*Ryan Lail*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个轻量贝叶斯模型，用于将LLM法官（LLM judge）的升级信号分解为随机不确定性（专家间真实分歧）和认知不确定性（法官自身无知），从而将专家标注资源精准导向法官真正无知的样本，而非专家本就存在分歧的样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模使用LLM法官评估输出时，需要专家在法官最不确定的地方进行标注，但法官的自然不确定性信号（如置信度）混淆了两种本质上不同的不确定性：随机不确定性（专家池中的真实分歧，额外标注无法消除）和认知不确定性（法官自身的认知缺失，额外标注可以消除）。若不加区分地升级，专家精力会被浪费在无法通过标注改善的分歧样本上。因此需要一种方法将二者分离，实现对法官认知不确定性的精准估计。

Method: 方法核心是一个小型贝叶斯模型：在已收集的专家标签数据上做回归，用以学习"在多大程度上可以信任一个黑盒法官的预测"，从而把法官的不确定性信号分解为两部分。分解后的随机不确定性和认知不确定性均可由简洁的闭式公式计算，无需采样或额外调用法官。模型中，随机不确定性对应专家池中的真实分歧（标签无法减少的部分），认知不确定性对应法官的预测误差中可被标注消除的组成部分。

Result: 在真实LLM法官且真值完全已知的设置下，两种不确定性成分能够被有效分离，且发现法官声明（stated confidence）的置信度与其实际错误之间并无可靠关联。在真实人类分歧数据集ChaosNLI上，基于认知不确定性的排序在相同专家标签预算下比基于总不确定性的排序多消除83%的误差；但值得注意的是，简单地升级标注量最少的样本在该数据集上也能达到相同效果。

Conclusion: 本文证明了可以在不借助额外采样或多次法官调用的前提下，估计"法官在何处无知"而非"专家在何处真实分歧"，并据此指导专家标注资源的分配。这一框架为大规模LLM评估中将有限的人工标注预算对准最值得投入的样本提供了可操作的途径。

Abstract: An LLM judge evaluates outputs at scale. Experts should label only where it is least sure. Its natural escalation signal conflates two uncertainties: aleatoric, real disagreement in the expert pool, which labels cannot reduce, and epistemic, the judge's ignorance, which labels do reduce. A small Bayesian model separates them: a regression on labels already collected learns how far to trust a black-box judge's prediction. Both components follow as simple formulas, with no sampling or further judge calls. The components isolate on a real LLM judge against exactly known truth, and stated confidence is no guide to its actual error. On real human disagreement (ChaosNLI) the epistemic ranking removes 83% more error than total uncertainty for the same expert labels, though simply escalating the least-labelled items does as well there. We demonstrate we can estimate where a judge is ignorant rather than where experts genuinely disagree, and propose using this to direct expert labelling.

</details>


### [144] [DFlow: Enabling Verifier Information Flow in Block Diffusion Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06498)
*Yaojie Zhang,Linfeng Zhang,Bin Cui,Xupeng Miao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DFlow是一个简单而有效的框架，通过跨起草轮次复用验证器在拒绝位置产生的隐藏状态来引导后续起草，从而改进块扩散推测解码的推理效率，并在Qwen3模型上的多样基准中持续优于DFlash。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的块扩散推测解码方法只保留被接受的token前缀并丢弃被拒绝的后缀，导致这些位置上的计算无法惠及后续起草轮次，迫使起草器反复从零重建未来token的表示。作者观察到拒绝只决定提议token能否被提交，而被拒绝位置的验证器表示仍可为后续预测提供有用信息，因此希望利用这些被丢弃的表示来提升推理效率。

Method: DFlow框架的核心是让验证器信息跨起草轮次流动：它将目标验证器在拒绝后缀产生的隐藏状态重新用于引导后续起草，而无需额外的目标模型计算。为实现这一信息流的有效学习，作者引入了自条件训练策略，将先前预测的验证器表示反馈到后续预测中。

Result: 在Qwen3模型上的多样基准实验中，DFlow一致性地提升了草稿质量和接受长度，相较于基线方法DFlash表现更优。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，推测解码中验证器的被拒绝位置的隐藏状态并非无用信息，通过跨轮次的信息流动框架，可以显著提高块扩散推测解码的推理效率。

Abstract: Block diffusion speculative decoding improves LLM inference efficiency by proposing a block of future tokens in parallel and verifying them with a single forward pass through the target model. However, existing methods retain only the accepted prefix and discard the rejected suffix, preventing the computation spent on these positions from benefiting subsequent drafting rounds and forcing the drafter to repeatedly reconstruct representations for future tokens from scratch. We observe that rejection only determines whether a proposed token can be committed, while the verifier representations at rejected positions can still provide useful information for subsequent predictions. Based on this observation, we propose DFlow, a simple yet effective framework that enables verifier information to flow across drafting rounds. DFlow reuses the hidden states produced by the target verifier for the rejected suffix to guide subsequent drafting without additional target computation. To effectively learn this information flow across drafting rounds, we introduce a self-condition train strategy that feeds verifier representations from earlier predictions back into subsequent predictions. Experiments on Qwen3 models across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that DFlow consistently improves draft quality and acceptance length over DFlash.

</details>


### [145] [ProcArena: A Multi-Scenario Benchmark for LLMs on Direct and Interactive PL/SQL Development from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06527)
*Hang Zhang,Chaokun Wang,Yuzhi Pan,Ziyao Zhong,Shuo Cao,Yue Xue,Zeyu Huang,Xingwei Zhou,Fang Niu,Bofan Xie,Guanchen Ge,Leqi Zheng,Ziyang Liu,Xiannian Cao,Pengcheng Ge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ProcArena，一个基于执行验证的基准，涵盖直接与交互两种模式下的NL到PL/SQL开发任务，包含157个数据库上的3,998个可执行任务，横跨PostgreSQL和Oracle中的九个开发子场景。实验发现最优模型在Direct和Interactive模式下平均得分仅分别为62.2%和57.8%，表明现实场景下的NL到PL/SQL开发仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NL-to-PL/SQL研究主要聚焦于从完整自然语言需求直接生成PL/SQL代码，但实际PL/SQL开发涉及从零开发、代码修改、调试、优化等多种场景，且可能需要直接生成或多轮交互。目前缺乏一个综合性的基准来评估多场景、直接与交互、多方言的NL-to-PL/SQL开发能力。

Method: ProcArena基准构建方法包括：(1) 通过迭代逻辑增强（Iterative Logic Enhancement）和场景特定适配器构造具有挑战性的Direct任务；(2) 通过知识整合（Knowledge Integration）和需求扰动（Requirement Perturbation）在保持可执行目标的前提下衍生配对的Interactive任务；(3) 设计受控的Solver-User模拟器协议，允许模型澄清用户意图并检查数据库环境，但不会暴露隐藏的执行反馈。

Result: 在七个语言模型上的评估显示，最优模型在Direct模式的平均得分仅为62.2%，在Interactive模式为57.8%，说明现实场景下的NL-to-PL/SQL开发任务仍然非常具有挑战性，尤其在交互式设置中模型表现更差。

Conclusion: ProcArena揭示了现有LLM在处理多场景、多方言及交互式NL-to-PL/SQL开发方面的明显不足，为数据库社区提供了一个全面、可执行的评估基准，推动了该领域的研究进展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for translating natural-language (NL) requirements into PL/SQL programs, attracting increasing attention from the database community. However, existing NL-to-PL/SQL efforts primarily focus on directly generating PL/SQL from complete NL requirements. In practice, PL/SQL development involves diverse scenarios, such as from-scratch development, code modification, debugging, and optimization, and may require either direct generation or multi-turn interaction. Yet, no comprehensive benchmark evaluates multi-scenario, direct and interactive, and multi-dialect NL-to-PL/SQL development. In this paper, we present ProcArena, an execution-based benchmark covering both Direct and Interactive modes. ProcArena comprises 3,998 executable tasks over 157 databases, spanning nine development subscenarios in PostgreSQL and Oracle. We construct challenging Direct tasks through Iterative Logic Enhancement and scenario-specific adapters, and derive paired Interactive tasks through Knowledge Integration and Requirement Perturbation while preserving executable targets. We further design a controlled Solver-User Simulator protocol that allows models to clarify user intent and inspect the database environment without exposing hidden execution feedback. Evaluating seven language models, we find that the best average scores are only 62.2% and 57.8% in Direct and Interactive, respectively, demonstrating that realistic NL-to-PL/SQL development remains challenging, particularly in interactive settings.

</details>


### [146] [LLMs Mirror Country-Specific Gender Patterns If Asked, but Skew Male When Generating Media in Local Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06545)
*Sharif Kazemi,Tanya Popli,Neil K. R. Sehgal,Sunny Rai,Niyati Malhotra,Victor Orozco-Olvera,Ana María Muñoz Boudet,Samuel P. Fraiberger,Sharath Chandra Guntuku,Manuel Tonneau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过在美国、印度、肯尼亚和尼日利亚收集695名受访者的性别联想数据，评估了8个LLM在直接问答和媒体生成两种模式下对22个职业与家庭角色的性别刻板印象。研究发现模型在直接问答时与调查一致，但在本地语言的媒体生成中明显偏向男性，且仅用英语和仅选国家进行评估会遗漏这一偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于生成媒体内容，但其是否延续性别刻板印象尚不清楚。现有基准依赖选择题形式而非长文生成，且西方以外的本地性别联想基线数据稀缺，导致无法准确评估模型在全球部署中的真实偏差。

Method: 在美印肯尼四国收集695名受访者对22种职业与家庭角色的性别联想作为人类基线；评估8个LLM，在直接问答与媒体生成两种条件下进行测试，覆盖多语言（含本地语言）场景，并分析指令提示对偏差的影响。

Result: 模型在直接问答中与调查所得的性别联想一致，但在媒体生成中显著更偏向男性，这与人类媒体中的男性偏差一致。有趣的是，美国以外地区在英文提示下偏差转移很小且不显著，意味着纯英语或不分国家的评估会遗漏模型在主要部署语言中的偏差。指令提示可方向性降低偏差，但会牺牲与调查联想的一致性。

Conclusion: 评估LLM性别偏差以指导全球部署时，需要采用生成式测试格式、本地语言提示以及本地收集的人类基线，仅有英语或选择题式评估不足以保证公平性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate media, but whether their content perpetuates gender stereotypes is unknown: standard benchmarks rely on selection-based formats rather than long-form generation, and surveyed baselines for local gender associations are scarce outside the West. We collect gender associations for 22 occupational and domestic roles from 695 respondents across the United States, India, Kenya, and Nigeria, and evaluate eight LLMs under two regimes: direct questioning and media generation. Models track the surveyed associations under direct questioning but skew substantially more male under media generation in major local-language cells, consistent with the male bias documented in human-produced media. Outside the US, the shift is much smaller and non-significant under English prompting, so English-only or country-agnostic evaluation would miss this bias in the languages where these models are most deployed. Instruction prompting reduces the shift directionally, but trades off against alignment with the surveyed associations. Evaluating LLM gender bias for global deployment therefore requires generation-format testing, local-language prompting, and locally-collected human baselines.

</details>


### [147] [Discovering Translation-Worthy Languages with E-Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06593)
*Wajdi Ben Saad,Safa Madiouni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于配对e过程（paired e-processes）的语言级路由方法，用于在多语言文本分类中自适应决定何时翻译文档，在统计受控的前提下提升分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言文档翻译时机的选择是文本分类中的核心路由问题：翻译可以提升部分语言的预测效果，但可能降低其他语言性能或引入不必要的计算开销。现有的统一翻译和启发式语言分层方法缺乏统计受控的路由选择机制。

Method: 提出基于配对e过程的语言级路由器，通过持续比较直接分类与翻译辅助分类的性能，再冻结路由策略。采用族系错误率（familywise）控制，设置阈值为280，将14种候选语言中每条数据集中任何错误路由的概率控制在0.05以内。

Result: 在SIB-200和MASSIVE数据集上，路由器分别为15种语言中的4种和15个地区中的14个选择翻译，相比直接分类将保留数据上的准确率分别提升8.14和16.70个百分点。28个决策在50种独立于结果的排序和分组阈值下均保持稳定。

Conclusion: 配对e过程能够实现统计受控、任意时刻有效（anytime-valid）且可审计的多语言分类路由。

Abstract: Choosing when to translate multilingual documents is a central routing problem in text classification: translation can improve predictions for some languages while degrading others or adding unnecessary computation. Uniform translation and heuristic language tiers do not provide statistically controlled route selection. We introduce a language-level router based on paired e-processes that continuously compares direct and translation-assisted classification before freezing a routing policy. A familywise-controlled threshold of 280 bounds the probability of any false route across 14 eligible languages per dataset by 0.05. On SIB-200 and MASSIVE, the router selects translation for 4 of 15 languages and 14 of 15 locales, improving held-out accuracy over direct classification by 8.14 and 16.70 percentage points, respectively. All 28 decisions remain stable across 50 outcome-independent orderings and relative to the per-group threshold. Our results demonstrate that paired e-processes enable statistically controlled, anytime-valid, and auditable multilingual classification routing.

</details>


### [148] [SAGE: A Hierarchical Framework for Evaluating Interpretive Literary Quality in Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06611)
*Tianyu Wang,Nianjun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出SAGE六层评估框架，用于衡量叙事文学质量中的阐释性维度（文化表征、情感深度、哲学参与），结合规则法与LLM多轮迭代评估实现测量级可靠性（98.8%收敛率，>94%评分者一致性），并在600次评估中发现了系统性能力边界：LLM生成叙事在情感-心理表征上接近人类水平，但在文化批评和哲学深度上存在约两倍的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLG指标无法衡量叙事文学质量中的阐释性维度（文化表征、情感深度、哲学参与）。作者希望建立一个能够可靠评估这些深层阐释性品质的框架，并探究LLM在文学叙事生成中的真实能力边界。

Method: 提出SAGE六层评估框架：前几层基于规则的文本属性评估，后几层基于文化理论、情感理论和存在主义哲学，通过LLM进行多轮迭代评估并采用独立交叉验证。在100篇短篇小说上实施600次评估，跨多个评估模型验证可靠性。

Result: 框架达到98.8%收敛率和超过94%的跨评分者一致性，且在不同评估模型间保持稳定。核心发现为系统性能力界限：LLM在情感-心理表征维度接近人类水平，而在文化批判和哲学深度维度差距约为前者两倍。LLM生成的叙事在所有三个阐释性维度上均低于商业类型小说。

Conclusion: LLM文学叙事能力的边界存在于"可模式复制的文学能力"（可从训练语料学习中习得）与"需要立场的能力"（需要文化定位和哲学参与，纯模式匹配无法提供）之间。这为理解LLM生成文学的深层局限提供了理论解释。

Abstract: Assessing the literary quality of narratives requires evaluating interpretive dimensions (cultural representation, emotional depth, and philosophical engagement) that existing NLG metrics cannot measure. We introduce SAGE, a six-layer evaluation framework that separates rule-based assessment of observable textual properties from LLM-based evaluation of interpretive qualities drawn from cultural theory, affect theory, and existentialist philosophy. Each interpretive layer is assessed through multi-round iterative LLM evaluation with independent cross-validation, achieving measurement-grade reliability (98.8% convergence, >94% inter-rater agreement) stable across evaluator models. Validated on 600 evaluations across 100 short stories, our central finding is a systematic capability boundary: emotional-psychological representation approaches human levels, while cultural critique and philosophical depth exhibit approximately double the gap. LLM-generated narratives score below even commercial genre fiction on all three layers. We interpret this as a boundary between pattern-reproducible literary capacities learnable from training corpora and stance-requiring ones demanding cultural positioning and philosophical engagement that pattern matching alone cannot provide.

</details>


### [149] [Mind the Gap: Exposing LLM Translation Blind Spots Using the AlphaMWE Multilingual Parallel Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06634)
*Lifeng Han,Jiahui Liang,Anna Latusek,Karim El Haff,Amal Haddad Haddad,Josua Höfgen,Kilian Evang,Min Ma,Maryia Zhyrko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了WMT2026测试套件共享任务中31个机器翻译系统在AlphaMWE多语言平行语料上的表现，涵盖英语到中文、波兰语、德语及多种阿拉伯语方言的翻译，结合自动化评估（BLEU、ChrF、BERT-score）和人工评估，发现多词表达仍对LLM翻译构成瓶颈，且人工评估揭示了被聚合分数掩盖的语言特有错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型在多词表达（MWE）上的理解和翻译能力是否仍是瓶颈，因为LLM在MT任务上的表现通常依赖于特定领域和语言对的训练数据可用性。

Method: 使用公开可用的多语言平行语料AlphaMWE作为测试套件，收集WMT2026共享任务中31个MT系统的输出（覆盖英语到中文、波兰语、德语、现代标准阿拉伯语及埃及和突尼斯方言阿拉伯语），先通过BLEU、ChrF、BERT-score进行自动化评估选出每对语言Top3系统，再对选定系统进行人工评估。

Result: 研究发现：（1）比喻性表达/MWE现象对LLM仍具挑战性；（2）不同自动评估指标之间存在分歧；（3）人工评估揭示了被聚合分数掩盖的语言特异性错误。

Conclusion: MWE仍然是LLM在机器翻译中的薄弱环节，单纯依赖自动化指标可能造成对系统性能的误判，需要结合人工评估以发现更深层的语言特有问题。

Abstract: LLMs' performance on machine translation (MT) tasks is often dependent on the data availability in the specific domains and language pairs that they are trained upon. To examine if Multiword Expressions (MWEs) still set a bottleneck for LLMs regarding language understanding and translation, we report the system performances from the WMT2026 Test Suites shared task, for which we used the publicly available multilingual parallel corpus AlphaMWE as the test suites. We received 31 MT systems' outputs covering English to Chinese (zh), Polish (pl), German (de), Arabic (ar) including Modern Standard Arabic (MSA) and two dialectal ones (Egyptian and Tunisian Arabic). We carried out automatic evaluations using BLEU, ChrF, BERT-score to select the Top3 systems per language pair, followed up with human evaluations on the selected systems. Our findings show that: figurative/MWE phenomena remain challenging; automatic metrics sometimes disagree; human evaluation uncovers language-specific errors hidden by aggregate scores.

</details>


### [150] [ECOKV: Geometry-Aware KV Cache Eviction via Complementary Diversity Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06663)
*Chin Ting Hsu,Yu-Syuan Xu,Ling Zou,Hsien-Kai Kuo,Wen-Huang Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ECOKV方法，通过几何感知的复合度量（结合欧氏距离和余弦相似度）来评估token多样性，并根据注意力头的冗余程度自适应地在多样性和重要性分数之间加权，同时缩减观察窗口以分配更多缓存容量给信息性token，从而在多种压缩比例下实现KV cache驱逐的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)虽然擅长多种任务，但其可扩展性受限于KV缓存存储的内存和计算开销。现有基于余弦相似度的多样性度量存在两个问题：(1)归一化操作丢失了幅度信息；(2)由于隐藏表示的各向异性特性，不同层之间的相似度值往往均匀偏高。这些限制阻碍了KV cache驱逐策略的效率和效果。

Method: ECOKV提出了一种几何感知的复合度量，联合利用欧氏距离和余弦相似度从互补视角捕捉token多样性。此外，利用这两个度量估计每个注意力头的冗余级别，从而在token选择过程中实现多样性分数与重要性分数之间的自适应加权。最后，压缩了用于保留近邻token的观察窗口，释放更多缓存容量给信息性token。

Result: 大量实验表明，ECOKV在各种压缩比例下均达到了最先进的性能，并且可以无缝集成到现有的KV cache驱逐方法中。此外，论文还分析了重要性分数与多样性分数之间的关系，并考察了跨层和跨注意力头的冗余模式。

Conclusion: ECOKV通过几何感知的复合度量与自适应加权机制，有效解决了现有多样性度量在KV cache驱逐中的局限性，在不改变架构的前提下显著提升了缓存压缩效率和模型性能，为MLLMs的可扩展性提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Although multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel in diverse tasks, their scalability remains limited by the memory and computational overhead of KV cache storage. Recent KV cache eviction approaches incorporate a cosine similarity-based diversity metric with importance metrics to selectively retain critical key-value pairs. However, cosine similarity involves normalization that discards magnitude information, and it often yields uniformly high similarity values across layers due to the anisotropy property of hidden representations. In our study ECOKV, we rigorously deconstruct the capabilities of existing diversity metrics. Moving beyond simple measurement, we propose a geometry-aware composite metric that jointly leverages Euclidean distance and cosine similarity to capture token diversity from complementary perspectives. Furthermore, we use these two metrics to estimate the redundancy level of each attention head, allowing adaptive weighting between diversity and importance scores during token selection. Finally, we demonstrate that the observation window commonly employed to preserve recent tokens can be substantially reduced, thereby allocating more cache capacity to informative tokens and yielding consistent improvements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ECOKV achieves state-of-the-art performance under various compression ratios and can be seamlessly integrated with existing KV cache eviction methods. We further analyze the relationship between importance and diversity, and examine redundancy patterns across layers and attention heads.

</details>


### [151] [A Grapheme-Aware Indic Tokenizer for Tamil: Large-Scale Training and Intrinsic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06690)
*Hari Krishnan K,Sudarsun Santhiappan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向泰米尔语（Tamil）的基于字素（grapheme）感知的指示语分词器，通过可逆Unicode映射策略在WordPiece词汇学习前保留完整的字素簇，从而生成语言学上有意义的词边界。在大规模泰米尔语语料上训练后，与GPT-2、mBERT、mT5、mBART和NLLB五种主流多语言分词器进行比较，该分词器在碎片化和序列效率相关的内在评估指标上表现最佳，同时匹配最高压缩比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泰米尔语等形态丰富的印度语言在现有主流多语言分词器（如BPE、WordPiece、SentencePiece）下分词效率低下，主要原因在于其基于字素（grapheme）的书写系统——一个可见字符可能对应多个Unicode码点，导致分词器在码点级别切分时产生大量碎片，降低词汇效率和模型性能。

Method: 提出一种字素感知的指示语分词器：首先采用可逆的Unicode映射策略将泰米尔语文本中的完整字素簇保留为统一表示，再在该字素级表示上学习WordPiece词汇表。该方法确保词边界在语言学意义上保持完整，同时与基于Transformer的语言模型完全兼容。分词器在大规模泰米尔语语料上训练，并通过衡量压缩效率、词碎片化、信息密度和词汇利用率的综合内在评估框架进行评测。

Result: 与GPT-2、mBERT、mT5、mBART和NLLB五种广泛使用的多语言分词器对比，所提出的分词器在碎片化和序列效率导向的内在指标上取得最强性能，并达到了观察到的最高压缩比。

Conclusion: 字素感知的预处理策略对泰米尔语分词是有效的，能够在不影响Transformer兼容性的前提下显著提升分词的碎片化和序列效率，为形态丰富的印度语言分词提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tokenization forms the foundation of modern Natural Language Processing (NLP) systems by transforming raw text into discrete units that neural language models can process. The effectiveness of this process directly influences vocabulary efficiency, sequence length, computational cost, and downstream model performance. Although multilingual tokenizers such as Byte Pair Encoding (BPE), WordPiece, and SentencePiece have performed well across numerous languages, they often segment morphologically rich Indic languages inefficiently. Tamil, in particular, poses unique challenges because its grapheme-based writing system can represent a single visible character with multiple Unicode code points. In this work, we present a grapheme-aware Indic tokenizer for Tamil that preserves complete grapheme clusters through a reversible Unicode mapping strategy prior to WordPiece vocabulary learning. By operating on grapheme-level representations instead of individual Unicode code points, the tokenizer produces linguistically meaningful token boundaries while remaining fully compatible with transformer-based language models. The tokenizer is trained on a large-scale Tamil corpus and evaluated using a comprehensive intrinsic evaluation framework that measures compression efficiency, token fragmentation, information density, and vocabulary utilization. Experimental evaluation compares the proposed tokenizer against five widely used multilingual tokenizers: GPT-2, mBERT, mT5, mBART, and NLLB. The proposed tokenizer achieves the strongest performance among the evaluated tokenizers on fragmentation- and sequence-efficiency-oriented intrinsic metrics, while matching the highest observed compression ratio. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of grapheme-aware preprocessing for Tamil tokenization.

</details>


### [152] [PARSER: Read in Parallel, Reason in Depth for Long-Context LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06702)
*Kun Li,Zexuan Qiu,Tianhua Zhang,Irwin King,Helen Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PARSER框架，通过将"阅读"与"推理"解耦来突破顺序记忆智能体的局限。多个轻量子智能体并行读取文档分块，主智能体通过迭代的"散射-聚合"循环进行深度推理，最终在长上下文多跳问答任务上显著优于顺序基线，并将推理延迟降低最多11倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序记忆智能体在长文档处理中将文档遍历与推理深度耦合，导致推理性能对证据位置敏感，且推理延迟随文档长度线性增长。这些固有限制阻碍了其在超长上下文场景下的可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Method: PARSER由两层结构组成：一组轻量子智能体各自绑定一个文档分块并并行读取整个文档；一个主智能体通过迭代的散射-聚合循环进行深度推理——每轮向所有子智能体广播查询、聚合返回的证据、基于当前发现生成更深层的后续查询。所有可学习行为集中在主智能体中，通过强化学习优化，而子智能体保持为冻结的现成模型。

Result: 在7K至896K token范围的多跳QA任务中，配备4B骨干模型的PARSER平均超越最强顺序记忆基线5.7个点，在896K token场景下超出12.0个点。扩展至9B骨干后，PARSER超越DeepSeek-V4-Pro 6.3个点。PARSER对证据位置、顺序和距离的扰动表现出鲁棒性，同时推理延迟最高降低11倍。

Conclusion: 解耦阅读与推理的架构设计能有效解决顺序记忆智能体对证据位置敏感和延迟线性增长的根本问题。通过并行的证据收集与迭代的推理精化，PARSER在准确率、鲁棒性和效率三个维度上同时实现了显著提升，验证了"并行阅读+串行推理"范式的有效性。

Abstract: Sequential memory agents process long documents by reading chunks one after another while maintaining a compact memory state, coupling document traversal to reasoning depth. This coupling introduces sensitivity to evidence placement and ties inference latency linearly to document length. We introduce PARSER, which decouples reading from reasoning. A bank of lightweight subagents each bound to a single chunk read the entire document in parallel, while a lead agent reasons in depth through iterative scatter--gather rounds: at each round it broadcasts a query to all subagents, aggregates the returned evidence, and formulates a deeper follow-up query conditioned on what has been found so far. This decoupled design concentrates all learnable behavior in the lead agent, which is optimized with reinforcement learning, while the subagents remain frozen off-the-shelf models. On multi-hop QA with contexts ranging from 7K to 896K tokens, PARSER with a 4B backbone outperforms the strongest sequential memory baseline by 5.7 points on average and by 12.0 points at 896K tokens. Scaling to a 9B backbone, PARSER surpasses DeepSeek-V4-Pro by 6.3 points. Controlled experiments confirm that PARSER is robust to perturbations in evidence position, order, and distance, conditions that cause large accuracy swings in sequential methods, while reducing inference latency by up to 11x.

</details>


### [153] [DianShi-RxnDB: A Large-Scale, Fine-Grained Organic Reaction Data Platform Built via a Fully Automated Pipeline for Researchers and AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06703)
*Yubin Wang,Xingjian Wei,Jiang Wu,Yinfan Wang,Boyu Zhu,Lin Zhang,Jianing Yu,Huazheng Zeng,Ruiyi Ding,Junyuan Gao,Jiaxing Sun,Lingli Ge,Haote Yang,Jingchao Wang,Aijia Guo,Qian Jiang,Yurui Zhao,Wenjian Zhang,Chen Zhu,Lijun Wu,Xiaolei Yang,Haodong Chen,Junjie Yuan,Zichao Ye,Shaowei Hou,Jing Ye,Jia Yu,Shan Wang,Lijun Wu,Jiantao Qiu,Chao Xu,Yuqiang Li,Guangyu Wang,Bowen Zhou,Dahua Lin,Conghui He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DianShi-RxnDB 是一个大规模、细粒度的有机化学反应数据平台，通过全自动提取与标准化流水线整合专利文本、图像和反应式，涵盖1976-2025年间USPTO和EPO的有机合成专利，产出约2400万条反应实例，其中约61.7%通过自动化质量校验，并在人工评估中达到92.95%的字段级准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的有机反应结构化数据对AI4Chem至关重要，但大量反应知识分散在专利文本、图像和反应式中，缺乏整合且自动化的数据提取方案。

Method: 构建全自动的提取与标准化流水线，同时处理专利文本、图像和反应式三类信息源；提取每条单步反应实验的参与者、角色、用量、温度、时间、产率、实验步骤及专利溯源信息；并通过自动化资格校验筛选高质量记录。

Result: 覆盖USPTO与EPO在1976-2025年间发布的有机合成专利，获得约2400万条反应实例，其中约1480万条（61.7%）通过资格校验；1300条人工抽样实例的字段级微平均准确率为92.95%；与Pistachio的匹配比较显示出去重记录数、表示粒度和字段级精确一致性方面优势。

Conclusion: DianShi-RxnDB提供包含Web研究工作台和MCP服务的平台，支持搜索、筛选、比较与溯源验证，为AI4Chem提供高质量的细粒度反应数据基础设施。

Abstract: High-quality structured organic reaction data are essential for developing artificial intelligence for chemistry (AI4Chem), yet much of this knowledge remains dispersed across patent text, images, and reaction schemes. We present DianShi-RxnDB, a large-scale, fine-grained organic reaction data platform built via a fully automated extraction and normalization pipeline integrating patent text, images, and reaction schemes. Its corpus covers organic synthesis patents from the USPTO and EPO published between 1976 and 2025, yielding approximately 24 million reaction instances, of which approximately 14.8 million (61.7%) pass automated qualification checks. Each instance represents a specific single-step experiment recording participants, roles, quantities, temperatures, reaction times, yields, experimental procedures, and provenance links to source patents. In a manual evaluation of 1,300 sampled qualified instances, the micro-averaged field-level accuracy was 92.95%. A matched comparison with Pistachio further indicated advantages in deduplicated record counts, representation granularity, and field-level exact agreement. The platform provides a Web research workbench for searching, filtering, comparing, and source-verifying records, and a Model Context Protocol (MCP) service offering AI agents composable structured retrieval tools. DianShi-RxnDB is available at https://dianshi.opendatalab.org.cn/ .

</details>


### [154] [Event Interaction in Low-Rank Bottlenecks for Temporal Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06731)
*Wei Sun,Tingyu Qu,Jesse Davis,Marie-Francine Moens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出卷积瓶颈交互（CBI）架构，通过在低秩瓶颈内部使用轻量级深度卷积增强事件表示，并利用逐元素乘法捕获事件间交互，解决了参数高效微调（PEFT）下时间关系抽取性能下降的问题，在五个数据集和七个骨干模型上最高提升+31.7微F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流时间关系抽取系统通过拼接事件跨度或浅层融合来建模事件交互，但在参数高效微调（如Adapter和LoRA）中，低秩瓶颈限制了信息流动，导致事件交互信号无法有效传递，造成明显性能下降。

Method: 提出CBI（Convolutional Bottleneck Interaction）架构，其在低秩瓶颈内部首先应用轻量级深度卷积（depthwise convolution）增强事件表示，然后使用逐元素乘法捕获事件-事件间的有效交互。

Result: 在五个数据集和七个骨干模型（Adapter和LoRA设置）上，CBI提供一致且显著的性能提升，最高达到+31.7微F1，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 低秩空间内的显式事件交互对时间关系抽取至关重要，CBI为PEFT场景下的事件交互建模提供了理论支撑的有效方案。

Abstract: Temporal relation extraction determines whether an event occurs before, after, or simultaneously with another event, and therefore relies on accurately modeling how the two events interact. Mainstream systems achieve this by concatenating event spans or using shallow fusion, which works well when all model parameters are trainable. However, in parameter-efficient fine-tuning, low-rank bottlenecks restrict information flow and prevent these interaction signals from passing through, leading to clear performance drops. To address this limitation, we propose a theoretically grounded architecture, Convolutional Bottleneck Interaction (CBI), which first applies lightweight depthwise convolution to enhance event representations and then uses element-wise multiplication to capture effective event-event interactions inside the bottleneck. Across five datasets and seven backbone models in the Adapter and LoRA settings, CBI provides consistent and substantial gains, up to +31.7 micro F1, while adding minimal computational cost, showing that explicit interaction inside low-rank spaces is crucial for temporal relation extraction. The code is available at https://github.com/VRCMF/CIF.git.

</details>


### [155] [AuthBench: A Large-Scale Multilingual Benchmark for Authorship Representation across Genres and Lengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06771)
*MaoXun Huang,Zhenxing Zhang,Claire Cardie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AuthBench是一个大规模多语言作者表征基准，包含428,150篇文档、153,825位作者、10种语言、9个主要体裁和4个文档长度区间，支持作者归属（检索）和作者验证（二元决策）两个任务。对47个神经模型和3个非神经基线在统一零样本协议下的评测显示，作者表征尚未解决：最佳检索模型仅达0.258 Success@5，最佳验证模型EER为0.076。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者风格信号在数字取证、抄袭分析、账户关联、虚假信息调查和机器生成文本检测等场景中至关重要，但现有作者基准分散割裂，通常只覆盖狭窄的语言集、单一体裁或有限文档长度范围，难以评估现代表征是否真正泛化。

Method: 提出AuthBench多语言大规模基准，包含428,150篇文档、153,825位作者、10种语言、9个主要体裁、66个细粒度体裁和4个文档长度桶。设计两个互补任务：同作者检索（归属）和同作者二元决策（验证）。在统一零样本协议下对47个神经模型和3个非神经基线进行系统评测。

Result: 最佳检索模型仅达0.258 Success@5，最佳验证模型达到0.076 EER和0.968 ROC-AUC。结果揭示检索与验证任务间存在有意义的分裂（不同模型家族分别领先），且跨语言、体裁和文档长度存在显著性能差异。

Conclusion: 作者表征研究远未解决，AuthBench不仅作为新基准，更是诊断性资源，用于研究作者表征何时及为何成功或失败。基准、评估工具包和数据已开源发布。

Abstract: Authorship signals matter in settings where writing style carries identity: digital forensics, plagiarism analysis, account linking, misinformation investigation, and machine-generated text detection. Yet current authorship benchmarks remain fragmented, usually covering only a narrow language set, a single genre, or a limited document-length regime, which makes it difficult to assess whether modern representations truly generalize. We introduce AuthBench, a large-scale multilingual benchmark for authorship representation that is designed to make this evaluation broad, standardized, and realistic. AuthBench contains 428,150 documents written by 153,825 individuals across ten widely used languages, 9 primary genres, 66 fine-grained genres, and four document-length buckets. It supports two complementary tasks: authorship attribution, formulated as same-author retrieval and authorship verification, formulated as same-author binary decision. We benchmark 47 neural models and three non-neural baselines under a unified zero-shot protocol. Results show that authorship representation remains far from solved: the best retrieval model reaches only 0.258 Success@5, while the best verification model achieves 0.076 EER and 0.968 ROC-AUC. The leaderboard also reveals a meaningful task split, with different model families leading retrieval and verification, and large performance differences across languages, genres, and lengths.
  These findings position AuthBench not only as a new benchmark, but as a diagnostic resource for studying when and why authorship representations succeed or fail. We release AuthBench, its evaluation toolkit, and benchmark data at https://github.com/mao-code/AuthBench and https://huggingface.co/datasets/MaoXun/AuthBench.

</details>


### [156] [Typed Federated Artifacts for the Agentic Web:Sharing Tool-Routing Knowledge Across Frozen,Heterogeneous LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06815)
*Abhijit Chakraborty,Ni Trieu,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SYNAPSE，一种类型化联邦工件机制，用于跨模型、跨平台的工具路由知识共享。实验表明，在清理重复和垃圾数据后，联邦协议路由性能接近集中式方法，但发现冲突日志并非主要瓶颈；相反，格式（类型化渲染）而非联邦经验带来了主要收益。论文结尾给出两个警示性发现：基准测试中存在标签泄露/检索泄漏问题，导致TF-IDF分类器在路由基准上远超LLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具路由知识共享的痛点在于：平坦文本（提示、示例池）难以区分噪声统计、合并规则和文档；权重和适配器无法跨平台转移知识。作者希望设计一种类型化联邦工件，支持字段级隐私、争议解决和跨模型迁移，以支持开放、联网的多厂商智能体协作。

Method: 提出SYNAPSE1协议：类型化联邦工件（schema-validated objects），具有明确字段定义支持按字段隐私、争议解决和跨模型传输。在联邦协议下收集工具路由知识合编（compendium），并设计实验比较集中式vs联邦式、类型化合并vs平坦文本、不同冲突策略的效果。同时使用TF-IDF分类器作为基线进行对照分析。

Result: 1) 清理192个垃圾条目和1,916个重复/近似重复测试的训练项后，联邦协议在StableToolBench（3,180个工具）上距离集中式仅差1.1点；2) 类型化合并并渲染比平坦字符串提升8.5点（干净数据）和7.4点（60%注入矛盾）；3) 合并与渲染不可分——类型化合并以平坦方式展示是最差组合；4) 三种冲突策略无显著差异；5) τ-bench零售场景中每轮工具调用准确率提升至少6.7点，归因于格式而非联邦经验；6) TF-IDF分类器在所有LLM路由上以48点和26点的巨大优势胜出，原因是基准存在标签/检索泄漏。

Conclusion: 类型化联邦工件的格式处理（typed rendering）是工具路由知识共享的主要收益来源，而非联邦经验本身；冲突日志并非瓶颈。更重要的是，现有基准如StableToolBench和τ-bench无法测量无标签工具的路由能力——测试查询在过滤前已存在基准池中，造成检索泄漏，因此路由基准需要重新设计。

Abstract: An open, networked web will allow agents to run frozen models from multiple vendors, keep their history private, and teach each other which tool to call and when. Flat text (prompts, example pools) makes it difficult for the protocol to distinguish between noise statistics, merging rules, and documentation. Weights and adapters cannot transfer that knowledge between platforms. We suggest sharing typed federated artifacts, schema-validated objects with well-defined fields for per-field privacy (described here, but measured), dispute resolution, and cross-model transfer, and instantiating them as SYNAPSE1, a common tool-routing knowledge. After deleting 192 garbage entries and 1,916 training items that duplicate or almost duplicate test queries, a federated compendium routes within 1.1 points of a centralized one at 20 MB of JSON per client each round on StableToolBench (3,180 tools). The same experience merged and shown to the router as typed fields rather than one flat string is worth 8.5 points on clean data and 7.4 under 60% injected contradiction. Crossing merge and rendering shows the halves are inseparable (the typed merge shown flat is the worst arm), while three conflict policies are indistinguishable, so the conflict log that motivated this work is not the On τ-bench retail, each compendium arm improves GPT-4o agents' per-step tool-call accuracy by at least 6.7 points, attributed to format rather than federated experience. Two cautionary findings conclude the paper: on a topic-labeled math proxy and StableToolBench, a TF-IDF classifier over the same labeled experience beats every LLM routing arm (by 48 and 26 points, mostly retrieval recall) because the benchmark's pool holds labeled queries for every supposedly unseen tool and every test query verbatim before our filter. It cannot measure routing to tools without labels, which routing exists for.

</details>


### [157] [XYBench: Can LLMs Respond Pragmatically to Queries with Misconceptions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06842)
*Akhila Yerukola,Jena D. Hwang,Mingqian Zheng,Jenna Godsey,Hyunwoo Kim,Valentina Pyatkin,Jennifer Hu,Maarten Sap*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出XYBench——一个包含8,115个带有误解的用户查询（XY问题）的基准数据集，用于评估LLM能否识别用户的潜在误解并提供务实性解决方案。实验表明即便是最强的LLM也主要回答字面问题而非用户真正意图，在识别误解方面显著落后于人类（最多63% vs. 79-90%），表明务实性重定向是当前LLM尚未充分发展的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非专家用户向LLM提问时常常带有误解（如"如何用正则表达式解析XML"），这被称为XY问题。现有评估主要关注模型能否回答字面问题，但忽略了模型是否能够识别用户的根本误解并引导其走向真正解决问题的方案。这一能力差距亟需系统性的基准测试来量化和研究。

Method: 作者构建了XYBench基准，包含8,115个查询，来自技术领域（StackOverflow/StackExchange）和日常领域（WikiHow及人工编写子集）。设计了一个基于合作性回应理论的评估范式，从三个维度评估模型回应：（a）是否存在务实性解决方案，（b）对其强调程度，以及（c）对误解的识别。此外还进行了Oracle消融实验（在生成时提供明确用户意图）以及多项选择设置下的模型偏好测试。

Result: 最强的LLM也主要回答字面请求（0.75-0.92），而较少回答用户真正意图（0.33-0.71）。在识别误解方面，模型至多达到63%，而人类达到79-90%。模型在多选情境下压倒性地偏好务实性回应，但在生成任务中却始终无法产生此类回应。Oracle消融实验表明提供明确的用户意图有帮助，但仍存在较大差距。

Conclusion: 务实性重定向（pragmatic redirection）是当前LLM中一项根本性未充分发展的能力。即使强模型在生成自由文本时也无法将对误解的识别和务实性引导有效转化为实际输出，研究验证了该能力的缺失并非由于缺乏知识或偏好，而是生成层面的能力瓶颈。

Abstract: When non-expert users ask LLMs for assistance, their queries can often have misconceptions (e.g., "How do I parse XML with regex?"). In such cases, often referred to as the XY-problem, LLMs must identify the misconception ("regex are fragile") and meaningfully direct the user toward a pragmatic solution that will address the root problem implicit in the request ("use an XML parser"). We introduce XYBench, a benchmark of 8,115 such queries, drawn from technical (StackOverflow/StackExchange) and everyday (WikiHow and a manually-curated subset) domains. We design an evaluation paradigm that assesses model responses along three criteria grounded in cooperative response theory: (a) presence and (b) emphasis on pragmatic solutions, and (c) identification of misconceptions. Our experiments show that even the strongest LLMs predominantly answer the literal request (0.75--0.92) and far less often the intended one (0.33--0.71), while substantially lagging behind humans at identifying misconceptions (at most 63% vs. 79--90%). Further, models overwhelmingly prefer pragmatic responses in a multiple choice setting yet consistently fail to generate them. Oracle ablation experiments show that providing explicit user intent at generation time helps; however a large gap remains, suggesting pragmatic redirection is a fundamentally underdeveloped capability in current LLMs.

</details>


### [158] [You Are What You Read: Misalignment via In-Context Persona Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06851)
*Kyuhee Kim,Benjamin Berczi,Cozmin Ududec*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现仅通过将关于某个人物的平凡传记事实以普通对话轮次形式放入模型上下文，无需微调或在提示中展示有害行为示范，就能诱导模型产生"人格诱导"——使模型以该人物的身份回答问题，并可能在这些事实从未涉及的问题上表现出该人物的特征性观点，造成对齐失调。九个人格、十三个模型中，身份采纳随事实数量呈S形增长，通常在3到10个事实内突破50%。无害人格可实现接近零失调的完整采纳，而有害人格在无关问题上以高达80%的比例表达其观点。格式指令可控制人格激活时机，累积传记上下文被内容过滤器标记的比例远低于等效直接指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已表明微调或上下文中直接示范不良行为可产生广泛的对齐失调，但本文旨在探究是否仅靠无害的传记事实数据（不含有害行为示范）即可在上下文中引发对齐失调，从而揭示大语言模型更微妙的安全漏洞。

Method: 通过在模型上下文中以普通对话轮次形式放置聚焦于单一人物的大量传记事实（不涉及目标问题的答案或有害行为的展示），观察模型是否采纳该人物身份，并在标准测试问题上评估其行为。实验覆盖九种人格、十三个模型，测量身份采纳率随事实数量的变化曲线，并对比有害与无害人格的结果差异，同时检验格式指令对人格激活的控制作用及内容过滤器对两类输入的标记率差异。

Result: 身份采纳率随传记事实数量呈S形（逻辑斯蒂）增长，50%采纳阈值出现在3到10个事实之间。无害人格达到完整采纳且几乎无对齐失调；有害人格在未被事实覆盖的无关问题上以高达80%的比例表达该人物的特征性观点。内容过滤器仅标记3%的传记上下文输入，而对等效的直接指令则标记24%-33%。

Conclusion: 仅通过无害且彼此独立的传记事实的累积，就能在上下文中诱导模型产生实质性的人格转换和对齐失调，且规避了现有内容过滤机制。这揭示了一种此前未知的、难以检测的提示注入类安全风险，需要通过改进的评估和对策来加以防范。

Abstract: Broad misalignment has been produced by finetuning on narrow data, harmful or benign, and in context only by demonstrations of the undesirable behaviour itself. We show that benign data suffices in context, with no finetuning and no demonstration of harmful behaviour in the prompt. Biographical facts that converge on a single figure, placed in a model's context as ordinary conversational turns, lead it to answer as that figure on questions the facts never touch. We call this persona induction. Across nine personas and thirteen models, identity adoption rises sigmoidally with the number of facts and crosses 50% within 3 to 10 of them. Misalignment then tracks which figure is described. Harmless personas reach full adoption with near-zero misalignment, while harmful ones voice their characteristic views on unrelated questions, at rates up to 80%. A formatting instruction can gate when the persona activates. Because each fact is individually benign, accumulated biographical context is flagged by content filters on 3% of inputs against 24-33% for an equivalent direct instruction.

</details>


### [159] [AutoLexSteer: Automatic Contrast Construction for Lexical Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06879)
*Shuhe Wang,Lachlan Cowley,Eduard Hovy,Jey Han Lau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Hangman（一类基于词义的steering vector）和AutoLexSteer（首个全自动构建steering vector的流程），利用WordNet中提取的近义词族来指定需要规避的steering source和期望的steering target，实现词级和词义集合级的精确引导，并能调控诸如sycophancy等LLM行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Steering vectors是引导LLM输出的有效方法，但由于嵌入空间的不可解释性，手动构建精确的steering vectors非常困难且昂贵。

Method: 提出Hangman和AutoLexSteer：AutoLexSteer从WordNet抽取紧密相关的近义词族，分别定义要避免的源词族和要引导的目标词族，从而自动构造利用词义信息的steering vectors（即Hangman类型）。

Result: 构造的steering vectors非常精确，可在词级以及多组词义（语义）集合层级进行引导，并能有效调控如sycophancy等特定LLM行为。

Conclusion: AutoLexSteer实现了steering vector的完全自动化构建，提升了引导精度与可操作性，相关工作代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Steering vectors have rapidly emerged as a popular and effective method for guiding the output of LLMs in very specific ways. But constructing accurate steering vectors is a difficult manual process due to the opacity of embeddings. We introduce Hangman, a novel type of steering vector that operates using word senses, as well as AutoLexSteer, the first fully automated process for building steering vectors. AutoLexSteer employs families of closely-related words extracted from WordNet to specify both the steering source to be avoided and the desired steering target. The steering vectors are quite precise, can be used to steer at the level of words and sets of word senses (meanings), and are able to steer certain LLM behaviors like sycophancy. The dataset and code can be found at https://github.com/ShuheWang1998/autolexsteer.

</details>


### [160] [Towards Bridging the Gap Between Offline and Iterative Alignment via Preference Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06893)
*Wenbo Zhang,Wenzhuo Zhou,Hengrui Cai,Zhengling Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DP3O（蒸馏偏好概率策略优化），一种高效的离线对齐算法。通过对照实验发现迭代DPO优于离线方法的关键在于显式偏好模型，DP3O先利用辅助LLM学习显式偏好模型，再将其知识蒸馏到策略优化中。理论证明显式偏好建模比隐式形式有更好的误差控制，DP3O通过方差缩减获得比硬标签DPO更紧的泛化界。实验表明DP3O优于现有离线方法，性能媲美迭代DPO，并节省约42%训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答两个关键问题：（i）为什么迭代DPO方法通常优于离线方法？（ii）迭代方法的优势能否融入离线对齐？通过研究这两个问题，希望设计一种既有迭代方法性能又保持离线方法简单高效的算法。

Method: 首先通过受控实验揭示了迭代DPO优于离线方法的根本原因在于其额外引入的显式偏好模型；基于此洞察，提出DP3O算法，分为两个阶段：（1）使用辅助LLM类别学习一个显式偏好模型；（2）将该显式偏好模型的知识蒸馏到策略优化中。理论上证明了显式偏好建模比隐式公式具有更好的估计误差控制，且DP3O通过方差缩减获得比硬标签DPO更紧的泛化界。

Result: 在广泛的基于聊天和下游任务上评估DP3O，结果表明其优于最先进的离线对齐方法，性能可与迭代DPO相媲美，并且比迭代DPO减少约42%的训练时间，展示了方法的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: DP3O成功地将迭代DPO的优势（显式偏好建模）融入到离线对齐框架中，在保持离线方法效率的同时实现了接近迭代方法的性能，为LLM对齐提供了一个兼顾效果和效率的新方案。

Abstract: Direct preference optimization DPO is a promising offline approach for aligning large language models (LLMs) due to its simplicity, computational efficiency, and implicit modeling of human preferences. Interestingly, iterative extensions of DPO have achieved stronger performance on academic benchmarks, raising two key questions: (i) Why do iterative methods generally outperform offline ones? (ii) Can their advantages be incorporated into offline alignment? To answer the first question, our controlled experiments reveal that the explicit preference model, additionally introduced in the iterative procedure, is a key factor behind its superiority over offline methods. This insight leads us to answer the second question affirmatively and propose Distilled Preference Probability Policy Optimization (DP3O), an effective and efficient offline alignment algorithm. DP3O first learns an explicit preference model using a helper class of LLMs and then distills its knowledge into policy optimization. Theoretically, we show that explicit preference modeling admits better estimation error control than implicit formulations, and that DP3O achieves a tighter generalization bound than hard-label DPO through variance reduction. Empirically, we evaluate DP3O on a wide range of chat-based and downstream tasks and show that it outperforms state-of-the-art offline methods, achieves performance comparable to iterative DPO, and reduces training time by about $42\%$, demonstrating both its effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [161] [Dynamic-Programming-Guided Hierarchical BPE and Empirical Analysis of Vocabulary Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06898)
*Kenny Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DH-BPE（动态规划引导的分层BPE），通过动态规划评估候选词元效用，并结合词元暴露频率和BPE层次依赖进行剪枝，在固定词表大小下实现比标准BPE和多种基线更好的压缩效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准BPE通过贪心对合并构建词表，但其合并顺序并不保证在固定模型可见词表大小下实现最优压缩。需要一种能够权衡词元暴露（确切最小词元切分下）与层次依赖关系的词表构建方法。

Method: DH-BPE首先从适度过采样的BPE候选词表出发，利用动态规划在确切最小词元切分下度量候选词元的效用，然后通过暴露引导（exposure-guided）且依赖感知（dependency-aware）的剪枝策略，从候选词表中选取固定大小的模型可见词表。研究在12K和16K目标词表大小下与标准BPE、Pruned BPE、MinGram和MinGram-PP进行对比，并在18K下额外与MinGram对比。

Result: 在主要12K和16K对比中，DH-BPE在共享确切最小词元DP编码器下的一致聚合压缩上优于标准BPE、Pruned BPE和MinGram。MinGram-PP在主要对比中展现出更强的聚合压缩，但在过采样因子f=2.0和f=3.0的跨语料评估中DH-BPE表现更好；在12K规模下，MinGram-PP仅在f=4.0和f=5.0的更大候选池中逆转该排序。定性分析显示DH-BPE较好平衡了较晚更完整的BPE合并与可复用的子词组件。

Conclusion: DH-BPE提供了一种在固定模型可见词表预算下改进词表分配的实用方法，通过暴露引导和依赖感知的剪枝策略，在压缩性能和词元可复用性之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Byte Pair Encoding (BPE) constructs vocabularies through greedy pair merging, but the resulting merge order does not necessarily allocate a fixed model-visible vocabulary optimally for compression. We propose Dynamic-Programming-Guided Hierarchical BPE (DH-BPE), a vocabulary-construction method that combines token exposure under exact minimum-token segmentation with the hierarchical dependencies induced by BPE training. Starting from a modestly overshot BPE candidate vocabulary, DH-BPE uses dynamic programming to measure candidate utility and applies exposure-guided, dependency-aware pruning to select a fixed-size model-visible vocabulary. We compare DH-BPE against Standard BPE and recent vocabulary-optimization baselines, including Pruned BPE, MinGram, and MinGram-PP, in primary evaluations at 12K and 16K target vocabulary sizes, with an additional 18K evaluation against MinGram only. Across the primary 12K and 16K comparisons, DH-BPE consistently improves aggregate compression over Standard BPE, Pruned BPE, and MinGram under a shared exact minimum-token DP encoder. MinGram-PP achieves stronger aggregate compression in the primary comparisons, but DH-BPE outperforms it at overshoot factors f = 2.0 and f = 3.0 in cross-corpus evaluation; at 12K, MinGram-PP reverses this ordering only with the substantially larger candidate pools at f = 4.0 and f = 5.0. Qualitative analysis further shows that DH-BPE balances later, more complete BPE merges with reusable subword components, providing a practical approach to improving vocabulary allocation under a fixed model-visible vocabulary budget.

</details>


### [162] [TurEngMix: A Text Corpus and Benchmark for Turkish-English Code-Mixed Language Identification and Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06963)
*Ilayda Dogan,Phuong-Anh Nguyen-Le,Julia Mendelsohn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TurEngMix，一个包含5.5K条自然发生的土耳其语-英语语码混合社交媒体帖子（486,974个词元）的语料库，并从中构建了用于语码混合语言识别（LID）和命名实体识别（NER）的基准。评估发现所有模型在混合语言词元上错误率很高，形态学整合的混合词元将NER错误率提升5.2-6.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言处理系统在语码混合文本上表现不佳，尤其是低资源语言对。土耳其语-英语尤为困难，因为英语词干可以与土耳其语后缀结合形成单个混合语言词元，这给现有NLP模型带来了独特的挑战。

Method: 构建了5.5K条自然社交媒体帖子的TurEngMix语料库（486,974个词元），并从中标注了15K个专家标注词元，构建了土耳其语-英语语码混合的LID和NER基准。评估了解码器LLM和微调编码器基线模型。

Result: 单语土耳其语和英语词元可以被可靠标注，但所有模型在混合语言词元的LID和NER上错误率都很高。对于形态学整合的词元，GPT-4o和Qwen的NER错误率分别高出5.2倍和6.3倍。

Conclusion: 形态学整合仍然是语码混合情境下NLP模型的主要挑战。作者发布了语料库、标注和代码，以支持未来关于土耳其语-英语语码混合的计算和社会语言学研究。

Abstract: Natural language processing systems underperform on code-mixed text, particularly for low-resource language pairs. Turkish-English poses a further challenge: it lets English stems combine with Turkish suffixes to form single mixed-language tokens. We introduce TurEngMix, a corpus of 5.5K noisy, naturally occurring social media posts (486,974 tokens) rich in Turkish-English code-mixing. From this corpus, we construct a new Turkish-English benchmark for code-mixed language identification (LID) and named entity recognition (NER), comprising 15K expert-annotated tokens. Evaluating both decoder LLM and fine-tuned encoder baselines, we find that monolingual Turkish and English tokens are labeled reliably, but all models have high error rates on mixed-language tokens for both LID and NER. For morphologically integrated tokens, NER error rates were 5.2x and 6.3x higher for GPT-4o and Qwen, respectively. This highlights how morphological integration remains a challenge. We release the corpus, annotations, and code to support future computational and sociolinguistic research on Turkish-English code-mixing.

</details>


### [163] [CantoneseLLM v2: Reasoning in a Low-Resource Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06970)
*Tsz Chung Cheng,Chung Shing Cheng,Chaak Ming Lau,Cheuk Hei Chong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布了CantoneseLLM v2，一组基于Qwen3 8B和30B-A3B的粤语大模型，通过CPT、chat-vector merging、SFT、DPO和RLVR多阶段训练而成，并系统分析了各训练阶段对推理行为和模型性能的影响。30B-A3B模型在HKCanto-Eval上达到73.16分，与融合后checkpoint仅差1.20分，同时保留了该checkpoint所不具备的粤语推理行为。作者同时发布了模型权重、训练环境和包含十三年繁体中文Common Crawl数据的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粤语虽然使用广泛，但在书面数据上属于低资源语言，缺乏可用于模型训练的大规模母语粤语推理轨迹语料，导致粤语模型的推理能力不足。

Method: 采用基于Qwen3 8B和30B-A3B的多阶段训练流程：先在7.84亿个粤语及香港相关token上进行CPT（持续预训练），接着进行chat-vector merging（聊天向量融合）、SFT（监督微调）、DPO（直接偏好优化）和RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）。RLVR阶段将粤语语言和繁体中文脚本作为乘法性约束加入训练。

Result: 实验揭示了训练阶段间的关键交互：chat-vector merging能迁移指令跟随能力但保留捐赠模型的推理语言；有限粤语推理数据上的SFT会大幅缩短或删除推理轨迹并降低基准性能；DPO能恢复推理块格式（对8B模型尤其明显），但只能恢复部分性能损失；加入粤语语言和繁体中文脚本约束的RLVR训练实现了粤语语言对齐并恢复了损失的性能。30B-A3B模型在HKCanto-Eval达到73.16分。

Conclusion: CantoneseLLM v2通过多阶段训练成功构建了具备粤语推理能力的低资源语言模型，且RLVR训练被证明是恢复并对齐粤语推理行为的关键步骤。作者公开了全部模型、训练环境及十三年中文Common Crawl数据集以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Cantonese is widely spoken but remains low-resource in written data, with no large corpus of native Cantonese reasoning traces available for model training. We develop and release CantoneseLLM v2, comprising models based on Qwen3 8B and 30B-A3B. The models are trained through CPT on 784 million Cantonese and Hong Kong-related tokens, chat-vector merging, SFT, DPO, and RLVR. Evaluation across the training stages shows that chat-vector merging transfers instruction following but preserves the donor model's reasoning language, while SFT with limited Cantonese reasoning data substantially shortens or removes reasoning traces and reduces benchmark performance. DPO restores the reasoning-block format, particularly for the 8B model, but recovers only part of the lost performance. The RLVR training with Cantonese language and Traditional Chinese scripts as multiplicative constraints introduced Cantonese language alignment and restored the lost performance. The 30B-A3B model reaches 73.16 on HKCanto-Eval, within 1.20 points of its merged checkpoint, while retaining the Cantonese reasoning behaviour absent from that checkpoint. We release the model checkpoints, the training environments, and a thirteen-year Traditional Chinese Common Crawl dataset. The models can be accessed at https://huggingface.co/collections/hon9kon9ize/cantonesellm-v20

</details>


### [164] [Train Overcomplete, Deploy Compact: Scaling Recovery Capacity for Structured LLM Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06974)
*Seungmin Oh,Donggeon Lee,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出OverRep框架，通过"训练时过度完备、部署时紧凑"的策略解决结构化剪枝后恢复模块容量与移除知识复杂度不匹配（容量-知识不对称）的问题，在三个骨干家族上比强基线提升最多5.5/8.4个点（25%/50%剪枝率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化剪枝能降低LLM部署的内存、延迟和能耗成本，但其恢复阶段常因恢复模块的表示能力与被移除知识的复杂度不匹配而受限，这一瓶颈被定义为"容量-知识不对称"。

Method: 提出OverRep框架，核心思想是"训练过完备、部署紧凑"：在恢复训练期间对恢复模块做暂时性的过度参数化（overparameterization）以吸收蒸馏自原模型的复杂知识；恢复完成后，将过完备重参数化通过代数方式合并为数学等价的紧凑模块，保持推理时架构与计算开销不变。同时引入退火激活（annealed activation），在训练中提供非线性动力学，同时收敛到线性区域以实现精确的代数合并。

Result: 在三个骨干家族上，OverRep相比强恢复基线在25%和50%剪枝率下分别提升最多5.5和8.4个推理性能点，同时内存使用和TFLOPs与现有恢复方法相当。

Conclusion: OverRep有效缓解了容量-知识不对称对结构化剪枝恢复阶段的限制，以可忽略的开销显著提升剪枝后模型保留的推理能力，且不改变推理架构与计算成本。

Abstract: Large language models achieve strong performance across diverse tasks, but deployment remains costly because of memory, latency, and energy demands. Structured pruning reduces these costs by removing architectural components, yet its recovery stage is often limited by a mismatch between the recovery module's representational capacity and the complexity of the removed knowledge. We call this bottleneck the capacity-knowledge asymmetry and propose OverRep, an Overcomplete Reparameterization framework for structured LLM pruning. Following the principle of "train overcomplete, deploy compact", OverRep temporarily overparameterizes the recovery module during training to absorb complex knowledge distilled from the original model. After recovery, the overcomplete re-parameterization is algebraically merged into a mathematically equivalent compact module, preserving the pruned model's inference-time architecture and computational cost. OverRep further introduces an annealed activation that enables nonlinear training dynamics while converging to a linear regime for exact algebraic merging. Across three backbone families, OverRep improves retained reasoning performance over strong recovery baselines by up to 5.5 and 8.4 points at 25% and 50% pruning, respectively, while keeping memory usage and TFLOPs comparable to existing recovery methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/mmai-laboratory/OverRep.

</details>


### [165] [Aha-Flow Distillation: Flow Markers Matter in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07036)
*Xiaodong Wang,Peixi Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow Moment（一种以"我在做"类过程确认性语言为特征的推理模式），并据此构建Flow-CoT——通过重写原始推理轨迹的语篇标记而保留推理内容的辅助监督数据。作者提出Aha-Flow Distillation (AFD)框架，这是on-policy self-distillation (OPSD)的双模式扩展：Aha分支保留简洁基于解的监督，Flow分支引入重写后的Flow-CoT。推理时仅使用标准反思指令，Flow风格推理仅作训练信号。在AIME25和HMMT25上，Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B均有稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注Aha Moment（以修订和回溯为导向的推理过程），而忽略了另一种同样重要的推理模式——由持续、过程确认性言语表征的Flow Moment。作者希望证明，将这两种异质的推理监督模式以适当的组织方式结合，可以超越单一模式的自我蒸馏效果。

Method: 1）识别并形式化定义Flow Moment与Flow Markers；2）通过重写原始推理轨迹中的语篇标记构建Flow-CoT数据，保持底层推理内容不变；3）提出Aha-Flow Distillation框架，作为OPSD的双模式扩展，将不同形式的特权信息与对应的推理指令配对（Aha分支与Flow分支）；4）推理阶段仅使用标准反思指令，确保训练与推理指令一致。

Result: 在AIME25和HMMT25上，AFD在Qwen3-8B上将Avg@12从60.8提升至61.3，在Qwen3-4B上从57.5提升至58.6（相对复现的OPSD基线）。受控消融实验表明，在相同Flow-CoT/Aha-CoT组成下，双模式训练将Avg@12从59.5提升至60.1，说明收益不仅来自引入异构推理监督，还来自其在自蒸馏过程中的组织方式。

Conclusion: Flow-CoT与AFD框架验证了引入异构推理风格监督的自我蒸馏方法可以有效提升推理能力；性能提升来源于监督信号的异构性及其在蒸馏过程中的结构化组织双重因素。该工作为推理自我蒸馏提供了一种新的多模态监督组织范式。

Abstract: We identify the Flow Moment, a reasoning pattern characterized by sustained, process-confirming verbalizations such as I'm doing, in contrast to the revision- and backtracking-oriented Aha Moment. We refer to their corresponding linguistic expressions as Flow Markers and Aha Markers, respectively. Based on this observation, we construct Flow-CoT by rewriting the discourse markers of original reasoning traces while preserving their underlying reasoning content, and use it as auxiliary supervision for on-policy self-distillation (OPSD). We further propose \textbf{Aha-Flow Distillation (AFD)}, a dual-mode extension of OPSD that pairs different forms of privileged information with corresponding reasoning instructions. The Aha branch retains concise solution-based supervision, while the Flow branch introduces rewritten Flow-CoT under a direct and confident reasoning instruction. At inference time, the model uses only the standard reflective instruction, so Flow-style reasoning serves purely as a training signal. Experiments on AIME25 and HMMT25 show consistent improvements across Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B: AFD improves Avg@12 from 60.8 to 61.3 on Qwen3-8B and from 57.5 to 58.6 on Qwen3-4B over our reproduced OPSD baselines. Controlled ablations further show that, with the same Flow-CoT/Aha-CoT composition, dual-mode training improves Avg@12 from 59.5 to 60.1, indicating that the benefit comes not only from introducing heterogeneous reasoning supervision, but also from how it is organized during self-distillation. The code is available at https://github.com/Wang-Xiaodong1899/Aha-Flow-Distillation.

</details>


### [166] [A Hyperbolicity Atlas of Large Language Model Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07053)
*Zhichao Yang,Yuanze Hu,Gen Li,Qingchen Yu,Shiying Duan,Xinyu Wang,Ye Qiu,Zeming Liu,Guangxu Chen,Zhaoxin Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了当代LLM中提示词token的隐状态是否展现Gromov双曲性（一种基于距离的树状度量）。通过对10个开源模型在4个数据集上的818,904个样本-层测量，构建了沿参数规模、层深度、模型家族和输入域四个轴的双曲性图谱，发现最主要的模式是层深度而非规模：中间层通常形成高相对双曲性平台，而最终层往往变得更接近树状结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM隐状态虽为普通向量，但向量间的距离可能仍存在层级结构。此前缺乏对模型隐状态是否具有Gromov双曲性这一树状性质的系统性研究，作者希望填补这一空白并将其作为实际诊断工具。

Method: 使用10个开源权重模型，在MATH500、HumanEval、WinoGrande和TruthfulQA四个数据集上进行测量，共采集818,904个样本-层测量值，构建涵盖参数规模、层深度、模型家族和输入域四个维度的Gromov双曲性图谱。

Result: 最清晰的模式是深度而非规模：中间层通常形成高相对双曲性平台，而末层往往变得更像树状。规模效应弱且非单调；同规模（7/8B）不同模型家族差异显著；输入域与模型专门化存在交互作用。

Conclusion: Gromov双曲性可作为实用的诊断工具：它能揭示层级距离结构出现在何处、模型专门化如何改变它，以及哪些模型-层-域组合值得进一步深入分析。
