Abstract: LLM hidden states are ordinary vectors, but the distances among those vectors may still show hierarchical structure. To our knowledge, this paper is the first systematic study of whether prompt-token hidden states in contemporary LLMs exhibit Gromov Hyperbolicity (GH), a distance-based measure of tree-likeness. Using 818,904 sample-layer measurements from ten open-weight models across MATH500, HumanEval, WinoGrande, and TruthfulQA, we build a GH map over four axes: parameter scale, layer depth, model family, and input domain. The clearest pattern is depth, not scale: middle layers usually form a high-relative-hyperbolicity plateau, while final layers often become substantially more tree-like. Scale effects are weak and non-monotonic, matched 7/8B model families differ strongly, and domains interact with model specialization. These findings make GH useful as a practical diagnostic: it shows where hierarchical distance structure appears, how specialization changes it, and which model-layer-domain comparisons deserve closer analysis.

</details>


### [167] [Where to Look and What to Use: Retrieve-Localize-Generate for Long-Term Conversational Memory Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07093)
*Yifan Wang,Xinkui Lin,Yongxiu Xu,Shen Gao,Ruochen Yang,Kun Huang,Yubin Wang,Jie Wu,Wei Liu,Jian Luan,Hongbo Xu,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MemLoc框架，通过统一的检索-定位-生成流程解决长程会话记忆问答中证据碎片化与噪声内容引发的lost-in-the-middle问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法在长期会话记忆问答中面临两大挑战：(1) 证据碎片化，相关证据分散在不同时间段的会话中；(2) 检索到的会话内容中存在噪声，触发lost-in-the-middle效应，降低模型回答质量。

Method: MemLoc是一个统一的Retrieve-Localize-Generate框架。检索阶段将每个会话分解为多粒度记忆单元，通过内部记忆图进行查询路由并采用基于熵的粒度选择；同时通过跨会话记忆图建模语义与时间依赖，实现由粗到精的top-K记忆候选检索。定位阶段引入基于推理的证据定位器，使用Self-reflective Hint Policy Optimization (SHPO)训练，通过渐进式精化提取查询相关片段抑制噪声，并跨候选重排序去除冗余，生成带轻量位置ID的紧凑证据集。生成阶段利用这些位置ID作为精确的grounding信号，引导LLM定位到正确记忆位置，缓解lost-in-the-middle效应并保持原始上下文完整性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MemLoc在检索准确性和响应质量上均达到最先进水平，同时保持高效性。

Conclusion: MemLoc通过统一的检索-定位-生成框架，有效解决了长程会话记忆问答中证据碎片化和噪声干扰问题，取得了SOTA性能并兼顾效率。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) enables large language models (LLMs) to answer questions by accessing external knowledge and has been widely adopted for long-term conversational memory question answering. However, existing methods suffer from two key challenges: (1) fragmented evidence scattered across temporally distant sessions, and (2) noisy content within retrieved sessions that triggers the lost-in-the-middle effect. To address these challenges, we propose MemLoc, a unified Retrieve-Localize-Generate framework for long-term conversational memory QA. For retrieval, MemLoc decomposes each session into multi-granularity memory units and performs query routing via an inner-memory graph with entropy-based granularity selection. It further models cross-session semantic and temporal dependencies through a cross-memory graph, enabling coarse-to-fine retrieval of top-K relevant memory candidates. For localization, we introduce a reasoning-based evidence locator trained with Self-reflective Hint Policy Optimization (SHPO), which performs progressive refinement by extracting query-relevant fragments within memory units to suppress noise and reranking across candidates to remove redundancy, producing a compact evidence set with lightweight location IDs. For generation, these IDs act as precise grounding signals that guide the LLM to the correct memory positions, mitigating the lost-in-the-middle effect while preserving original contextual integrity. Extensive experiments on four benchmarks demonstrate that MemLoc achieves state-of-the-art retrieval accuracy and response quality while maintaining efficiency. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Nikol-coder/MemLoc.

</details>


### [168] [Revisiting Complete Reasoning Traces for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07103)
*Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Byeongho Heo,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出：在LLM后训练（如SFT）中，完整的推理轨迹并非必要且存在大量冗余，仅用轨迹端点（起点和答案）训练即可获得同样甚至更好的推理能力，且该方法对RL和on-policy蒸馏等后训练方法同样有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理后训练通常依赖完整的长推理轨迹，但轨迹中存在大量复杂的中间路径和绕路（detours）。作者质疑LLM是否真的需要学习完整的推理轨迹才能提升推理能力，希望通过研究冗余性来重新审视这一常见做法。

Method: 1) 进行pilot study对比full trajectories与partial trajectories在后训练中的效果；2) 通过注意力分析（attention-based analyses）和受控token移除实验（controlled token-removal studies）揭示中间token对最终推理质量的贡献极小；3) 提出利用端点（endpoints）进行后训练的方法，并扩展到RL和on-policy蒸馏等后训练范式。

Result: 发现完整轨迹带来的收益有限，而部分轨迹即使在重度截断下依然有效；中间token对推理质量的贡献可忽略不计；仅用端点训练即可通过内部知识推断缺失步骤，产生一致的推理行为变化，并同样适用于RL和on-policy蒸馏。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹中存在大量冗余信息，完整推理轨迹在后训练中可能并非必要；未来应重新审视在SFT及更广泛后训练方法中使用完整推理轨迹的做法，考虑基于端点的更为高效的训练策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often post-trained on pre-collected reasoning trajectories to improve their reasoning capability. Such trajectories tend to be long due to complex, interwoven paths, which often include detours on the path toward the answer. However, it has been underexplored whether LLMs indeed benefit from learning complete trajectories in post-training, such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Starting from our pilot study, we find that full trajectories provide only limited benefit, while partial trajectories are effective even under heavy truncation. We analyze redundancy in reasoning trajectories through attention-based analyses and controlled token-removal studies, both of which show that intermediate tokens contribute minimally to final reasoning quality. This suggests that avoiding redundant information may allow LLMs to internally infer coherent alternatives by inferring missing steps from their internal knowledge, given known trajectory endpoints. Furthermore, we show that training LLMs using endpoints leads to consistent changes in reasoning behavior, and that it also benefits post-training methods based on reinforcement learning or on-policy distillation, highlighting the need to revisit complete reasoning traces. Code is available at https://github.com/naver-ai/revisiting-trace.

</details>


### [169] [The Illusion of Debiasing: Persona Steering Redistributes Rather Than Reduces Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07117)
*Ziyue Feng,Hongbo Fang,James A. Evans*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究以人格条件作为受控探针，沿深度轴（自我报告→开放式生成→词级参数联想）测量提示干预对三个指令微调模型的影响，发现人格提示可被表面读取但不重构内部结构——模型能遵循单一特征指令却无法复现人类特征间协方差，且随深度增加该解离加深，表明提示驱动的引导作用于输出通道，存在表面可操作难以掩盖的结构到达极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统提示、人设、角色指令等基于提示的干预能可靠地改变语言模型的输出，但尚不清楚这种改变达到何种层级——是真正重构了模型的内部表征结构，还是仅对输出通道进行表层调制？理解这一点对评估模型的可控性与可靠性至关重要。

Method: 采用人格条件化作为受控探针。沿深度轴设计三层测量：自我报告（closed-form QA）、开放式生成、以及词级参数联想任务。实验覆盖三个指令微调模型，通过比较模型内部特征相关性与人类种间协方差、以及人格提示对不同深度指标的差异化影响来考察解离现象。

Result: 发现一种分层解离：人格提示在表面层面可读取（legible）但非结构性（not structural）——模型能够遵循单一特征指令，却无法复现人类特征间的协方差结构。解离随深度加深而增强：人格提示保持或放大闭式问答偏差，改变绝对语气但使组间差异不变，且对已饱和的联想基线几乎无扰动。

Conclusion: 基于提示的操控主要作用于输出通道，存在结构性的到达极限（structural reach limit），而表面的可操控性（surface manipulability）可能掩盖这一内在限制。该发现提示评估提示有效性的现有方式可能高估了其对模型深层结构的实际影响。

Abstract: Prompt-based interventions: system prompts, personas, role instructions, reliably reshape what a language model says, but it is unclear which layer they reach. Do they reconfigure internal structure, or only modulate the output channel? We use persona conditioning as a controlled probe, measuring its effects along a depth axis from self-report, through open-ended generation, to word-level parametric association, across three instruction-tuned models. We find a graded dissociation. Personas are legible but not structural: models follow single-trait instructions yet fail to reproduce human inter-trait covariance. The dissociation deepens with depth: personas hold or amplify closed-form QA bias, shift absolute tone while leaving between-group disparity unchanged, and barely perturb an already saturated associative baseline. Prompt-based steering thus operates in the output channel and has a structural reach limit that surface manipulability can mask.

</details>


### [170] [PTCG: Persona-guided Tree-based Counterargument Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07120)
*Eunbeen Son,Yohan Jo,Joonsuk Park,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PTCG框架，结合类思维树的分步生成与剪枝以及说话人角色选择，用于生成多样且有说服力的反驳论点，在多样性和说服力上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反驳论点生成方法通常只生成单一的反驳论点，无法捕捉现实辩论中所需的多样性和说服力，限制了批判性思维和均衡对话的表达。

Method: 提出Persona-guided Tree-based Counterargument Generation (PTCG)框架，核心包含两个组件：(1) 从原始论点估计作者的人物特质；(2) 融合代表不同视角的说话人特质，结合受Tree-of-Thoughts启发的分步生成与剪枝策略，实现视角采择并生成多样化的反驳论点。

Result: 通过LLM-as-a-Judge、基于分类器的评估以及人工评估，PTCG在反驳论点的多样性和说服力方面相比基线方法均表现出一致的改进。

Conclusion: PTCG通过角色引导的树状生成框架，有效解决了反驳论点生成的多样性与说服力不足问题，为真实场景中的辩证讨论提供了更均衡的视角支持。

Abstract: The ability to generate counterarguments is important for critical thinking and balanced discourse, yet existing approaches typically produce only a single counterargument, failing to capture the diversity and persuasiveness required in real-world debates. To address this limitation, we propose Persona-guided Tree-based Counterargument Generation (PTCG), a framework that combines Tree-of-Thoughts-inspired step-wise generation and pruning with speaker persona selection. By estimating the author's persona from the original argument and incorporating speaker personas representing distinct perspectives, PTCG operationalizes perspective-taking and enables the generation of diverse counterarguments. Results from LLM-as-a-Judge, classifier-based assessment, and human evaluations indicate that PTCG shows consistent improvements in both the diversity and persuasiveness of counterarguments compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [171] [Line-Coupled Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07129)
*Shiyuan Li,Shaorong Zhang,Zhaorui Yang,Qian Zhang,Greg Ver Steeg,Bingyao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出行耦合语言模型（LCLM），一种以文本行为并行生成单元的自回归模型。LCLM在每个解码步骤同时推进多个文本行，通过共享因果上下文耦合各行，同时保持标准的next-token目标和因果注意力。实验表明，在881M参数下，LCLM每次前向传播平均产生2.94个内容token，验证交叉熵损失为2.44，而标准自回归基线每次仅产生1.00个token且损失为2.39。即使每次前向传播生成16个token时，LCLM的损失（2.34）仅比自回归基线（2.25）高0.09。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型每个解码步骤仅生成一个token，前向传播的有效输出受限。虽然扩散模型、插入式解码和多token预测能实现并行生成，但它们要么增加训练时的token流量，要么难以预测强依赖的未来token。作者旨在设计一种既能并行生成又不需要额外训练开销、同时保持标准next-token目标的模型架构。

Method: LCLM将多个文本行的token交错排列到单一因果序列中，并为每个活跃行预测下一个token。行之间通过共享的因果上下文耦合，采用行交错旋转位置编码（line-staggered rotary positions），完全保留标准的next-token目标和因果注意力机制，避免了额外的训练开销。

Result: 对照实验表明跨行目标之间的依赖性显著低于同行相邻目标，支持文本行作为并行生成单元。在881M参数下，LCLM每次前向传播平均生成2.94个内容token，验证损失为2.44；而基线只生成1.00个token且损失为2.39。LCLM在并行生成16个token时损失仅比基线高0.09（2.34 vs 2.25）。

Conclusion: LCLM验证了以行为单位进行并行自回归生成的可行性：文本行之间的弱依赖性使其成为合适的并行单元，而行间通过共享上下文实现有效耦合。该模型在不改变标准训练目标的前提下显著提升了每次前向传播的有效输出，展示了在多token并行生成中精度与效率的良好权衡。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models generate one token per decoding step, limiting the useful output of each forward pass. Although diffusion models, insertion-based decoding, and multi-token prediction enable parallel generation, they either incur additional training-time token traffic or struggle to predict strongly dependent future tokens. We introduce the Line-Coupled Language Model (LCLM), an autoregressive model that advances multiple text lines together by predicting the next token for every active line while coupling the lines through shared causal context. LCLM interleaves line tokens into a single causal sequence and uses line-staggered rotary positions, retaining the standard next-token objective and causal attention. Controlled experiments show that cross-line targets are substantially less dependent than consecutive same-line targets, supporting lines as parallel generation units. With 881M parameters, LCLM produces an average of 2.94 content tokens per forward pass with a validation cross-entropy loss of 2.44, compared with 1.00 token per forward pass and a loss of 2.39 for the vanilla autoregressive baseline. Most notably, even when LCLM generates 16 tokens per forward pass, its loss is only 0.09 higher than that of the vanilla autoregressive baseline (2.34 vs. 2.25).

</details>


### [172] [Retrieval-Augmented Multi-Prompt Ensemble for Minor-Grain Breeding Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07134)
*Hang Zhao,Jiahao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RAME（检索增强多提示词集成）框架，用于CCL2026-Eval任务5的小众谷物育种信息抽取（MGBIE），该框架无需训练，通过混合检索进行少量样本选择、三提示词集成（严格/宽松/平衡）以及大规模重复采样多数投票，在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上取得总分0.499，排名第一，较GPT-5.5驱动的官方基线提升11.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小众谷物育种文献中需要联合抽取12种实体类型和6种关系类型，现有方法在该任务上表现有限，需要一种无需训练且能有效提升抽取精度的方法。

Method: RAME包含三个核心组件：(i) 基于混合BM25-embedding检索器的检索增强少量样本选择；(ii) 横跨精确率到召回率谱系的三提示词集成（Strict, Relaxed, Balanced）；(iii) 大规模重复采样结合多数投票来过滤噪声预测。系统基于DeepSeek-V4-Flash构建。

Result: 在官方排行榜上取得Total Score 0.499（NER 0.730, RE 0.346），排名第一，超过由GPT-5.5驱动的官方Track-A基线（0.448），实现11.4%的相对提升。

Conclusion: RAME验证了训练免费框架在领域信息抽取任务上的有效性，通过受控多样性和多数投票策略能有效提升LLM输出的一致性和置信度。

Abstract: This paper presents our system for CCL2026-Eval Task 5: Minor-Grain Breeding Information Extraction (MGBIE), which jointly extracts 12 entity types and 6 relation types from minor-grain breeding literature. We propose RAME (Retrieval-Augmented Multi-Prompt Ensemble), a training-free framework that elicits multiple LLM outputs under controlled diversity and aggregates them by majority voting to obtain high-confidence predictions. RAME combines (i) retrieval-augmented few-shot selection via a hybrid BM25-embedding retriever, (ii) a three-prompt ensemble (Strict, Relaxed, Balanced) spanning the precision to recall spectrum, and (iii) large-scale repeated sampling with majority voting to filter noisy predictions. Built on DeepSeek-V4-Flash, RAME achieves a Total Score of 0.499 (NER 0.730, RE 0.346) on the leaderboard, ranking 1st and surpassing the official Track-A baseline powered by GPT-5.5 (0.448), representing an 11.4% relative improvement. Code is available at https://github.com/king-wang123/CCL26-RAME.

</details>


### [173] [Vishing-Tactics-Bench: Forecasting Exploitation Trajectories in Voice Phishing Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07151)
*Jeongmin Lee,Dongmyung Sul,Seung Yun,Jinxia Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Vishing-Tactics-Bench，一个基于Endsley态势感知框架的语音钓鱼（vishing）防御基准，将事后欺诈分类重塑为实时危害预测。该基准将MITRE ATT&CK适配为6战术分类体系（Vishing-Tactics），对35,340条诈骗话语（来自5,645通合成中文电话）进行标注，并定义了爆炸轨迹预测（Exploitation Trajectory Forecasting）协议，在通话进行中预测其将走向信息收集还是金融利用这两种终端危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音钓鱼（vishing）是实时发生的，待通话结束后再做事后分类已为时已晚，危害已经造成。因此更有价值的问题是：在一通正在进行中的通话中，它正战术性地朝哪种具体危害（信息收集或金融利用）推进？作者认为需要将防御重点从事后欺诈分类转移到实时的危害投影（harm projection），即提前预测通话的最终危害走向，以便在危害发生前采取干预措施。

Method: 作者构建了Vishing-Tactics-Bench基准：(1) 基于Endsley态势感知（SA）框架，将vishing防御重构为逐轮预测通话最终是否达两种终端危害之一的任务；(2) 将MITRE ATT&CK适配为6战术分类体系（Vishing-Tactics）;(3) 在5,645通合成中文通话中对35,340条诈骗话语进行战术标注；(4) 定义Exploitation Trajectory Forecasting（爆炸轨迹预测），一种基于生存分析的协议，包含两个终端危害和三个指标：AP@k、C-index和散度误差（divergence error）；(5) 基线从马尔可夫启发式到微调LLM不等。

Result: 实验结果表明：战术轨迹可作为通话战术状态的可解释表示，支持针对特定危害的预测；在严格虚警预算下的分层领先时间（lead-time）分析进一步识别出通话进程中轨迹信号在哪个时间点能够产生早期预警。

Conclusion: 战术轨迹预测为实时vishing防御提供了可操作的早期预警能力，能够支撑下游应用中在危害发生前选择恰当干预策略。

Abstract: Voice phishing (vishing) unfolds in real time; by the time a call has ended and post-hoc classification is possible, the harm has already been done. The more actionable question is which concrete harm (Information Gathering or Financial Exploitation) an ongoing call is tactically progressing toward. We present Vishing-Tactics-Bench, a benchmark grounded in Endsley's situation-awareness (SA) framework that recasts vishing defense from after-the-fact fraud classification to harm projection: predicting at each turn whether the call will reach either terminal harm. We adapt MITRE ATT&CK to vishing as a 6-tactic taxonomy (Vishing-Tactics) and label 35,340 scammer utterances across 5,645 synthetic Chinese calls. We define Exploitation Trajectory Forecasting, a survival-style protocol over the two terminal harms with three metrics: AP@k, C-index, and divergence error. Baselines ranging from a Markov heuristic to fine-tuned LLMs show that the tactical trajectory serves as an interpretable representation of the call's tactical state, supporting harm-specific forecasting, which can then be used for the downstream application of intervention selection; a stratified lead-time analysis at a tight false-alarm budget further identifies at what point in a call the trajectory signal yields early warning.

</details>


### [174] [FreqBLiMP: Frequency-Controlled Minimal Pairs Reveal Robustness and Fragility of LLMs Under Lexical Rarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07153)
*Tyrone White,Yuki Arase*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 FreqBLiMP，一种频率受控的 BLiMP 扩展基准，在显式 Zipf 频率区间下重新生成全部 67 个范式以保持最小对语法对比，用于测试语言模型在词汇频率变化时语法偏好是否稳定。研究发现词汇频率降低会一致地降低句子似然，但对总体对比可接受性准确率仅造成适度下降，不过这种聚合稳定性掩盖了不同语言现象间的大幅差异：模型在显性构词形态句法泛化上保持稳健，而在依赖词元特定信息（lemma-specific）的现象上表现退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最小对基准（如 BLiMP）在评估语言模型的语言学知识时，忽略了词汇频率这一自然语言使用中普遍存在且高度偏斜（Zipf 分布）的属性。因此现有评估无法验证当对比涉及稀有词汇时语法偏好是否保持稳定，存在评估盲区。

Method: 引入 FreqBLiMP，一个频率受控的 BLiMP 扩展：在显式 Zipf 频率区间下重新生成全部 67 个最小对范式，同时保持每个最小对的语法对比不变。对多个开放权重 LLM 家族进行跨规模评估。

Result: 词汇频率下降会导致句子似然一致且单调地下降，但对比可接受性准确率仅适度下降。然而该聚合稳定性掩盖了跨语言现象的显著差异：模型在显性形态句法泛化上保持稳健，而在依赖词元特定信息（lemma-specific）的现象上发生退化。

Conclusion: 聚合层面的语言能力稳健性具有误导性，频率鲁棒性因语言现象类型而异。模型在需要词元特定知识的语法判断上对词汇频率敏感，凸显了频率控制评估对全面理解 LM 语言学能力的重要性。

Abstract: Minimal-pair benchmarks such as BLiMP evaluate linguistic knowledge by testing whether language models (LMs) prefer acceptable sentences over minimally different unacceptable ones. However, these benchmarks largely ignore lexical frequency variation, despite lexical frequency being a pervasive and highly skewed property of natural language use. Consequently, existing evaluations do not test whether grammatical preferences remain stable when contrasts involve rare lexical items. We introduce FreqBLiMP, a frequency-controlled extension of BLiMP that regenerates all 67 paradigms under explicit Zipf-frequency regimes while preserving each minimal-pair's grammatical contrast. Evaluating multiple open-weight LLM families across scales, we find that decreasing lexical frequency produces a consistent, monotonic decrease in sentence likelihood, but only a modest reduction in overall contrastive acceptability accuracy. However, this aggregate stability masks substantial variation across linguistic phenomena, with LLMs remaining robust on overt morphosyntactic generalization while degrading on phenomena that require lemma-specific information.

</details>


### [175] [In-Place Instruction Following in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07160)
*Zheng Nie,Zherui Li,Jiaming Zhang,Kun Wang,Zhenhong Zhou,Yufei Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）中的原地指令遵循（IIF）任务，构建了IIF-Bench层次化基准，并提出GRAFT后训练框架，将平均IIF分数从57.75提升至73.10（+15.35分）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）通过双向迭代去噪生成文本，天然支持用户指定的任意输出位置约束（即原地提示，IPP），但这一范式缺乏系统的任务形式化、基准评估和针对性优化方法。

Method: 1) 将IPP正式化为原地指令遵循（IIF）任务；2) 构建IIF-Bench层次化基准，覆盖字面、风格和话语功能三类约束，并配套基于rubric的局部-全局评估协议；3) 通过推理时注意力偏置探针发现vanilla dLLMs在去噪过程中对约束片段优先级不足；4) 提出GRAFT，一个面向IPP的后训练框架，结合约束感知的监督微调（SFT）与偏好优化。

Result: 在四个代表性dLLMs上，GRAFT将平均IIF分数从57.75提升至73.10（+15.35分），其中字面约束和话语功能约束分别获得15.91和15.57分的绝对提升，同时保持通用生成能力不受损。

Conclusion: GRAFT能有效提升dLLMs在IIF任务上的表现，验证了面向原地提示范式的专门后训练方法的价值，且不会牺牲模型的通用生成能力。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) generate text via bidirectional iterative denoising, naturally supporting user-specified constraints anchored at arbitrary output positions, a paradigm known as In-place Prompting (IPP). We formalize this as the In-place Instruction Following (IIF) task and construct IIF-Bench, a hierarchical benchmark spanning literal, style, and discourse-function constraints, paired with a rubric-based local-global evaluation protocol. An inference-time attention-bias probe suggests that vanilla dLLMs often under-prioritize constraint spans during denoising. We then propose GRAFT, an IPP-oriented post-training framework combining constraint-aware SFT and preference optimization. On four representative dLLMs, GRAFT raises the average IIF score from 57.75 to 73.10 (+15.35 points), with absolute gains of 15.91 and 15.57 points on literal and discourse-function constraints, while preserving general generation ability.

</details>


### [176] [CircuitLens: Reasoning Circuits as Data Selection Signals for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07183)
*Zhuofan Chen,Ziqian Jiao,Yikai Cui,Zhixin Cai,Jun Bai,Wenge Rong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于推理电路参与度（CRS）的RLVR训练数据选择信号，并发现其对数据选择效果具有条件依赖性：在7B模型上低参与度数据反而优于高参与度数据，且优势仅在特定基准、语料规模和奖励条件下成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR训练对训练数据的选择高度敏感，但现有选择标准（如难度过滤、手工筛选、奖励轨迹评分）都将数据价值视为问题本身的内在属性，忽略了学习模型自身的影响。缺乏考虑模型-数据交互关系的数据选择信号。

Method: 通过对比消融识别出46个对推理敏感的注意力头，利用这些头在冻结基座模型上的单次前向传播计算Circuit Reasoning Score（CRS），无需奖励标签或轨迹生成，以此衡量问题对模型推理电路参与的激活程度，并依据CRS高低对数据进行分档排序。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-7B上，CRS最低的十分位数据在三个中等难度基准上（GSM8K +2.0pp、OlympiadBench +1.6pp、Minerva +2.9pp）优于随机选择，而最高CRS十分位提升更少且与中间十分位无显著差异。但该优势存在边界条件：领域精选数据池中无法区分各选择方法；1.5B规模下有效方向相反；最低奖励训练条件下下游泛化最强。

Conclusion: 在Qwen2.5-Math测试范围内，RLVR数据选择是"机制依赖"的（regime-dependent），而非可简化为问题质量的静态排序。CRS信号反驳了"越强的推理电路参与产生越好的训练数据"这一直觉假设，提示数据选择应结合模型规模、数据池构成和奖励条件等联合考量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is sensitive to which problems a model trains on, yet existing selection criteria--difficulty filtering, hand-curation, reward-trajectory scoring--assess data value as an intrinsic property of problems, independent of the model that will learn from them. We introduce Circuit Reasoning Score (CRS), a selection signal derived from 46 reasoning-sensitive attention heads identified via contrastive ablation, computed in a single forward pass on the frozen base model without reward labels or rollouts. CRS runs against the intuitive hypothesis that stronger reasoning-circuit engagement produces better training data: on Qwen2.5-Math-7B, the lowest-engagement decile improves over random selection on three medium-difficulty benchmarks (GSM8K +2.0 pp, OlympiadBench +1.6 pp, Minerva +2.9 pp), while the highest-engagement decile gains less and is indistinguishable from the middle decile. The advantage has boundary conditions: on a domain-curated pool no selection method separates from the others; at 1.5B scale the useful direction differs; and the lowest-reward training condition produces the strongest downstream generalization. Within the Qwen2.5-Math settings tested, RLVR data selection appears regime-dependent rather than reducible to a static ranking of problem quality.

</details>


### [177] [SIFTING: A Novel LLM-Based Framework for Structured and Transparent Information Extraction from Clinical Free-Text Reports, with Application to Tumor Staging in Lung Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07185)
*Mirco Hess,Gerben van Veenendaal,Joris Wakkie,Yiwen Soo,Malcolm H. Lawson,John D. Maclay,Arjun Nair,Neal Navani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SIFTING框架，一种基于LLM的临床自由文本信息提取方法，通过段落级处理和结构化提示与严格输出控制，将提取结果链接到源文本以实现可追溯性。在130份肺癌放射学报告的T分期提取任务中（使用自托管的4-bit量化Llama-3.3-70B模型），达到90%准确率，性能与最大规模SOTA推理LLM相当，且与临床专家可互换（p<0.001），同时提供完整的源文本可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在临床自由文本信息提取方面展现出潜力，但其输出往往非结构化且缺乏可追溯性，导致验证困难和临床工作流程采纳受限。现有方法难以在不牺牲准确性的前提下提供透明、可验证的提取结果。

Method: SIFTING框架结合了LLM的语言理解能力与段落级处理、结构化提示和严格输出控制，将发现的每条信息链接到源文本片段。在130份肺癌放射学报告中提取肿瘤T分期信息（称为SIFTING-T-stage任务），使用自托管的4-bit量化Llama-3.3-70B开源模型（35GB），性能与四位临床专家构建的参考标准进行比较，并采用bootstrap重采样估计置信区间。

Result: SIFTING-T-stage在参考标准上达到90%准确率（95%CI: 84-95）。其性能与最大规模的具有推理能力的SOTA LLM相当，与临床专家具有可互换性（p<0.001），同时通过源文本引用实现了完整可追溯性。

Conclusion: SIFTING实现了从临床自由文本中进行准确、结构化、可追溯的信息提取。它确保数据控制、可复现性和可验证的输出，能够支持临床验证和工作流整合。

Abstract: Background: Large language models (LLMs) show promise for extracting information from clinical free-text documents, but their outputs are often unstructured and lack traceability, complicating validation and adoption in clinical workflows. In this work we introduce SIFTING, an LLM-based framework designed to address these shortcomings.
  Methods: SIFTING combines the language comprehension capabilities of LLMs with segment-level processing and structured prompts with strict output control, linking findings to the source text to enable both accurate and transparent information extraction. To demonstrate its capabilities, we applied the framework to the task of extracting tumor T-stage information from 130 lung cancer radiology reports (SIFTING-T-stage). A compact 4-bit quantized version of the open-source LLM Llama-3.3-70B (35 GB) was used in a fully self-hosted setup, providing full control over data and model. Performance was evaluated against a reference standard created by four clinical experts and compared with a range of LLMs as used in a conventional single-prompt approach, using bootstrap resampling to estimate confidence intervals.
  Results: SIFTING-T-stage achieved an accuracy of 90% (95% CI: 84-95) against the reference standard. We found its performance to be comparable to even the largest state-of-the-art LLMs with reasoning capabilities and to be interchangeable with clinical experts (p < 0.001), while at the same time offering full traceability through source text references.
  Conclusion: SIFTING enables accurate, structured, and traceable information extraction from clinical free-text documents. It ensures data control, reproducibility, and verifiable outputs that can support clinical validation and workflow integration.

</details>


### [178] [CEDAR: Error-Bounded Residual Routing for Efficient Long-Context Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07237)
*Siyu Li,Dong Wang,Jie Zhou,Wei Li,Yang Xu,Sijie Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CEDAR是一种粗到细的动态注意力路由方法，通过为每个语义块提供廉价的键值摘要残差路径，并根据估计误差扩展至精确token注意力，在保持全局覆盖的同时实现约3倍加速，几乎完全恢复硬稀疏路由丢失的模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的事后稀疏注意力方法存在两个关键缺陷：硬选择（hard selection）对未选中的分块赋予零概率，导致路由失误不可恢复；且固定的扩展预算对简单和模糊的查询投入相同工作量，缺乏自适应分配机制。

Method: CEDAR采用粗到细（coarse-to-fine）的框架：每个语义块通过残差注意力路径贡献廉价的键-值摘要；估计近似误差高的分块被扩展为精确token注意力。精确和摘要贡献在单一softmax归一化中结合，使得细化替换而非重复粗证据。论文推导了由块内键/值离散度决定的输出误差界，并用其分配可变细化预算。

Result: 在受控聚类注意力研究中，残差摘要相对硬丢弃在相同精确块预算下将重建误差降低超过98%；在长上下文基准测试中，CEDAR恢复了硬稀疏路由丢失的大部分质量，同时在128K上下文中保持了约3倍的内核加速。

Conclusion: CEDAR证明了通过保留全局覆盖的残差摘要路径结合动态精细化预算分配，可以在不牺牲语言模型质量的前提下实现高效的长上下文预填充加速，是一项在效率和模型保真度之间达成更优平衡的解决方案。

Abstract: Post-hoc sparse attention accelerates long-context prefill by routing each query to a small set of token-level interactions. Hard selection, however, assigns zero probability to every omitted chunk: a routing miss cannot be recovered, and a fixed expansion budget spends the same work on easy and ambiguous queries. We introduce Coarse-to-fine Error-aware Dynamic Attention Routing (CEDAR), a coarse-to-fine method that keeps the language model frozen while preserving global coverage. Each semantic chunk contributes a cheap key--value summary to a residual attention path; chunks with high estimated approximation error are then expanded to exact token attention. Exact and summarized contributions are combined in a single softmax normalization, so refinement replaces, rather than duplicates, coarse evidence. We derive an output-error bound governed by within-chunk key/value dispersion and use it to allocate a variable refinement budget. A controlled clustered-attention study shows that residual summaries reduce reconstruction error by more than 98% relative to hard dropping at equal exact-chunk budgets. Experiments on long-context benchmarks demonstrate that CEDAR recovers most of the quality lost by hard sparse routing while maintaining approximately $3\times$ kernel speedup at 128K context.

</details>


### [179] [Separating Stream Stability from Long-Term Recall in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07282)
*Peipei Cao,Xin Zhang,Jie Tang,Xiao Li,Siying Li,Qing Pei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将流式语言模型的能力分解为三个不同的"时域"——稳定性时域、访问时域和效用时域——并引入ThreeH评估框架，在统一的状态和计算预算下同时测量这三个时域。通过注意力汇聚（attention sink）流式方法的实验表明，其稳定性时域可以是无限的，但访问和效用时域是有限的（受限于活动缓存）；而循环状态和检索机制则能扩展语义时域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式语言模型的讨论常常与长上下文和记忆系统混为一谈，但二者解决的问题不同。注意力汇聚虽然能稳定无限长的自回归生成，但模型无法使用已经离开近期token缓存的内容。作者认为这种区别应在系统声明和评估中明确，因此需要一种能区分稳定性、可访问性和任务效用的评估框架。

Method: 作者定义了三个时域：(1) 稳定性时域——预测行为保持良好；(2) 访问时域——过去内容仍能因果影响输出；(3) 效用时域——任务性能保持可接受。提出ThreeH评估契约，在共同的状态和计算预算下测量三个时域。构造性展示了稳定性时域可为无限而访问和效用时域为有限。应用于注意力汇聚流式方法，并在128K token流、延迟绑定回忆和延迟决策任务上进行实验。

Result: 实验表明注意力汇聚能保持本地建模能力，但无法访问超出活动缓存的内容；循环状态和检索状态能够扩展语义时域（访问和效用时域）。框架还揭示了缓存策略、循环状态、检索和外部内存在流式系统中各自扮演的角色。

Conclusion: 稳定性、访问性和效用性是流式语言模型中应当被分开测量的不同能力维度。注意力汇聚的价值在于提供恒定内存下的稳定流式生成，而不应被视为语义记忆机制；真正的长程语义能力需要依赖循环状态、检索或外部内存等机制。

Abstract: Methods for streaming language models are often discussed alongside long-context and memory systems, although they solve different problems. An attention sink can stabilize autoregressive generation over an indefinitely long stream while the model remains unable to use content that has left its recent-token cache. We argue that this distinction should be explicit in system claims and evaluation. We introduce three horizons: the stability horizon, over which predictive behavior remains well behaved; the access horizon, over which past content can still causally affect the output; and the utility horizon, over which a task retains acceptable performance. We show constructively that the stability horizon can be infinite while the access and utility horizons are finite. We then propose ThreeH, an evaluation contract that measures all three horizons under a common state and compute budget. Applying the framework to attention-sink streaming clarifies its strength, constant-memory, stable generation, without treating anchor tokens as semantic memory. The framework exposes roles for cache policies, recurrent state, retrieval, and external memory. Experiments on 128K-token streams, delayed binding recall, and delayed decisions show that attention sinks preserve local modeling but not content beyond the active cache; recurrent and retrieval state extend the semantic horizon.

</details>


### [180] [Probing the Structure and Dynamics of LLM Value Expression through Value Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07296)
*Kaicheng Zhang,Jingyi Xiao,Renjun Hu,Xiaoling Liu,Yunshi Lan,Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出冲突驱动的价值探测（Conflict-driven Value Probing）框架，将LLM的价值表达理解为结构化但动态的现象，而非静态单一值。通过对十个LLM施加四类价值冲突干预，识别出三个模式：表达二元性、功能可操控性和有界可塑性，揭示了价值表达在上下文中的可重构性及其行为边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有LLM伦理评估通常将模型价值视为静态和单一的，忽视了价值表达随上下文动态变化的特性。作者认为需要将LLM价值表达理解为结构化但动态的现象，以更准确地刻画其价值系统。

Method: 提出冲突驱动价值探测框架：将LLM置于价值冲突情境中，实施四类干预（对冲突进行扰动）来探测价值表达，并应用于十个不同的LLM进行系统性评估。

Result: 识别出三个反复出现的模式：(1)表达二元性——模型从抽象评估的广泛理想主义倾向转向具体冲突中的务实优先；(2)功能可操控性——模型能轻易地将其价值表达轮廓重构至任务定义的价值目标；(3)有界可塑性——这种重构存在约束，压力诱导安全与目标导向的优先级转变，负面框架则能区分受保护价值与更易被重定向的价值。

Conclusion: LLM价值表达既具有结构也具动态性：上下文能灵活地重构其表达的优先级，但这种重构受行为边界约束。该行为学解释为理解LLM的可控性、对齐和安全性提供了基础。

Abstract: Ethical evaluation of Large Language Models (LLMs) often characterizes model values as static and monolithic. In contrast, we argue that LLM value expression is better understood as a structured yet dynamic phenomenon. To investigate this, we introduce Conflict-driven Value Probing, a controlled framework that places LLMs in value conflicts and implements four types of interventions that perturb these conflicts to probe LLM value expression. Applying this framework to ten LLMs, we identify three recurring patterns. (1) Expression duality: models shift from broad idealistic orientations in abstract assessment toward more pragmatic priorities in concrete conflicts. (2) Functional steerability: models readily reconfigure their expressed value profiles toward task-defined value objectives. (3) Bounded plasticity: such reconfiguration is not without constraints, i.e. pressure induces a security- and goal-oriented priority shift while negative framing distinguishes protected values from those more amenable to redirection. Together, these findings characterize both the structure and dynamics of LLM value expression: context flexibly reconfigures expressed priorities, yet within behavioral boundaries. This behavioral account provides a foundation for understanding controllability, alignment, and safety in LLMs. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ZeroGen-Lab/CFProbe.

</details>


### [181] [Marginal Fidelity Does Not Establish User Simulation in Demographic Synthetic Survey Panels: Response Contracts, Support Collapse and Conditioning Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07305)
*Alexander Doudkin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文质疑"人口统计合成调查面板"以聚合答案匹配公开调查作为验证标准的有效性。研究发现：(1) 响应合约（committed sets vs 每选项概率启发）主导测量保真度，概率启发能显著降低选项边际MAE；(2) 发表边际一致性无法区分"个体响应者模拟"与"直接总体估计"，无个体模型的总体流行度查询在多数对比中胜出。作者认为聚合边际一致性能证明的是启发合约与待估量（无需模拟个体即可获得），而非个体模拟的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成调查面板通常以匹配公开调查的聚合答案来验证其真实性，但作者质疑这种验证方式所建立的证书究竟证明了什么，即聚合边际一致性是否真正证明了合成的个体层面模拟质量。

Method: 研究跨越3个国家4个调查组织的6个多选电池组，将分析限制在3个合成队列与人类目标共享相同总体框的仪器上（另3个作为敏感性分析）。比较两套响应合约（committed sets与每选项概率启发），并通过约束感知概率向量与无个体人口流行度查询，对比已发表边际一致性对响应者模拟和直接总体估计的区分能力。

Result: (1) committed sets在128个模型-电池选项中留下66个空格，概率启发为0个；概率将8个无上限模型-仪器比较中的选项边际MAE降低4.53至7.30点。(2) 无个体人口流行度查询平均MAE 6.27，优于committed panel的12.39并赢得全部9项对比；约束感知概率向量平均5.34且9项中胜4项。(3) 在3个未公开的人口学单元中，两种方法均无法胜过直接引用国家分布。

Conclusion: 发表的总体边际一致性是有关启发合约和无需模拟响应者即可获取的待估量的证据，而非个体模拟质量的证据。该验证标准可能无法区分真实的个体级合成模拟与简单的总体估计。

Abstract: Demographic synthetic survey panels are often validated by matching aggregate answers to published surveys. We test what that certificate establishes across six multiselect batteries from four survey organisations in three countries. The headline analysis is restricted to three instruments whose synthetic cohort and human target share the stated population frame; three other batteries remain sensitivity analyses.
  The response contract dominates measured fidelity. In the aligned instruments, committed sets leave 66 of 128 model-battery option slots empty in panels of up to 500 respondents, versus 0 of 128 under per-option probability elicitation. Across eight uncapped model-instrument comparisons, probabilities reduce option-marginal MAE by 4.53 to 7.30 points. The capped instrument reverses on two models until the vectors are projected onto its stated maximum. These are measurement effects: human targets are realised check-all responses, whereas the vectors are latent inclusion propensities.
  Published marginal agreement also fails to discriminate respondent simulation from direct population estimation. On nine aligned model-battery pairs, a no-persona population-prevalence query averages 6.27 MAE versus 12.39 for committed panels and wins all nine comparisons. Constraint-aware probability vectors average 5.34 and beat the query on four of nine, so the baseline challenges the validation criterion rather than proving direct estimation uniformly best. On three unpublished demographic cells, neither approach beats reciting the national distribution. Population-marginal agreement is therefore evidence about an elicitation contract and an estimand obtainable without simulated respondents, not evidence of individual simulation.

</details>


### [182] [RouteRelay: Event-Triggered Cross-Layer Route Reuse for Efficient Dynamic Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07306)
*Bin Li,Sisi Liu,Chenyang Hu,Chaoyang Zhang,Wei Li,Hui Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RouteRelay是一种与路由算法无关的方法，通过跨层复用路由元数据而非每层重建完整的chunk-chunk分数矩阵，在保持高路由召回率的同时显著减少长上下文稀疏注意力中路由器的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态稀疏注意力通过将每个查询块路由到少量键块来降低长上下文预填充成本，但路由器每层都重建chunk-chunk分数矩阵，即使选中的路由在跨层间变化很小，造成了大量冗余计算。

Method: 锚点层执行完整路由；中间层仅对上一层的top-k路由和由近失块与随机探测块组成的紧凑哨兵集进行重新评分。仅当哨兵挑战了查询行最弱的选中块时才执行重新路由。论文给出了top-k稳定性条件、错过挑战者的概率界以及按行选择性GPU执行设计。

Result: 在低、中、高跨层漂移下分别仅重新路由25.0%、55.4%和78.2%的行，同时保持至少99.99%的路由召回率；当键块数从128增至1024时，保持100%召回率的同时仅评估完整路由分数对的38.4%-51.6%。但CPU未融合执行仍慢于稠密矩阵乘法，主要瓶颈为行压缩与账本更新。

Conclusion: RouteRelay通过元数据复用显著减少路由计算量并保持高召回率，但实际加速受限于GPU内核工程中的行压缩和账本更新开销，这是后续优化的关键方向。

Abstract: Dynamic sparse attention reduces long-context prefill cost by routing each query chunk to a small set of key chunks at every Transformer layer. The sparse attention kernel avoids most token interactions, but the router still rebuilds a chunk--chunk score matrix layer after layer, even when the selected routes change little. We introduce RouteRelay, a router-agnostic method that reuses only route metadata across depth while continuing to compute attention with the current layer's queries, keys, and values. Anchor layers perform full routing. Intermediate layers rescore the previous top-$k$ route and a compact sentinel set of near-miss and randomly probed chunks. A query row is rerouted only when a sentinel challenges its weakest selected chunk. We give a top-$k$ stability condition, a probabilistic bound on missed challengers, and a row-selective GPU execution design. In a reproducible empirical evaluation, RouteRelay retains at least 99.99% route recall while rerouting 25.0%, 55.4%, and 78.2% of rows under low, moderate, and high cross-layer drift, respectively. Across routing scales, RouteRelay retains 100.0% recall while evaluating 38.4--51.6% of full-routing score pairs as the key-chunk count grows from 128 to 1024. Its unfused CPU execution remains slower than dense matrix multiplication, exposing row compaction and ledger updates as the main kernel-engineering targets.

</details>


### [183] [SPARROW: Scalable Taxonomy Induction via Structure-Preserving Partitioning and Constraint-Guided Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07307)
*Yirui Zhang,Yixuan Tang,Yandong Sun,Mong-Li Lee,Anthony Kum Hoe Tung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SPARROW框架，解决大规模LLM分类学归纳中divide-and-merge范式的两个结构失效问题（结构碎片化与父节点错位），通过保持结构的谱分割和约束引导的增量融合实现可扩展且高质量的分类学构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的分类学归纳方法在概念集规模扩大时性能急剧下降，作者认为这种退化不仅源于上下文长度限制，更源于层次推理中的结构性失败。divide-and-merge范式虽可解决规模问题，但存在两种结构性失效模式（结构碎片化与父节点错位）导致全局层次质量受损，亟需针对性解决。

Method: 提出SPARROW框架，包含两个关键组件：(1) 保持结构的谱分割（structure-preserving spectral partitioning），在将概念划分为子集时保留块内的层次连接信号；(2) 约束引导的增量融合（constraint-guided incremental fusion），将块级关系视为结构约束而非全局放置的ground truth，逐步把局部分类学合并为全局层次结构。

Result: 在大型基准测试上的实验表明，SPARROW在不同主干模型（backbones）上一致地取得了最强的全局结构质量。

Conclusion: SPARROW通过针对性地解决divide-and-merge范式中的结构碎片化与父节点错位问题，实现了大规模场景下高质量的分类学归纳，代码已开源。

Abstract: Taxonomy induction aims to organize concept sets into coherent hierarchical structures. Recent LLM-based methods can induce taxonomies directly from flat term lists, avoiding the need for corpora, but degrade sharply as concept sets scale up. We argue that this degradation stems not only from context length limitations, but also from structural failures in hierarchical reasoning. To address this, we adopt a divide-and-merge paradigm that partitions concepts into smaller subsets, induces local taxonomies, and merges them into a global hierarchy. However, we identify two structural failure modes inherent to this paradigm: Structural Fragmentation, where partitioning weakens local hierarchical signals, and Parent Displacement, where locally plausible relations are misplaced in the global hierarchy. To address both, we propose SPARROW, a scalable taxonomy induction framework that combines structure-preserving spectral partitioning to retain hierarchical connectivity within each block, and constraint-guided incremental fusion that treats block-level relations as structural constraints rather than ground truth for global placement. Experiments on large-scale benchmarks show that SPARROW consistently achieves the strongest global structural quality across backbones. The code is available at https://github.com/rebeccazyr/SPARROW.

</details>


### [184] [LANTERN: Language Model Assessment on Noisy and Transformed Tasks for Understanding Error and Robustness Nuances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07309)
*Vamsi Krishna Kodavali,Rituraj Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统地评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）在多种输入扰动下的鲁棒性，包括词错误率、字符重复与复制、选项修改以及指令跟随的变异性。通过构建包含多选题和指令跟随任务的合成与增强数据集，在不同规模（小、中、大）以及基础版和指令微调版LLM上进行实验，量化了模型在扰动条件下的响应变异性，并突显了与基线模型的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的鲁棒性评估仍是关键挑战，特别是在评估其对输入数据扰动的敏感性方面，缺乏系统化的多维度评估框架。

Method: 构建合成与增强数据集，涵盖多选题（MCQ）和指令跟随任务；在多个维度（词错误率、字符重复与复制、选项修改、指令跟随变异性）上进行系统性的扰动评估；对多种规模（小、中、大）和不同类型（基础版与指令微调版）的LLM进行广泛实验。

Result: 量化了LLM在扰动条件下响应的变异性，并揭示了与基线模型相比的显著差异，验证了不同评估场景下模型稳定性的差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果为理解LLM在不同评估场景下的稳定性提供了洞见，有助于开发更鲁棒可靠的语言模型及更稳健的评估方法论。

Abstract: Robustness evaluation of large language models (LLMs) remains a critical challenge, particularly in assessing their sensitivity to perturbations in input data. In this work, we systematically evaluate LLM robustness across multiple dimensions, including word error rate, character repetition and duplication, modifications in choices, and variability in instruction following. To facilitate this evaluation, we construct a synthetic and augmented dataset encompassing a diverse set of LLM benchmarks, specifically targeting multiple-choice question (MCQ) datasets and instruction-following tasks. We conduct extensive experiments on LLMs of varying scales-small, medium, and large-as well as across base and instruction-tuned variants. Our analysis quantifies the variability in model responses under perturbed conditions and highlights discrepancies relative to baseline models. The findings provide insights into the stability of LLMs across different evaluation scenarios contributing to the development of more robust and reliable language models as well as robust evaluation methodologies.

</details>


### [185] [Content-Based Addressing for Long Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07314)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的位置编码方案：将token流划分为单元（units），单元内部保留标准RoPE位置编码，而每个单元的整体地址由其内容（而非计数位置）计算得出。这种方法避免了长上下文导致的RoPE相对旋转位置偏移超出训练分布的问题，证明可在单元插入或重排时保持固定token对的注意力比较不变。字符级Tiny Shakespeare诊断中，在256字符上下文上训练的模型在4096字符处困惑度仍保持3.82，而连续RoPE则恶化至12.09。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE基于整型位置生成旋转角度，在局部token顺序上有效，但随着上下文长度增长会产生位置训练-测试失配：推理时出现的相对旋转偏移在训练中未见。现有方法（重缩放、插值、随机化、偏置）仍依赖不断增长的位置计数器，无法从根本上解决位置信息的扩展问题。

Method: 将token序列划分为多个单元（units），单元内部使用标准RoPE位置；每个完成的单元被赋予一个由内容计算得到的地址（content-based address）。新增单元时只是将同一学习映射应用于新内容，而非扩展位置范围或标识符表。作者证明了该结构：1) 精确保留局部RoPE；2) 插入或重排其他单元时固定token对的注意力比较不变；3) 仅因单元数量增加不会产生新的相对旋转。

Result: 字符级Tiny Shakespeare实验中，在256字符上下文训练的模型，验证困惑度在256字符时为4.04，扩展到4096字符时仅为3.82（甚至更好）；而连续RoPE从4.71恶化到12.09。第二个诊断实验表明基于内容的寻址能从多个序列化事实中检索和使用信息。

Conclusion: 受控浅层实验支持一个直接的处方：位置编码用于局部寻址，内容编码用于跨单元寻址。该工作虽非大规模基准测试，但为长上下文建模提供了新的架构方向。

Abstract: Rotary position embedding (RoPE) uses each token's integer position to determine the rotation applied inside attention. This works well for local token order, but increasing context length creates a positional train-test mismatch: RoPE produces relative rotations at offsets not seen during training. Methods that rescale, interpolate, randomize, or bias positions specify how attention handles those offsets, but still derive positional information from a growing token counter. We instead divide a token stream into units, retain ordinary RoPE positions within each unit, and assign every completed unit an address computed from its content. Adding units then applies the same learned map to new content rather than extending a positional range or an identifier table. We prove that this construction preserves local RoPE exactly, leaves the attention comparison between two fixed tokens unchanged when other units are inserted or reordered, and does not create new relative rotations merely because more units are added. In a character-level Tiny Shakespeare diagnostic, a model trained on 256-character contexts has validation perplexity 4.04 at 256 characters and 3.82 at 4096, while continuous RoPE changes from 4.71 to 12.09. A second diagnostic shows that content-based addressing can retrieve and use information from multiple serialized facts. These are controlled shallow experiments, not scale benchmarks, but they support a direct prescription: use position to address locally and content to address across units.

</details>


### [186] [BlueprintAgent: Constraint-Triggered Targeted Revisits for Simulation-Ready Generation from Scanned Structural Blueprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07362)
*Zhouyuan Xu,Chen Yang,Linhao Wang,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BlueprintAgent（BPA），一个约束触发的多模态智能体，用于从扫描的钢筋混凝土建筑蓝图（施工图）中自动提取可仿真（simulation-ready）的框架模型。BPA将多模态大语言模型作为主要阅读器和决策者，工程约束被实现为可调用的验证器，当实体级冲突报告触发时，MLLM会对局部区域进行针对性复核。在300张真实扫描蓝图（来自20个匿名RC框架项目）上的实验表明，BPA的Beam F1达到0.994，远超单MLLM零样本的0.301和固定流水线的0.820。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将在役钢筋混凝土建筑蓝图转换为仿真就绪模型（支持确定性FEM导出和合格工程师审查的结构化框架表示）是安全评估和抗震加固的基础，但该过程目前仍需人工完成。直接使用多模态大语言模型读取扫描图纸并不可靠，输出经常违反梁-柱支承、跨数或3D连续性等工程约束。因此需要一种能确保工程约束满足的自动化提取方法。

Method: BPA将MLLM作为主要阅读与决策者，OCR和计算机视觉提供局部证据。核心机制是将工程约束实现为可调用的验证器（validators），这些验证器产生实体级冲突报告，触发MLLM对局部区域进行有目标的重新审视（targeted revisit）。这是一种推理时控制方法，区别于固定流水线和自由形式的自我反思。具体地，验证器检测到的冲突（如梁-柱支承违背、跨数错误、3D连续性断裂）会提示MLLM仅对冲突所在局部区域重新推理，而非全图重新处理。

Result: 在300张来自20个匿名RC框架项目的真实扫描蓝图上，BPA与五个基线和六个消融进行对比。BPA的宏平均Beam F1达到0.994，而单MLLM零样本仅为0.301，固定流水线为0.820。消融实验显示，移除MLLM主导的轴线判定（axis adjudication）后，复杂多图纸项目的Beam和Column F1显著下降。结果还表明，对于密集技术图纸，工程约束最适合作为实体级针对性复核的触发器，而非作为事后输出过滤器。

Conclusion: BPA验证了约束触发的多模态智能体架构在工程图纸结构化提取中的有效性：将工程约束作为推理时控制来触发局部MLLM复核，远比零样本提示或固定流水线可靠。该方法为从扫描蓝图到仿真就绪模型的全自动化转换提供了可行路径，支持安全评估和抗震加固等下游应用。

Abstract: Converting in-service reinforced-concrete (RC) building blueprints into simulation-ready models---structured frame representations that support deterministic FEM export and qualified-engineer review---underpins safety assessment and seismic retrofit, but the process remains manual. Direct prompting of a multimodal large language model (MLLM) over a scanned sheet is unreliable: outputs often violate engineering constraints on beam--column support, span count, or 3D continuity. We present BlueprintAgent (BPA), a constraint-triggered multimodal agent for simulation-ready frame extraction from scanned blueprints. BPA treats the MLLM as the primary reader and decision maker, with OCR and computer vision supplying localized evidence. Its central mechanism realizes engineering constraints as callable validators whose entity-level conflict reports trigger targeted MLLM revisits over the local region---an inference-time control distinct from fixed pipelines and free-form self-reflection. We evaluate BPA on 300 real scanned blueprint sheets from 20 anonymized RC frame projects, against five baselines and six ablations. BPA reaches a macro-averaged Beam F1 of 0.994, against 0.301 for single-MLLM zero-shot and 0.820 for a fixed pipeline; removing MLLM-led axis adjudication collapses Beam and Column F1 on complex multi-sheet projects. For dense technical drawings, engineering constraints are best deployed as triggers for entity-level targeted revisits rather than as post-hoc output filters.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond Fluent Generation: A CPU Reliability Benchmark for MCP-Style Tool Calling in Sub-2B Small Language Models for Edge Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07370)
*Abrar Shahriar Qurat-Ul-Ain Mastoi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了5个低于20亿参数的开源小语言模型（Phi-1.5、Pythia-1.4B、TinyLlama-1.1B-Chat、Qwen2.5-0.5B、Qwen2.5-1.5B）在单板计算机上的MCP风格工具调用能力，基于100个提示词进行评测。结果显示Qwen2.5-1.5B表现最佳（贪心解码75%、采样79%），而Phi-1.5得分为0%，且仅5/1000的原始响应可直接解析为JSON，几乎完全依赖恢复解析器。CPU资源探测显示Qwen2.5-1.5B需要7,960 MiB内存和30.782秒平均延迟，揭示了边缘部署中可靠性与资源消耗之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限的单板计算机（如Raspberry Pi、Jetson Nano等）推动了对设备端小语言模型代理的需求，以降低云依赖、改善数据本地性并容忍间歇性连接。然而，MCP风格的工具调用不仅需要流畅生成文本，还要求模型输出机器可读的JSON、正确选择工具、提供所有必需参数并避免意外操作，这在资源受限的SLM上是一个重大挑战。研究者希望通过系统评估小模型在此任务上的能力边界，为边缘部署提供依据。

Method: 作者选取五个低于20亿参数的开源模型，在100个覆盖天气查询、网页搜索、计算、邮件撰写和任务创建等场景的提示词上，使用贪心解码和核采样两种策略进行评测。采用恢复解析器去除Markdown代码围栏、提取花括号分隔子串，并评分JSON可解析性、工具名正确性、参数完整性和值一致性。同时进行CPU资源探测以测量内存占用和延迟，并进行严格的post-hoc审计。

Result: Qwen2.5-1.5B在贪心解码下达到75%、采样下79%的正确率；Qwen2.5-0.5B贪心下72%但采样骤降至32%；Phi-1.5得0%，Pythia和TinyLlama最高仅7%。严格的逐项审计显示1000条原始响应中仅5条可直接解析为JSON，几乎完全依赖输出恢复机制。资源探测表明Qwen2.5-1.5B需7,960 MiB内存和30.782秒平均延迟，Qwen2.5-0.5B需3,637 MiB和10.627秒。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了SLM在边缘工具调用场景中存在显著的可靠性与资源消耗的权衡：较小模型资源占用低但采样策略下可靠性急剧下降，较大模型可靠性较好但资源需求接近边缘设备的实际承载上限。当前大多数模型严重依赖输出恢复解析器而非原生结构化输出，安全部署需要结合schema验证、受约束生成、最小权限执行以及对关键动作的人工升级机制。作者同时明确指出研究未直接覆盖所列单板硬件或完整的MCP实现。

Abstract: Resource constrained single-board computers including Raspberry Pi, NVIDIA Jetson Nano, Arduino UNO Q, Orange Pi, and LattePanda motivate on-device small language model (SLM) agents that reduce cloud dependence, improve data locality, and tolerate intermittent connectivity. Model Context Protocol (MCP)-style tool invocation demands more than fluent generation: an agent must emit machine-readable JSON, select the correct tool, supply all required arguments, and avoid unintended actions. We establish a platform-agnostic CPU baseline by evaluating five open-weight models below two billion parameters Phi-1.5, Pythia-1.4B, TinyLlama-1.1B-Chat, Qwen2.5-0.5B, and Qwen2.5-1.5B on 100 prompts spanning weather retrieval, web search, calculation, email composition, and task creation, under greedy decoding and nucleus sampling. A recovery parser strips Markdown fences, extracts brace-delimited substrings, and scores parseability, tool-name correctness, argument completeness, and value agreement. Under this criterion, Qwen2.5-1.5B achieves 75% (greedy) and 79% (sampling); Qwen2.5-0.5B achieves 72% (greedy) but drops to 32% under sampling. Phi-1.5 scores 0%; Pythia and TinyLlama reach at most 7%. A strict post-hoc audit finds only 5 of 1,000 raw responses directly parseable as JSON, exposing near-total dependence on output recovery. A CPU resource probe shows Qwen2.5-1.5B requires 7,960 MiB and 30.782 s mean latency; Qwen2.5-0.5B uses 3,637 MiB and 10.627 s, revealing a reliability-resource trade-off for edge deployment. These results do not cover the named boards directly or a full MCP implementation. Safe deployment requires schema validation, constrained generation, least-privilege execution, and human escalation for consequential actions.

</details>


### [188] [Beyond Single-Negative Preference: Multi-Negative DPO for LLM-Centric Historical Entity Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07379)
*Tien Nam Nguyen,Emanuela Boros,Ahmed Hamdi,Adam Jatowt,Mickaël Coustaty,Antoine Doucet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MDPO（多负样本直接偏好优化），通过在同一训练实例中利用所有检索到的负候选与正确实体进行成对比较，改进了LLM在历史实体链接任务中的偏好优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM历史实体链接中的偏好优化通常每个训练实例只使用一个负候选，丢弃了其余检索到的候选信息，导致优化不充分，无法充分利用候选集。

Method: 提出多负样本直接偏好优化（MDPO），一种基于参考模型的成对目标函数，将正确实体与每个提及关联的所有有效拒绝候选进行比较。MDPO保留DPO的Bradley-Terry公式，同时通过掩码、长度归一化的序列分数充分利用完整候选集。

Result: 在hipe-2020和newseye数据集（涵盖法语、德语、英语、瑞典语和芬兰语历史报纸文本）上评估，MDPO相比监督微调和单负样本DPO均有提升，尤其在NIL提及、语义歧义、OCR噪声和历史困难名称上表现突出。分析显示候选检索仍是端到端实体链接的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 在训练实例中纳入所有负候选是一种简单有效的改进方法，可显著提升基于LLM的历史实体链接性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently shown promise for historical entity linking, but preference optimization for this task is often formulated with only one negative candidate per training instance. This discards information from the remaining candidates retrieved for the same mention. We introduce multi-negative direct preference optimisation (MDPO), a reference-based pairwise objective that compares the correct entity with all valid rejected candidates associated with each mention. MDPO preserves the Bradley-Terry formulation of DPO while exploiting the complete candidate set through masked, length-normalised sequence scores. We evaluate MDPO on hipe-2020 and newseye, covering French, German, English, Swedish, and Finnish historical newspaper text. Experiments show that MDPO improves over supervised fine-tuning and single-negative DPO, with particularly strong gains for NIL mentions, semantic ambiguity, OCR noise, and historically difficult names. Further analyses disentangle candidate-generation and selection errors, showing that candidate retrieval remains a key bottleneck for end-to-end entity linking. These results demonstrate that incorporating all within-instance negative candidates is a simple and effective improvement for LLM-based historical entity linking.

</details>


### [189] [An LLM-Associated Register Shift in Korean Journal Abstracts: A Morphology-Aware Excess-Vocabulary Study, 2018-2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07447)
*Aron Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将"超标词汇量"（excess vocabulary）方法从英文文献适配到韩文学术摘要，分析2023年后KCI与越南语摘要中LLM处理痕迹。在398,296篇KCI摘要和47,165篇越南语摘要上，研究发现韩文摘要2023年无异常、2024年末开始出现、2025年上升并在2026年中趋于平稳。代表性标记词"sisahada"（建议）在2026年摘要中占比21.4%（期望5.3%），而普通动词降至趋势的四分之一。基于单字统计的LLM处理下限估计为2024-2026年3.5%、10.5%、16.1%，拆分半集方法给出7.8%、20.6%、33.0%。学科控制和翻译路线替代解释均不能完全消除该信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有研究使用超标词汇量衡量2022年后学术英语因LLM使用而产生的变化，但尚不清楚这一信号在非英语语言中如何体现。本文将其适配到韩文，以探究LLM对非英语学术写作的影响范围和特征。

Method: 将英文的excess vocabulary方法适配到韩文，采用形态学单元（morphological units）进行处理，在398,296篇KCI韩文摘要（2018-2026年8月）和47,165篇越南语摘要上进行分析。通过安慰剂检验设立基准下限（单字统计0.1-2.2点，拆分半集统计最高2.9点）。引入三项模型标注的lemma风格集、同期英文摘要对照、期刊配对控制、三供应商对照摘要（含翻译路线测试）等稳健性检验，并使用Holzwarth等人的估计器交叉验证。

Result: 韩文摘要中LLM痕迹2023年不显著，2024年末开始出现，2025年上升并在2026年中趋平。2026年标记词"sisahada"出现率21.4%（期望5.3%），普通动词降至趋势的四分之一。单字条件下限估计LLM处理率为2024-2026年3.5%、10.5%、16.1%；拆分半集法为7.8%、20.6%、33.0%；Holzwarth估计器给出2025-2026年41.9%和72.1%。学科控制和配对控制仅部分削弱信号（学科控制后保留33.0点中的14.7点；期刊配对后保留34.1点）。翻译路线不能解释该现象，同文英文摘要中信号早一年出现。

Conclusion: LLM对韩文学术写作的影响在2024年末开始显现并于2025-2026年持续扩大，该信号无法由学科构成变化、翻译路线或期刊效应解释，属于真正的语言风格转变。但估计的LLM处理率对方法假设高度敏感，需要谨慎解读绝对数值。

Abstract: Excess vocabulary, a word's frequency above its pre-2023 trend, is how the change in scholarly English after 2022 has been measured. We adapt it to Korean with morphological units on 398,296 KCI abstracts (2018-August 2026), with 47,165 Vietnamese abstracts for comparison. Placebo floors are 0.1-2.2 points for the single-word statistic and at most 2.9 for the re-selected split-half set statistic. Korean abstracts show nothing in 2023, onset in late 2024, a rise through 2025 flattening in mid-2026: sisahada "suggest" appears in 21.4% of 2026 abstracts against 5.3% expected; plain verbs like araboda "look into" fall to a quarter of trend. Under stated assumptions the single-word conditional lower bound on LLM-processed abstracts is 3.5%, 10.5% and 16.1% for 2024-2026 and a split-half set bound 7.8%, 20.6% and 33.0%. Holzwarth et al.'s estimator under the same discipline gives 41.9% and 72.1% for 2025-2026. Subject-matter controls reduce but do not remove it: restricting the set to lemmas three language-model annotators all call style leaves 14.7 of the 33.0 points, and pairing each 2026 abstract with its journal's closest base-period abstract leaves 34.1. Tested translation routes do not explain it: the surface marks of translated Korean fall as the markers rise. In the same articles' English abstracts the excess appears a year earlier; where the English side carries none, the Korean shift persists at 30 to 66% of the rate where it does. Control abstracts from three providers reproduce the rising words, with marker turnover consistent with model generations; implied prevalences are scenario-dependent.

</details>


### [190] [FramingQA: Does the Question Shape the Answer? Measuring the Compositional Framing Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07448)
*Hazel H. Kim,Andrew M. Bean,Guilherme Affonso Ferreira de Camargo,Shanyu Chauhan,Felix Drinkall,Jade Kosché,Chenyang Ma,Glory Nwaugbala,Nabeel Seedat,Bradley Max Segal,Samuel Recht,Hinrich Schütze,Philip H. S. Torr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍FramingQA基准，用于衡量大语言模型在不同问题框架（question framing）下的敏感度，覆盖法律、医学、金融和机器人模拟四个高风险领域，通过三层嵌套的框架偏差注入设计（根级、命题级、全局级）系统评测了九个开源模型（3.8B-70B）在四个模型家族上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型对与用户隐含立场一致的细微措辞变动（即问题框架）非常敏感，可能导致用户获得被问题表述方式污染而非基于事实本身的建议。在高风险领域（法律、医疗、金融等），这种偏差的代价极为高昂。在现实场景中，无论是专家还是非专家用户都频繁向LLM提出包含不完整或误导性假设的问题，使得模型极易受这些框架的影响，因此需要系统化地量化和评估这一问题。

Method: 作者设计了FramingQA基准，将框架偏差注入分为三个嵌套层级：（1）根级（root）：直接使用带有框架偏差的问题措辞；（2）命题级（propositional）：在中性问题前注入带框架偏差的前提；（3）全局级（global）：前提与带框架偏差的问题配对。研究在四个领域（法律、医学、金融、机器人模拟）中对九个开源模型（参数量3.8B到70B）跨四个模型家族进行评估。

Result: 研究发现，模型在单一方差（per-variant）上的准确率高并不能保证在固定事实信息下对不同措辞问题的鲁棒性，即模型对框架偏差的敏感度与整体准确率并非必然正相关。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了大语言模型在高风险领域中对问题框架敏感的系统性问题，表明仅追求高准确率不足以保证模型在现实场景中的可靠性和安全性，需要进一步关注模型对不同表述方式的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We introduce FramingQA, a benchmark that measures the model sensitivity to question framing across law, medicine, finance, and robotic simulations. Large language models (LLMs) often change their responses to subtle rephrasings that align with an implied stance by users. This can leave users with advice tainted by how they happened to phrase a question rather than by the underlying facts, and the consequences are highly costly in high-stakes domains. Because in the realistic scenarios, both expert practitioners and non-expert users frequently ask LLMs questions containing incomplete or misleading assumptions, models are highly susceptible to those framings. To test this, we inject the framing bias across three nested levels: a framing-biased question phrasing (root), an injected framing-biased premise prepended to a neutral question (propositional), and a premise paired with a framing-biased question (global). Evaluating nine open models (3.8B-70B) across four families, we find that strong per-variant accuracy does not guarantee the robustness across differently phrased questions under the fixed factual information.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Beyond Right and Wrong: Evaluating Second-order Social Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05437)
*Sunny Rai,Jinyi Kuang,Reyhan Jamalova,Annie Lou,Cristina Bicchieri,Niyati Malhotra,Victor Hugo Orozco-Olvera,Ana Maria Munoz-Boudet,Lyle H Ungar,Sharath C Guntuku*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估大语言模型元规范（metanorm）推理能力的新框架，引入NormReact数据集（450个规范违反场景，涵盖情感评估和行为响应两个维度），发现当前LLM过度预测负面制裁、低估现实中人类在规范执行中的宽容和克制，且随社会距离增加，模型与人类判断的一致性显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往AI对齐工作主要关注一阶社会规范（教模型什么行为可接受/不可接受），但忽略了社会智能的另一关键维度——元规范（metanorm），即预见谁将执行规范以及如何执行（如公开羞辱或监禁）。这种二阶期望对于模型在冲突调解、政策模拟等规范敏感领域的可信应用至关重要，但目前缺乏系统评估框架。

Method: 作者引入一个元规范推理评估框架，沿两个维度展开：情感评估（emotional appraisal）和行为响应（behavioral response），并设计两类新任务：预测违反者的自我调节和观察者的他人调节。同时构建NormReact多视角数据集，包含450个规范违反场景，手工标注了违反者性别和观察者社会亲近度维度下的情感与行为响应，并对六种LLM进行评估。

Result: 在六个主流LLM中，模型普遍过度预测负面制裁——在人类预期不干预的场景中模型仍预测惩罚性反应；同时随着观察者与违反者社会距离的增大，模型与人类判断的一致性持续恶化，表明当前LLM描绘了一个比现实更严苛的社会世界。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统在从冲突调解到政策模拟等规范敏感领域可能产生扭曲的"社会调节图景"：过度呈现惩罚、忽视现实中规范执行所特有的宽容、克制与关系校准。这提示未来对齐工作需要将元规范推理能力纳入评估范畴。

Abstract: Previous AI alignment efforts have focused primarily on first-order social norms -- teaching models what is socially acceptable or unacceptable (e.g., `do not steal'). However, social intelligence depends not only on norm recognition, but also on anticipating who will enforce it and how (e.g., public shame or even imprisonment). These second-order expectations, known as metanorms, govern how people respond when social rules are broken. We introduce a novel framework for evaluating metanorm reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs) along two dimensions: emotional appraisal and behavioral response, and propose new classification tasks, namely, predicting self-regulation in violators, and other-regulation in observers. We release a multi-perspective dataset, NormReact, of 450 norm violation scenarios, hand-annotated for emotions and behavioral responses across norm violators' gender and observers' social closeness. Current LLMs portray a harsher social world: across six models, they overpredict negative sanctions where humans would expect inaction, and alignment with human judgments deteriorates as social distance increases. These findings suggest that AI systems in norm-sensitive domains from conflict mediation to policy simulation, may risk producing a distorted picture of social regulation: one that over-represents punishment and under-represents the tolerance, restraint, and relational calibration that characterize actual norm enforcement in real world.

</details>


### [192] [CriticGen: Generation-Aware Evaluation as Actionable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05439)
*Huifang Du,Zecheng Zuo,Sen Wang,Chenghao Fan,Haofen Wang,Yehui Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CriticGen，一个细粒度、生成感知的评估框架，通过先生成样本特定的评估维度和评分标准（动态rubric），再基于该rubric联合生成分数、理由、可执行改进建议和改进后答案，将评估转化为可操作的答案改进控制。实验表明CriticGen在rubric质量、评分相关性、理由与建议的F1分数以及答案改进率上均显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型评估方法粒度粗糙且与生成过程脱节，生成的解释过于泛化，无法为模型改进提供可操作的反馈。作者希望弥补评估与生成之间的鸿沟，使评估结果能够直接驱动答案质量的提升。

Method: CriticGen包含两个阶段：(1) 在主观、客观和自派生约束等高层类别下，为每个样本生成特定的评估维度和评分标准，形成动态rubric；(2) 以该rubric为条件，联合生成分数、评分理由、可执行的改进建议以及改进后的答案，实现rubric条件化的细粒度评估与目标性答案改进。

Result: CriticGen将rubric的相关性/覆盖率从3.33/4.03提升至3.97/4.24；Pearson和Spearman相关性分别达到0.9556和0.9560；基于准则的理由和可执行建议的F1分数从0.6369/0.5994提升至0.7554/0.7900；其反馈使73.17%的答案得到改进，非退化率达93.28%。

Conclusion: 细粒度评估应当做到实例特定且可操作。CriticGen通过生成样本特定的动态rubric并以其引导评估与改进联合过程，证明了评估框架可以同时实现高精度的评分和可靠的答案改进，为将评估转化为模型控制提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Current evaluation methods for large language models are coarse-grained and decoupled from generation, producing generic explanations that fail to provide actionable feedback for model improvement. We propose CriticGen, a fine-grained, generation-aware evaluation framework that turns evaluation into actionable control for answer improvement. CriticGen first generates sample-specific evaluation dimensions and scoring criteria under high-level categories such as subjective, objective, and self-derived constraints. These criteria then serve as a dynamic rubric for jointly producing a score, a reason, an executable refinement suggestion, and a refined answer. This rubric-conditioned refinement process enables models to diagnose flaws and perform targeted answer improvement. Experimental results show that fine-grained evaluation should be both instance-specific and actionable. CriticGen induces higher-quality rubrics, improving relevance/coverage from 3.33/4.03 to 3.97/4.24. CriticGen also achieves the best score correlations, with 0.9556 Pearson and 0.9560 Spearman, and raises the F1 of criterion-grounded reasons and executable suggestions from 0.6369/0.5994 to 0.7554/0.7900. Crucially, its feedback translates into reliable answer improvement, improving 73.17% of answers with a 93.28% non-degradation rate.

</details>


### [193] [When Does Memory Help? A Cost-Aware Evaluation of Long-Term Memory in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05441)
*Shweta Mishra,Shashank Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MERIT是一个评估LLM智能体长期记忆在工具型任务中边际效用的基准测试与框架。与现有仅基于对话问答的回忆式基准不同，MERIT通过成本核算衡量记忆是否真正改变智能体行为。在23,440个评分片段中，记忆将依赖型任务成功率从0.00提升至0.55-1.00；在更新事实场景下，嵌入检索不稳定、混合存储反而不如纯事实存储，而结构化存储与LLM摘要保持在0.70-1.00。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体长期记忆评估基准（如LoCoMo、LongMemEval）仅衡量对话历史问答能力，无法反映记忆事实是否真正改变工具使用智能体的行为决策。需要一个能够测量记忆对任务执行真实影响的评估方案。

Method: MERIT提供三个域的情景化工具使用任务，依赖于已验证的早期事实（自动泄漏检查确保不依赖）；设计包含更新事实回忆的难度阶梯；实施受控记忆损坏；对所有记忆操作进行完整token与美元计量。实验涵盖两代gpt-4.1-mini试点与预注册的3模型x3种子网格（GPT-4.1、Claude Haiku 4.5），并执行Claude Sonnet 5最新一代抽查。

Result: 记忆将依赖型任务成功率从泄漏验证的0.00基线提升至0.55-1.00。更新事实下嵌入检索跨模型波动0.30-0.95（最大种子差0.45），正确检索值仅有55%被智能体执行；而update-on-write存储（结构化事实存储与LLM摘要）保持在0.70-1.00，混合方案差于纯事实存储。交换记忆实现可改变任务成功率达60个百分点；全量回放不经济，每域最佳条件的边际效用是其每美元的2.7-3.9倍。

Conclusion: MERIT证明了记忆实现的选取可显著影响任务执行结果，强调了在不考虑成本效益的传统回忆式基准之外开展记忆评估的必要性。更新事实处理与记忆召回执行是当前智能体记忆的关键薄弱环节，记忆应朝着更新即写入与成本感知的方向设计。

Abstract: Long-term memory for LLM agents is evaluated today by conversational recall benchmarks (LoCoMo, LongMemEval), which measure question answering over dialogue history, not whether remembered facts change what a tool-using agent does. We present MERIT (Memory Evaluation for Realistic Instrumented Tasks), a benchmark and harness that measures the marginal utility of memory for task-executing agents under explicit cost accounting. MERIT provides episodic tool-use tasks in three domains whose dependence on earlier-episode facts is verified by an automated leak check; a difficulty ladder ending in updated-fact recall; controlled memory corruption; and full token and dollar metering of every memory operation. Across 23,440 scored episodes ($42.57), a two-generation pilot on gpt-4.1-mini and a preregistered 3-model x 3-seed grid (GPT-4.1, Claude Haiku 4.5; memory side held fixed), memory lifts dependent-task success from a leak-verified floor of 0.00 to 0.55-1.00. On updated facts, embedding retrieval collapses unpredictably (0.30-0.95 across models; max seed gap 0.45), and agents act on a correctly retrieved value only 55% of the time, while update-on-write stores (a structured fact store and, notably, LLM summarization) remain at 0.70-1.00; the hybrid is worse than the fact store alone. A latest-generation spot-check (Claude Sonnet 5, gated on a clean full-replay control) reproduces the pattern. Swapping a memory's implementation moves task success by up to 60 points, and full replay is never economical: the best condition per domain delivers 2.7-3.9x its marginal utility per dollar. We release the benchmark, harness, and all traces.

</details>


### [194] [AutoFyn Technical Report: Non-Parametric Expert Iteration for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05446)
*Adib Hasan,Daniel Schaffield,Akashnil Dutta,Tarik Adnan Moon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoFyn是一种基于Expert Iteration思想的智能体框架，通过持久化状态而非模型权重更新，在多个领域中取得了显著成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能体方法通常需要微调模型权重或依赖单一模型会话，难以在冻结模型的情况下持续改进性能。作者希望通过更新持久化状态来适应性地提升冻结模型的表现。

Method: AutoFyn采用Expert Iteration循环：每轮从全新的模型会话开始，通过持久化内存文件、报告和仓库状态等显式接口引入历史信息；轮次内由编排器协调多个专业智能体进行探索、规划和构建多种方案，任务驱动的验证器对工作结果进行验证并提供客观奖励信号，该奖励被回馈到持久化状态中以更新下一轮的有效策略。

Result: 在2026年国际数学奥林匹克竞赛的六个全新题目上，AutoFyn使所有有改进空间的模型得分高于其提供商的编码智能体；在Spider 2.0 dbt基准上构建出排名最高的智能体；并在Next.js、MetaMask、pnpm、Warp、LiteLLM、Langflow和Open WebUI中产出了16个经维护者确认的漏洞公告。

Conclusion: AutoFyn的形式化循环结合持久化状态和验证接口，能够在权重冻结的情况下通过外部状态更新实现策略改进，在数学、数据科学和网络安全三个领域的应用中均展现出有效性。

Abstract: We introduce AutoFyn, an agent harness inspired by the Expert Iteration algorithm, adapting a frozen model across many rounds by updating persistent state from verified reward signals rather than model weights. Each round begins from a fresh model session, and durable information is reintroduced only through explicit interfaces such as persistent memory files, reports, and repository state. Within a round, an orchestrator explores, plans and builds many alternative approaches with specialized agents, while a task-grounded verifier verifies the work and supplies an objective reward for measuring progress. This reward is distilled back into the persistent state, which updates the effective policy for the next round. In this technical report, we formalize this loop and describe its persistent state and verification interfaces. We then demonstrate its use in three domains, namely olympiad mathematics, data science, and cybersecurity. On the six fresh problems of the 2026 International Mathematical Olympiad, every model with room to improve scores higher under AutoFyn than in its provider's own coding agent. AutoFyn also built the top-ranked agent on the Spider 2.0 dbt benchmark, and has produced $16$ maintainer-confirmed vulnerability advisories in Next.js, MetaMask, pnpm, Warp, LiteLLM, Langflow, and Open WebUI.

</details>


### [195] [Damage-Aware Bandit Pruning for Vision and Language Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05448)
*Salem Ameen,Sunil Vadera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结构化后训练剪枝框架，将Transformer中的注意力头和MLP通道组选择建模为"损伤感知的多臂老虎机"问题。方法通过掩码操作在批次上估计配对损伤，采用UCB或分数Beta汤普森采样策略，在固定评估预算下逐步选择功能单元进行剪枝。实验覆盖多种语言与视觉Transformer模型，结果表明bandit方法通常优于预算贪心等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化后训练剪枝需要选择完整的功能单元（如注意力头和MLP通道组），其抑制应导致尽可能小的性能退化。现有方法在有限候选评估预算下的单元选择效率与有效性仍有不足，需要一个更高效的、损伤感知的单元选择策略。

Method: 将结构化单元选择形式化为损伤感知的multi-armed bandit问题。具体做法：在校准批次上临时掩码候选单元，定义配对损伤（掩码损失减去基损失）以减少批次间方差；采用平滑有界奖励驱动UCB策略或分数Beta Thompson Sampling；按顺序逐步添加单元以构建最终掩码；所选单元在原始稠密checkpoint中被功能性置零，反映有效的结构化抑制而非物理压缩。

Result: 在WikiText-2、LAMBADA、Imagenette上对GPT-2、OPT、Pythia、Qwen2.5、SmolLM2、ViT-B/16、DeiT-Tiny、Swin-Tiny进行了实验，与随机、幅度、静态显著性及预算贪心选择对比。5个种子下bandit方法通常在配对语言模型比较中降低退化。28项对比中23项的bootstrap置信区间排除零，11项配对检验p<0.05，BH校正后6项q<0.05。ViT-B/16和Swin-Tiny的匹配评估表明其增益不能仅由更大的候选评估预算解释。

Conclusion: 损伤感知的bandit方法为Transformer结构化剪枝的单元选择提供了有效的替代方案，在多种模型和任务上优于预算贪心等基线，且增益不是单纯源于更大的评估预算。该方法将逐步单元选择统一在bandit框架下，具有良好的鲁棒性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Structured post-training pruning of transformers requires selecting complete functional units whose suppression causes limited degradation. We formulate structured-unit selection for language and vision transformers as a damage-aware multi-armed bandit problem under a fixed candidate-evaluation budget. Attention heads and MLP channel groups are temporarily masked on calibration batches. Paired damage is the masked loss minus the base loss on the same batch, reducing batch-to-batch variation. A smooth bounded reward drives either a UCB-style policy or fractional-Beta Thompson Sampling, and the final mask is constructed sequentially by adding one unit at each step. The selected units are functionally zeroed in the original dense checkpoint; therefore, the reported parameter effects represent effective structural suppression rather than physical compression or measured speedup. Experiments on WikiText-2, LAMBADA, and Imagenette cover GPT-2, OPT, Pythia, Qwen2.5, SmolLM2, ViT-B/16, DeiT-Tiny, and Swin-Tiny, with comparisons against random, magnitude, static-saliency, and budgeted-greedy selection. Across five seeds, the bandit methods usually reduce degradation relative to budgeted greedy in the paired language-model comparisons. Of 28 comparisons highlighted in the paper, 23 bootstrap confidence intervals exclude zero and 11 paired tests have p < 0.05; six have q < 0.05 after Benjamini-Hochberg correction across the full family of 116 dataset-wise tests. Matched-evaluation results for ViT-B/16 and Swin-Tiny indicate that their gains are not explained solely by a larger candidate-evaluation budget.

</details>


### [196] [Compiling VGDL into Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05459)
*Mohit Jiwatode,Bodo Rosenhahn,Alexander Dockhorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个确定性框架，将Video Game Description Language（VGDL）描述的游戏直接编译为动态结构因果模型（DSCM），无需从游戏轨迹或LLM输出中推断因果结构，从而保证对游戏真实机制的绝对因果忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体常依赖虚假相关性，大语言模型易幻觉游戏规则，而现有因果强化学习虽能提升可解释性，但缺乏将复杂游戏机制正式映射到因果模型的系统方法。

Method: 将VGDL中描述的游戏组件（精灵动态、交互规则、终止条件）直接翻译为显式结构方程。每个游戏tick表示从t时刻到t+1时刻状态变量的因果转移，以此构建动态结构因果模型。

Result: 所构建的因果模型提供透明的因果路径，支持反事实推理、因果强化学习智能体训练以及程序化内容验证，并对游戏真实机制具有绝对因果保真度。

Conclusion: 该框架在符号化游戏描述与因果驱动的游戏AI之间建立了原则性的桥梁，为因果强化学习和游戏内容分析提供了可靠的坚实基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning and large language models often struggle to accurately capture the causal mechanics of game environments. Standard reinforcement learning agents tend to rely on spurious correlations, while large language models are prone to hallucinating game rules. Although causal reinforcement learning improves interpretability, there is currently no formal methodology to map complex game mechanics directly into causal models. To address this, we propose a deterministic framework that compiles games specified in the Video Game Description Language into Dynamic Structural Causal Models. Rather than inferring causal structures from gameplay traces or noisy large language models' outputs, our methodology directly translates game components, including sprite dynamics, interaction rules, and termination conditions, into explicit structural equations. Each game tick represents a causal transition from state variables at time $t$ to $t+1$. By establishing this grounded mapping, the approach guarantees absolute causal fidelity to the ground-truth game mechanics. The resulting models offer transparent causal pathways that support counterfactual reasoning, causal reinforcement learning agent training, and procedural content validation. This framework provides a principled bridge between symbolic game descriptions and causally grounded game AI.

</details>


### [197] [ARC-Bench: Closed-Loop Replanning Masks Broken Action Ranking in Frozen JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05461)
*Zhengshu Zhang,Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过引入ARC-Bench基准直接审计了冻结JEPA风格潜在世界模型中"潜在空间距离可作为动作排序依据"这一核心假设，发现该假设在官方JEPA-WM检查点上严重且结构性地失败（排名倒置），并揭示了闭环重规划掩盖了这一缺陷的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无奖励潜在世界模型的核心前提是潜在空间中的距离可以用于动作排序（候选动作的预测未来嵌入越接近目标嵌入越优），但这一潜在假设从未被直接审计。本文旨在填补这一空白，直接检验"潜在接近性可排序动作"的假设是否成立。

Method: 作者提出ARC-Bench协议：一个无泄漏、固定候选的动作排名评测基准，用于测量冻结的JEPA风格目标是否能正确对候选动作进行排名。测试对象为官方发布的JEPA-WM检查点，覆盖导航和操作类控制任务；并通过控制实验（视频预训练编码器替换、训练充分性、匹配预算骨干、度量循环性控制）排除简单解释。

Result: 该假设失败且呈结构性：官方操作审计中得分最高的候选几乎总是次优的，迷宫域中也出现同样的排名倒置。将DINOv2替换为V-JEPA 1/2（ViT-L/ViT-G尺度）缺陷依然存在。同时发现闭环重规划掩盖了这一缺陷——降低重规划频率后成功率先降后崩，被频繁重规划挽救的情节在PointMaze首次规划诊断中富集了严重排名失败。

Conclusion: ARC-Bench提供了测量工具，屏蔽机制提供了解释：闭环成功率系统性地高估了冻结潜在表示的排名能力。研究工作表明任何围绕潜在空间规划器进行自适应、摊销或重规划的方法都应在不直接审计官方JEPA-WM动作排名能力的情况下持谨慎态度。

Abstract: Reward-free latent world models plan by scoring candidate actions with distances in a frozen latent space: an action is preferred if its predicted future embedding lands closer to the goal embedding. This silently assumes that latent closeness is action-rankable, i.e., that ordering candidates by latent distance agrees with ordering them by true cost. We audit this assumption directly. We introduce ARC-Bench, a no-leak, fixed-candidate protocol that measures whether frozen JEPA-style objectives rank candidate actions correctly, and apply it to official released JEPA-WM checkpoints across navigation and manipulation-style control. The assumption fails, severely and structurally: on the official manipulation audits the top-scored candidate is almost always suboptimal, and the same inversion appears in the maze domains. A controlled visual-backbone extension shows that the defect persists when DINOv2 is replaced by video-pretrained V-JEPA 1 and V-JEPA 2 encoders at ViT-L/ViT-G scale. Provenance, undertraining, matched-budget backbone controls, and metric-circularity controls rule out trivial explanations. We then explain why this defect has stayed invisible: closed-loop replanning masks it. When we reduce the planner's replanning frequency, success collapses in both a navigation and a manipulation domain, and the episodes rescued by frequent replanning are enriched for severe first-plan ranking failures in the PointMaze first-plan diagnostic. Closed-loop success rates therefore systematically overstate the rankability of frozen latent representations. ARC-Bench supplies the measurement, and the masking mechanism the explanation, for methods that adapt, amortize, or replan around latent-space planners without directly auditing released JEPA-WM action rankability.

</details>


### [198] [RAPID: Reliability-Aware Pair Importance Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05481)
*Ali Mahdavi,Azadeh Zamanifar,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Omid Kashefi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RAPID（Reliability Aware Pair Importance Distillation），一种用于示例间关系蒸馏的配对选择与加权方法，将可靠性门控的关系目标与自适应配对提议解耦，并通过精确逆提议校正保证估计器无偏。在AG News和SST-2两个文本分类蒸馏任务中，可靠性门控的关系蒸馏取得了最高平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 示例间关系蒸馏通过匹配教师表征几何来传递知识，需要在一个mini-batch内计算所有示例对的关系，这在批大小上具有二次复杂度。均匀子采样对有限的关系预算利用效率不高，无法区分哪些配对关系更有价值，需要更智能的配对选择与加权策略来有效利用有限的预算。

Method: RAPID将目标构建与配对采样解耦为两个独立模块：1) 可靠性门控的关系目标——通过校准的教师熵确定哪些教师关系应被强调；2) 全支持自适应配对提议——利用分离（detached）的师生残差确定哪些关系值得评估。通过精确的逆提议校正（exact inverse proposal correction），使损失和梯度估计器相对于门控的mini-batch目标条件无偏。

Result: 在AG News（BERT到DistilBERT蒸馏，关系预算256，三对种子）中，可靠性门控关系蒸馏取得94.285±0.054%的平均准确率；在SST-2（DistilBERT到DistilBERT，关系预算64）中取得88.800±0.532%。RAPID分别以94.241±0.025%和88.685±0.462%位居第二，两者均优于交叉熵基线（94.154±0.124%和87.271±0.162%）。

Conclusion: 结果表明目标可靠性（由教师熵决定）与评估优先级（由自适应配对提议决定）是可分离的设计维度。可靠性门控的目标带来最高平均准确率，自适应提议的表现处于种子级变异范围内。该模块化视角为关系蒸馏的配对选择与加权提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Inter example relational distillation transfers a teacher's representation geometry by matching relations among examples within a mini batch. Computing all pairs has quadratic complexity in the batch size, whereas uniform subsampling may use a limited relation budget inefficiently. We introduce Reliability Aware Pair Importance Distillation, or RAPID, which separates a reliability gated relational target from a full support adaptive pair proposal. Reliability determines which teacher relations are emphasized, while calibrated teacher entropy and detached student-teacher residuals determine which relations are evaluated. Exact inverse proposal correction makes the loss and gradient estimators conditionally unbiased with respect to the gated mini batch target. We evaluate RAPID in two text classification settings: AG News with BERT-to-DistilBERT distillation using three paired seeds and a relation budget of 256, and SST-2 with DistilBERT to DistilBERT distillation using three paired seeds and a relation budget of 64. Reliability gated relational distillation achieves the highest observed mean student accuracy on both datasets: 94.285 plus or minus 0.054 percent on AG News and 88.800 plus or minus 0.532 percent on SST-2. RAPID ranks second, achieving 94.241 plus or minus 0.025 percent and 88.685 plus or minus 0.462 percent, respectively, compared with 94.154 plus or minus 0.124 percent and 87.271 plus or minus 0.162 percent for the cross entropy baseline. Pilot evaluations are counted toward the same total budget as the main relation evaluations. Across both settings, the gated target yields the highest mean accuracy, while the adaptive proposal remains within seed-level variation. These results support the modular view that target reliability and evaluation priority are separable design dimensions.

</details>


### [199] [PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN: Prior-Guided Joint Probabilistic Forecasting of Clinical Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05488)
*Weizhi Nie,Rihao Chang,Weijie Wang,Yuting Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN，一个以轨迹为先的框架，用于多变量生理学的联合概率预测。该方法结合了缺失感知时间编码器、软器官系统先验、患者特定关系、非线性Kolmogorov-Arnold消息传递和低秩多元Student-t头。在MIMIC-IV队列（6,882名患者，54,694个窗口）上，该方法在归一化MAE上排名第二（0.37727±0.00029），在RMSE上最优（0.52656±0.00034）。然而，其轨迹衍生的风险评分弱于专用GRU-D分类器（AUROC 0.603对0.650），表明准确预测生理学轨迹并不自动保证校准的事件检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床恶化是通过耦合且部分观测的轨迹展开的，而非单一诊断标签。作者认为现有方法忽略了多变量生理信号之间的耦合关系与轨迹的时序结构，因此需要一个轨迹优先的统一预测框架来联合建模生理学信号的演变。

Method: PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN由四部分构成：缺失感知时间编码器负责处理不完整观测；软器官系统先验注入领域知识；患者特定关系编码个体差异；非线性Kolmogorov-Arnold消息传递进行图节点间信息融合；低秩多元Student-t头输出联合概率分布。模型采用24小时历史窗口预测未来6小时的多变量生理信号。

Result: 在5个随机种子及13个模型的对比中，该方法在冻结的MIMIC-IV衍生队列（6,882名患者、54,694个窗口）上，取得归一化MAE第二低（0.37727±0.00029），RMSE最低（0.52656±0.00034），较确定性TimeKAN的MAE降低0.52%。概率预测方面边际NLL为0.66380、CRPS为0.27301，实测覆盖率对名义50%/80%/95%区间分别为0.533/0.831/0.958。移除关系结构造成最大的消融损失；增大协方差秩提升联合似然而几乎不影响点精度。但其基于轨迹的风险评分劣于专用GRU-D分类器（AUROC 0.603对0.650）。

Conclusion: 联合轨迹预测是具有良好的可检查性的中间任务，但准确的生理学预测本身并不能保证训练出一个校准的事件检测器。预测框架与下游临床分类目标之间存在差距，需要在后续工作中弥合。

Abstract: Clinical deterioration unfolds through coupled, partially observed trajectories, not a single diagnostic label. We introduce PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN, a trajectory-first framework for joint probabilistic forecasting of multivariate physiology. It combines missingness-aware temporal encoders, a soft organ-system prior, patient-specific relations, nonlinear Kolmogorov-Arnold messages, and a low-rank multivariate Student-t head. We evaluate 24-hour histories and six-hour forecasts on a frozen MIMIC-IV-derived cohort of 6,882 patients and 54,694 windows. Across five seeds and 13 models, PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN obtains the second-lowest normalized MAE (0.37727 +/- 0.00029) and the lowest RMSE (0.52656 +/- 0.00034). It reduces MAE by 0.52% relative to deterministic TimeKAN. For probabilistic forecasting, it reaches a marginal NLL of 0.66380 and a CRPS of 0.27301.
