Motivation: 基于提案的控制器（如学习的策略、语言模型规划器等黑盒生成器）越来越普遍地部署在运行时验证门之后。现有的逐候选认证模式无法组合：在重试或best-of-k选择下，单候选虚报水平α会膨胀到1-(1-α)^k。因此需要一种与生成器解耦的、可组合的安全性保证框架。

Method: 提出了"同时集合健全性"的概念——对包含无非可行动作的可准入提案集合进行认证，证明其是与生成器无关的准入健全性（所有生成器上执行非可行提案的最坏概率等于集合失败概率）的必要充分条件。进一步结合设计时证书和禁止旁路规则（no-bypass rule）导出合同安全的充分条件。同时证明了在部分观测下的观测约束定理（abar+β+δ≥1），并引入顺序风险账本（sequential risk ledger）配合时间一致置信管道使保证可实施。

Result: 建立了生成器无关的准入健全性的充要条件；合同安全违反界限Γ+Σε_t+η对任意甚至对抗性生成器替换保持不变。第二个定理为部分观测下任何准入机制提供了信息论下界。确定性准入计算将所有统计风险集中在状态估计中。Simplex风格运行时保证和控制障碍函数滤波被恢复为退化情形。

Conclusion: 集合级别的同时认证（而非逐候选认证）是实现生成器无关安全保证的关键。该框架为部署各类黑盒生成控制器提供了统一的安全验证理论，并包含现有方法作为特例。

Abstract: Proposal-based controllers---learned policies, language-model planners, and other black-box \emph{generators}---are increasingly deployed behind runtime verification gates. We ask when the closed-loop safety guarantee decouples from the generator. The prevailing per-candidate certification pattern does not compose: under retry or best-of-$k$ selection a per-candidate false-admission level $α$ can inflate to $1-(1-α)^{k}$. Our main theorem shows that \emph{simultaneous setwise soundness}---certifying a set of admissible proposals containing no nonviable action---is necessary and sufficient for generator-independent \emph{admission soundness}, the worst case over all generators of executing a nonviable proposal equalling the probability of setwise failure; together with a design-time certificate and a no-bypass rule it is sufficient for \emph{contract safety}, with violation bound $Γ+\sum_t\varepsilon_t+η$ invariant under arbitrary, even adversarial, replacement of the generator. A second theorem bounds every admission mechanism under partial observation: for a fixed probing and admission policy, if two state hypotheses whose information laws lie within total-variation distance $δ$ require different safe decisions, then $\abar+β+δ\ge1$. A sequential risk ledger makes the guarantee implementable with time-uniform confidence tubes, and shows that deterministic admission computations concentrate all statistical risk in state estimation. Simplex-style runtime assurance and control-barrier-function filtering are recovered as degenerate cases.

</details>


### [235] [DAREBench: Deployment-Aware and Reliable Evaluation of Models as Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06059)
*Yu Liu,Zhilin Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Shaojie Zhang,Zheyuan Deng,Tingwei Huang,Zhenbo Luo,Lei Jiang,Yanbing Liu,Pei Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DAREBench是一个面向通用智能体部署评估的基准测试，基于共享的OpenClaw执行环境，将233个任务组织为2×3工作负载矩阵（输入模态×执行形式），采用统一的基于契约的评估协议和证据审计，评估了23个商业API模型和12个本地部署开源模型的7,587次模型-任务运行，发现没有单一模型在所有工作负载组中占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正从问答系统演化为通用智能体，但现有评估基准受限于特定任务类型、执行环境或评分协议，导致可比性、可解释性和可靠性不足，难以支撑部署决策。需要一个能够捕捉工作负载变化并支持可靠智能体评估的基准。

Method: DAREBench基于共享的OpenClaw执行环境，从22个源基准中筛选和改编233个任务，组织为2×3工作负载矩阵（由输入模态和执行形式两个维度定义），在统一的基于契约的评估协议下运行，并采用基于证据的评分审计机制。在7,587次模型-任务运行中评估了23个商业API模型和12个本地部署的开源模型，同时报告准确率、token消耗及API参考成本。

Result: 结果显示：没有单一模型能在所有工作负载组中占据主导地位；文本和多模态任务在准确率与成本之间表现出不同的权衡特性；本地开源模型在若干工作负载组中具有竞争力，但整体上仍落后于前沿商业模型。

Conclusion: 智能体的部署和模型选择应综合考虑工作负载画像、部署模式以及准确率-成本权衡，而不应仅依赖单一综合分数做出决策。

Abstract: As large language models evolve from question-answering systems into general-purpose agents, evaluation must move beyond static answer correctness to assess multimodal perception, multi-step execution, tool use, and artifact delivery. However, existing benchmarks are often tied to specific task types, execution environments, or scoring protocols, limiting their comparability, interpretability, and reliability for deployment decisions. We introduce DAREBench (Deployment-Aware and Reliable Evaluation of Models as Agents), a benchmark designed to capture workload variation and support reliable agent evaluation. Built on a shared OpenClaw execution environment, DAREBench organizes 233 tasks selected and adapted from 22 source benchmarks into a $2\times3$ workload matrix defined by input modality and execution form, and evaluates them under a unified contract-based protocol with evidence-based score auditing. We evaluate 23 commercial API models and 12 locally deployed open-weight models over 7,587 model--task runs, reporting accuracy and token consumption alongside reference costs for API models. Results show that no single model dominates all workload groups, text and multimodal tasks exhibit distinct accuracy--cost trade-offs, and local open-weight models are competitive in several groups but still trail frontier commercial models overall. These findings suggest that agent deployment and model selection should consider workload profiles, deployment mode, and accuracy--cost trade-offs rather than rely on a single aggregate score.

</details>


### [236] [Explaining AI Agents Through Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06063)
*Vittoria Vineis,Fabiano Veglianti,Lorenzo Antonelli,Claudia Di Carlo,Matteo Silvestri,Gabriele Tolomei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对AI智能体的事后可解释框架，将智能体的执行轨迹转化为结构化报告和忠实于行为的自然语言解释，并在多个基准和架构上验证其生成高质量、可追踪解释的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在真实世界中越来越多地被部署，与外部工具交互并做顺序决策，但人类监督有限。现有XAI方法无法为这类交互式、多步骤系统提供过程级透明度，亟需专门针对AI智能体的可解释性方法。

Method: 提出一个事后(post-hoc)XAI框架，仅依赖智能体的执行轨迹，将其转换为结构化报告和忠实于可观察行为的自然语言解释。由于只基于执行轨迹，该框架可跨架构、环境和任务通用。

Result: 通过人类评估和自动评估，在多个基准和架构上证明该框架能生成高质量、轨迹忠实的解释，能可靠地识别无支撑的主张、不合理的动作和证据缺口，优于朴素的LLM生成的解释。

Conclusion: 基于执行轨迹的事后可解释框架能够为AI智能体提供过程级透明度和可审计的解释，弥补传统XAI方法在此类交互式系统中的不足。

Abstract: AI Agents are increasingly deployed in real-world settings, where they interact with external tools and make sequential decisions with limited human oversight. This creates a pressing need for reliable and auditable explanations of what an agent did and why. However, traditional Explainable AI (XAI) methods fall short of providing the process-level transparency required for such interactive, multi-step systems, motivating a paradigm shift toward approaches specifically designed for AI Agents. To address this gap, we present a post-hoc XAI framework that transforms a lengthy agent's execution trace into a structured report and a faithful natural-language explanation explicitly grounded in its observable behavior. Because it relies solely on execution traces, the framework applies across different agent architectures, environments, and tasks. Human and automated evaluations across multiple benchmarks and architectures show that our framework produces high-quality, trace-faithful explanations while reliably identifying unsupported claims, unjustified actions, and evidence gaps, outperforming naive LLM-generated explanations.

</details>


### [237] [Generating Instance Generators in PDDL Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06071)
*Nicola J. Müller,Naya Rudolph,Katharina Stein,Jörg Hoffmann,Ayal Taitler,Timo P. Gros*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用LLM自动生成PDDL规划域中的实例生成程序，并通过内置的正确性检查保证其合法性，从而替代传统的人工编写实例生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDDL作为AI规划领域的标准语言，只定义谓词和行为模式（域），却无法定义实例集合的合法约束。这导致实例生成一直是临时性的，依赖人工编写的特定于域和子集的生成器。已有推理和学习方法虽试图解决此问题，但存在可扩展性限制。

Method: 利用大型语言模型（LLMs）自动生成实例生成程序，并在程序中内置正确性检查（prescribed checks），从而提供合法性（soundness）保证。

Result: 自动生成的实例生成器能够高效地产生大量合法且多样化的实例。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的实例生成方法可行有效，克服了以往方法的可扩展性局限，为PDDL实例生成提供了一种新的通用且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: PDDL, the de-facto standard language in the AI Planning community, is designed to specify planning domains: sets of instances that share the same predicates and action schemas. Yet it does not provide any means to specify the actual instance set, i.e., legality constraints on initial states and goal conditions, as well as possibly domain subset constraints specifying an instance subset we are interested in. One consequence of this is that instance generation has always been ad-hoc, with manually written domain- and subset-specific instance generators. Recent work has started to address this, through reasoning and learning methods that however suffer from scalability limitations. Here we introduce an alternative approach, leveraging LLMs to generate instance-generation programs, with built-in soundness guarantees through prescribed checks. We show that these automatically generated instance generators return large numbers of sound and diverse instances efficiently.

</details>


### [238] [LayerRoute: Action-Conditioned Mixture-of-Layers Routing for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06079)
*Zheng Lu,Haoran Liao,Wanqi Zhong,Yunhe Ni,Lijie Wang,Xingjie Fan,Zhisheng Chen,Yantang Qu,Meijia Chen,Tianyu Xin,Zirui Song,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LayerRoute，一个动作条件化的表示路由接口，通过Layer Mixture Router动态混合缓存的VLM层表示，以及Action-State Reread复用早期动作表示，使VLA策略能够自适应地访问VLM层和动作表示，在多项仿真和真实世界基准上一致地改进了StarVLA-π和π0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA接口在表示访问上灵活性有限：VLM信息通过为每个动作层分配固定层暴露，而中间动作状态仅通过残差流隐式传播，没有显式复用。然而不同操作任务可能需要不同的层表示混合（从局部视觉几何到抽象的语言对齐语义），因此需要更灵活的表示路由机制。

Method: LayerRoute包含两个核心组件：(1) Layer Mixture Router——动作条件化的路由器，动态地对缓存的VLM层表示形成混合；(2) Action-State Reread——显式复用早期的动作表示。两者共同构成一个动作条件化的表示路由接口，使VLA策略能够按需自适应地访问不同的表示层级。

Result: 在多样化的仿真和真实世界基准上，LayerRoute一致地改进了StarVLA-π和π0.5，在LIBERO Long上实现了高达7.2的提升，仅增加了0.31%/3.87%的额外参数。消融实验验证了动作条件化层路由的收益，路由分析揭示了跨动作层和任务设置的结构化分配模式。

Conclusion: LayerRoute证明了通过动作条件化的自适应层路由而非固定层分配，可以显著提升VLA策略的性能，同时参数开销极小。路由分析展示的结构化分配模式为进一步理解VLA内部表示利用方式提供了见解。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies leverage pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) to guide action generation for robot control. VLMs provide hierarchical visual-semantic representations that evolve across layers, from local visual geometry to abstract, language-aligned semantics; different manipulation tasks may therefore require different mixtures of layer representations. Meanwhile, the action module maintains intermediate representations that evolve throughout action computation and may provide useful information for subsequent decisions. However, existing VLA interfaces offer limited flexibility in representation access: VLM information is exposed through fixed layer assignments for each action layer, while intermediate action states are only propagated implicitly through residual streams without explicit reuse. We introduce LayerRoute, an action-conditioned representation routing interface that enables adaptive access to VLM layers and action representations. The Layer Mixture Router dynamically forms mixtures of cached VLM representations, while Action-State Reread reuses earlier action representations. Across diverse simulation and real-world benchmarks, LayerRoute consistently improves StarVLA-$π$ and $π_{0.5}$, achieving up to 7.2 gains on LIBERO Long with only 0.31% / 3.87% additional parameters. Ablation studies validate the benefit of action-conditioned layer routing, while routing analyses reveal structured allocation patterns across action layers and task settings.

</details>


### [239] [SAP: State-Guided Data Synthesis with Argument Provenance for Multi-Turn Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06124)
*Zichen Tian,Jinpeng Chen,Cheng Gong,Suiyun Zhang,Rui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAP是一种状态引导的数据合成方法，通过引入工具参数溯源约束和回合级验证，高效构建具有长程依赖关系的高质量多轮工具使用轨迹，训练出的SAP-4B模型在多个基准上可与更大规模的模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量多轮工具使用数据对训练智能体模型至关重要，但现有数据合成方法往往低估了参数级依赖关系，导致模型即使选择了正确的工具，也可能因填入捏造、过时或弱溯源的参数值而导致任务执行失败。

Method: SAP将状态引导、工具参数溯源约束和回合级验证相结合，高效构建具有长程依赖关系和高准确率的工具使用轨迹。具体包括：(1)状态引导生成过程，(2)工具参数必须源于先前状态或输出的溯源约束，(3)在生成过程中进行回合级验证以确保轨迹质量。

Result: 利用SAP生成的数据训练出SAP-4B模型，在多个基准上与更大规模的模型相比具有高度竞争力。代码、合成数据和训练权重已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: SAP通过解决工具参数级依赖缺失的问题，展示了在有限模型规模下通过高质量受约束数据合成即可达到强竞争力的训练效果，为该方向提供了可复现的开源资源。

Abstract: High-quality multi-turn tool-use data is essential for training agentic models, yet existing data synthesis methods often underrepresent the argument-level dependencies that are critical to long-horizon tool use. As a result, even when a model selects the correct tool, task execution may still fail because the model fills tool arguments with fabricated, stale, or weakly grounded values. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{State-Guided Data Synthesis with Argument Provenance (SAP)}. SAP combines state guidance, tool-argument provenance constraints, and turn-level validation to efficiently construct tool-use trajectories with long-range dependencies and high accuracy. Using data generated by SAP, we build SAP-4B, which is highly competitive even when compared with much larger models across multiple benchmarks. Source code, synthesized data, and trained weights are available at https://github.com/Zichen1024/SAP.

</details>


### [240] [CWF: A Collaborative Writing Framework for Personalized and Reliable Popular Science Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06126)
*Ruibiao Fu,Di Tang,Yunlong Yang,Ran Wang,Sicheng Lu,Peixuan Wu,Xiaoyu Fan,Jiacheng Ma,HaoZhe Luo,Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"个性化且可靠的科普写作"新任务，旨在根据不同认知水平读者调整科学解释同时保持事实准确。构建了39,134条目的数据集和PSCB基准，提出DA-MoE解耦受众适应与领域知识，并设计多智能体事实核查机制，在PSCB上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科普写作需要在个性化（适配不同认知水平的读者）与事实准确性之间取得平衡，但提高个性化往往引入简化，从而增加幻觉和事实扭曲的风险。现有方法难以同时兼顾这两个方面，且在证据稀缺场景下缺乏可靠的验证与修正机制。

Method: 1) 构建了39,134条目的数据集及读者导向的个性化科学传播基准PSCB，联合评估受众适配度与事实准确性；2) 提出DA-MoE（Domain-Aware Mixture of Experts），通过分离建模显式解耦受众适应与领域知识，降低数据与计算需求并提升跨领域和跨受众的泛化能力；3) 设计多智能体事实核查机制，在证据稀缺场景下通过角色特化智能体辩论增强有限证据，并在图上传播置信度以实现稳健的验证与修订。

Result: 在PSCB基准上的实验表明，所提方法取得了最先进的性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 个性化与事实准确性虽然在科普写作中存在张力，但通过解耦受众适应与领域知识建模（DA-MoE）以及多智能体协作的事实核查机制，可以有效地同时提升两者，为科学传播的自然语言生成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We introduce Personalized and Reliable Popular Science Writing, a novel task that requires adapting scientific explanations to audiences with different cognitive levels while preserving factual accuracy. However, improving personalization often introduces simplifications that increase the risk of hallucination and factual distortion. To address these challenges, we first construct a dataset of 39,134 entries and a reader-centric Personalized Science Communication Benchmark (PSCB) that jointly evaluates audience adaptation and factual accuracy. To reduce data and computational requirements while improving generalization across domains and audiences, we introduce DA-MoE, which explicitly decouples audience adaptation from domain knowledge through separate modeling. To enable robust verification and revision in evidence-scarce scenarios, a multi-agent fact-checking mechanism that augments limited evidence with role-specific agent debate and propagates confidence over a graph is proposed. Experiments on PSCB show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/DPInnovationWorks/CWF.

</details>


### [241] [Substrate-Portable Execution for Production LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06128)
*Tarun Gopinath,Atul Kulkarni,Vijay Rajakumar,Shrikar Katti,Parthasarathy Govindarajen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种绑定自适应的代理执行平台（binding-adaptive agent execution platform），将工作流定义与执行基底解耦，使同一LLM代理工作流可以在实时流式推理、异步SWF编排和分布式Flink批处理三种执行模式间无缝切换，无需修改业务代码，且输出质量无差异，批处理推理成本降低50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境的LLM代理需要支持实时服务、异步后台任务和高吞吐批量工作负载等多种执行模式，每种模式对runtime有不同的服务级别目标。然而现有执行语义通常与单一运行时耦合，导致跨模式复用流式编排会产生阻塞问题，且无法利用提供50%折扣的批量推理API。Rufus（服务于数百万Amazon客户的对话式AI助手）在实际中遇到了这一可移植性问题。

Method: 平台将工作流定义为类型化的数据流图（typed dataflow graph），随后编译到三种不同的执行基底：in-process流式处理用于实时服务、AWS SWF持久化编排用于异步执行、Apache Flink分布式流处理用于批量推理。LLM推理被表示为可挂起的图节点，其行为随执行基底的自适应变化：在线模式支持流式投递、异步模式支持持久化重试、离线模式支持批量提交。整个过程无需修改任何工作流代码。

Result: 在五种编排模式（单次推理RAG、迭代式ReAct、组合式PreAct、条件路由、多代理深度研究）下，对数十个生产代理配置进行了验证。结果表明三种绑定基底下输出质量无可检测差异，批量执行将单查询推理成本降至与官方批量API定价一致的水平，且与流式路径在生产规模下并行运行。

Conclusion: 工作流定义与执行基底分离是解决LLM代理多模式执行可移植性的有效方案。绑定自适应的执行平台可以在不牺牲输出质量的前提下，让同一代理工作流适配实时、异步和批处理等多样化生产需求，同时充分利用批量推理API的成本优势。

Abstract: Production LLM agents execute tool-calling loops, retrieval chains, and compositional workflows in multiple modes, yet execution semantics are often coupled to one runtime. We encountered this portability problem in Rufus, a conversational AI assistant with a large tool catalog that serves millions of Amazon customers. Rufus supports real-time serving, asynchronous background tasks, and high-volume batch workloads such as evaluation and content pregeneration. Each mode has distinct service-level objectives and typically uses a separate runtime. Reusing streaming orchestration makes asynchronous and batch workloads blocking and prevents use of batch inference APIs, which offer a 50 percent discount at published prices. We present a binding-adaptive agent execution platform that separates workflow definition from execution substrate. Developers define a workflow once as a typed dataflow graph. The platform compiles the graph to in-process streaming for real-time serving, durable AWS SWF orchestration for asynchronous execution, or distributed Apache Flink stream processing for batch inference. No workflow code changes are required. LLM inference is represented as a suspendable graph node whose behavior depends on the substrate: streaming delivery online, durable retry asynchronously, and batched submission offline. We validated dozens of production agent configurations across five orchestration patterns: single-inference RAG, iterative ReAct, compositional PreAct, conditional routing, and multi-agent deep research. Across all three bindings, we found no detectable difference in output quality. Batch execution reduced per-query inference cost in line with published batch API pricing while operating alongside the streaming path at production scale.

</details>


### [242] [IIns-VAE+: A Robust Transfer Learning Framework for Environmental Identification in Wireless Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06131)
*Yuxiao Li,Keke Hu,Bobai Zhao,Santiago Mazuelas,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IIns-VAE+混合模型，将IIns-VAE框架与Minimax风险分类器(MRC)结合，以提升6G ISAC系统中无线感知环境下跨域迁移学习的适应性，在三种真实世界迁移场景下显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G ISAC系统的无线感知环境识别需要可靠的情境感知能力，但深度学习模型在跨环境域偏移下泛化能力差。已有IIns-VAE能够学习丰富表征，但其神经分类器仍易受分布变化影响，需要更具适应性的分类机制。

Method: 提出IIns-VAE+混合模型，结合IIns-VAE框架与Minimax风险分类器(MRC)，利用MRC的稳健性增强分类器在域偏移下的适应能力，在三种迁移学习场景（通用到特定房间、高到低标签分辨率、混合到特定环境）下进行验证。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的三种迁移学习场景中，IIns-VAE+均显著优于基线方法，证明其在构建未来6G系统适应性感知网络中的关键价值。

Conclusion: IIns-VAE+通过结合表征学习与稳健分类器，有效提升了模型在域偏移条件下的泛化能力，为6G系统中可适应、鲁棒的感知网络提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Environmental identification in wireless sensing is essential for 6G integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems to achieve reliable situational awareness. However, deep learning (DL) models for this task often fail to generalize under domain shift across diverse environments. While the Inter-Instance Variational Auto-encoder (IIns-VAE) learns features of rich representation, its neural classifier remains vulnerable to these distribution changes. In this paper, we propose IIns-VAE+, a hybrid model that combines the IIns-VAE framework with Minimax Risk Classifiers (MRC) to improve adaptability in transfer learning scenarios. We use real-world datasets to evaluate our framework across three transfer learning scenarios, including general to specific room environments, high to low label resolutions, and mixed to specific environments. The experimental results indicate that IIns-VAE+ significantly outperforms baselines, demonstrating its critical value in building adaptable and robust perceptive networks in future 6G systems.

</details>


### [243] [MVFA: A Multi-View Text-Guided Multimodal Fusion LLM Adapter for Sentiment Analysis and Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06188)
*Pengfei Shao,Jisheng Dang,Jiawen Fang,Ning Liu,Wencan Zhang,Bimei Wang,Jingwen Zhao,Jianhuang Lai,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MVFA（多视图文本引导多模态融合适配器），一种参数高效的框架，通过冻结LLM增强多模态情感分析与情绪识别能力，在三个数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感分析需要有效建模文本、声学、视觉模态间的异构交互。然而，将LLM适配到多模态情感计算领域面临两大挑战：全模型微调计算开销巨大，而现有轻量适配器在跨模态融合过程中无法保留丰富的文本语义线索。

Method: MVFA首先通过最大池化、均值池化和注意力池化构建互补的文本视图；这些视图随后引导与音频和视觉特征的跨模态交互。融合后的多模态表示通过增强Q-Former融合模块压缩为一组紧凑的可学习伪令牌。以ChatGLM3-6B-base为主要骨干，并在LLaMA2-7B和Qwen3-8B上验证跨骨干可迁移性。

Result: 在CH-SIMS V2.0上达到84.62% Acc2和84.59% F1，在MELD上达到67.36% Acc和66.03% WF1，在CHERMA上达到74.66% Acc，均取得SOTA性能且仅更新少量参数。

Conclusion: 多视图文本引导融合被证明是参数高效的多模态LLM情感计算适配的有效且可扩展范式。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis and emotion recognition in conversations demand effective modeling of heterogeneous interactions across textual, acoustic, and visual modalities. Although large language models (LLMs) offer powerful language understanding, adapting them to multimodal affective computing remains challenging: full-model fine-tuning is computationally prohibitive, while many existing lightweight adapters fail to preserve rich textual cues during cross-modal fusion. To address these limitations, we propose the multi-view text-guided multimodal fusion adapter (MVFA), a parameter-efficient framework that augments frozen LLMs with strong multimodal reasoning capability. MVFA first constructs complementary text views via max pooling, mean pooling, and attention pooling; these views then guide cross-modal interactions with audio and visual features. The fused multimodal representations are subsequently compressed into a compact set of learnable pseudo-tokens through an Enhanced Q-Former Fusion Module. Using ChatGLM3-6B-base as the primary backbone, we further validate MVFA on LLaMA2-7B and Qwen3-8B to examine its portability across multiple frozen LLM backbones. MVFA is evaluated on three challenging datasets: CH-SIMS V2.0, MELD, and CHERMA. Experimental results demonstrate that MVFA achieves state-of-the-art performance on key metrics while updating only a small fraction of parameters. Specifically, it attains 84.62\% Acc2 and 84.59\% F1 on CH-SIMS V2.0, 67.36\% Acc and 66.03\% WF1 on MELD, and 74.66\% Acc on CHERMA. These findings establish multi-view text-guided fusion as an effective and scalable paradigm for parameter-efficient multimodal LLM adaptation in affective computing. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Overwhelm1208/MVFA.

</details>


### [244] [Customer Relationship Intelligence: Integrating CRM and MDM for Enhanced Customer Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06189)
*Tejasvi c. Addagada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究检验了CRM、MDM和CKM如何共同构成客户关系智能（CRI）框架以增强客户参与（CE）。基于100名跨行业受访者的横断面调查，通过Spearman相关和序数逻辑回归分析发现：CRM和CKM是CE的显著正向预测因子，而MDM虽为正向但非显著的直接效应；并行中介分析未支持MDM通过CRM或CKM的间接效应（H4不成立）。模型解释CE方差的20.5%。结论认为CRM和CKM是CE的主要驱动因素，MDM作为基础性数据质量赋能者发挥支撑作用，结果属探索性发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在填补客户关系管理领域的一个理论空白——大多数研究单独考察CRM、MDM或CKM各自对客户参与的影响，而缺乏一个整合视角来理解三者如何协同构成更全面的CRI框架。作者希望厘清MDM在其中的角色定位（直接驱动还是基础赋能），以指导企业在数据治理与客户关系管理方面的资源投入决策。

Method: 采用横断面调查设计，收集零售、医疗保健、IT和电信四个行业的100名参与者数据。统计分析包括：(1) Spearman rho相关系数检验变量间的双变量关系；(2) 序数逻辑回归（IBM SPSS）检验CRM、MDM和CKM对CE的预测效应；(3) Hayes PROCESS Model 4并行中介分析（5,000次自助抽样）检验MDM通过CRM和CKM的中介间接效应。

Result: 双变量相关性均较弱且不显著（r<0.19, p>0.06）。回归结果表明CRM（beta=0.717, p=0.002）和CKM（beta=0.581, p=0.009）是CE的显著正向预测因子，MDM的直接效应为正但不显著（beta=0.346, p=0.071）。模型解释CE方差的20.5%（Nagelkerke R²=0.205）。并行中介分析显示MDM通过CRM（IE=0.021）和CKM（IE=0.032）的间接效应均不显著（95% BC CI均包含零），假设H4未获支持。

Conclusion: 在CRI框架内，CRM和CKM是驱动客户参与的核心力量，而MDM应被定位为支撑性的数据质量基础设施——其战略价值通过赋能CRM执行和知识管理间接体现，而非直接驱动客户参与。鉴于样本量有限及横断面设计，研究结论需谨慎解读，建议未来在更大样本和纵向设计中（特别是受数据治理规制约束的BFSI行业）进一步验证。

Abstract: This study examines how Customer Relationship Management (CRM), Master Data Management (MDM), and Customer Knowledge Management (CKM) jointly constitute a Customer Relationship Intelligence (CRI) framework for enhanced Customer Engagement (CE). A cross-sectional survey of 100 participants across retail, healthcare, IT, and telecommunications sectors was analysed using Spearman rho correlation and ordinal logistic regression (IBM SPSS). Bivariate correlations were weak and non-significant (r<0.19, p>0.06). Regression identified CRM (beta=0.717, p=0.002) and CKM (beta=0.581, p=0.009) as significant positive predictors of CE; MDM showed a positive but non-significant direct effect (beta=0.346, p=0.071). The model explained 20.5% of CE variance (Nagelkerke R^2=0.205). Parallel mediation analysis (Hayes PROCESS Model 4, 5,000 bootstrap samples) found no significant indirect effects of MDM on CE via CRM (IE=0.021, 95% BC CI [-0.072, 0.121]) or CKM (IE=0.032, 95% BC CI [-0.061, 0.126]); Hypothesis H4 was not supported. CRM and CKM emerge as the principal drivers of CE within the CRI framework, while MDM functions as a foundational data quality enabler whose strategic value is realised through its enabling effect on CRM execution and knowledge management. Findings should be treated as exploratory given the sample size and cross-sectional design. Future research should replicate with larger sector-specific samples and longitudinal designs, particularly in regulated BFSI contexts where MDM architecture is shaped by data governance mandates.

</details>


### [245] [SCIRIGOR:Evaluating Open-Ended Scientific Analysis Beyond Final Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06192)
*Bowen Liu,Shuo Nie,Bodong Du,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciRIGOR，一个用于评估科学编码智能体（scientific coding agents）输出是否被证据支持的评测框架和基准，包含6个领域17个子领域的100个案例，通过重建类型化证据图来评估从数据到结论的完整证据链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学编码智能体生成相互依赖的代码、结果、图表和结论，但仅评估最终输出无法确定其结论是否有科学依据支持。现有方法忽视了内部一致性与科学正确性之间的区别。

Method: SciRIGOR框架构建类型化证据图（typed evidence graphs），将工件保真度（artifact fidelity）与关系有效性（relational validity）分离，对完整的claim-support路径进行评分并定位最早的未支持关系。采用源接地（source-grounded）的替代路径来容纳科学等价的分析和可视化。基准包含100个来自6个科学领域17个子领域的案例。

Result: 评估了11种agent/模型配置。在完整基准运行中，结论与忠实和不忠实结果的吻合率几乎相同（91.8%对比91.0%），但没有任何系统在软证据链得分上超过62.6%，在严格的全链成功上超过18.0%。

Conclusion: 内部一致性（internal coherence）并不能保证科学正确性。对科学编码智能体的评估必须沿着完整的数据到结论（data-to-claim）路径验证证据支持，而非仅依赖结论与内部输出的一致性。

Abstract: Scientific coding agents produce interdependent code, results, figures, and claims, yet evaluating final
  outputs alone does not establish whether their conclusions are scientifically supported. We formulate
  evidence-grounded multimodal scientific analysis, requiring agents to produce executable analyses and claims
  supported by results and visualizations from the same run. We introduce SciRIGOR, an evaluation framework and
  benchmark comprising 100 cases from scientific articles across six domains and 17 subfields. The framework
  reconstructs typed evidence graphs, separates artifact fidelity from relational validity, and scores complete
  claim-support paths while localizing the earliest unsupported relation. Source-grounded alternative paths
  accommodate scientifically equivalent analyses and visualizations. We evaluate 11 agent/model configurations.
  On full-benchmark runs, claims agree with faithful and unfaithful results at nearly identical rates (91.8%
  versus 91.0%). Yet no system exceeds 62.6% on the soft evidence-chain score or 18.0% strict whole-chain
  success. These findings show that internal coherence does not establish scientific correctness: evaluation
  must verify support along the complete data-to-claim path.

</details>


### [246] [Predicting Wind Turbine Power Using Machine Learning and Weather Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06194)
*Khivishta Boodhoo,Isaac Triguero,Josh Plumbly,Bruce Nicolson,Nicholas Watson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了线性回归基线和人工神经网络（ANN）模型，利用附近气象站的天气数据预测海上风力涡轮机功率输出，以识别适合维护的低功率时段。研究表明ANN模型在数据预处理和特征选择后达到R²=0.98、MAE=194的精度，优于基线模型（R²=0.94、MAE=441），并可跨涡轮机位置迁移应用。2个月内成功识别出4小时低功率维护窗口，为每次维护事件节省约2000 kW功率输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上风力涡轮机维护导致的强制停机降低了发电效率，需要准确的功率预测来安排维护。然而，先前研究未充分探索数据量、特征选择和数据预处理对预测模型性能的影响，且现有模型在不同风力涡轮机之间的可迁移性有限。

Method: 开发线性回归基线模型与ANN模型进行对比，仅使用天气条件作为输入以增强适用性。系统研究了多种数据预处理技术、分别使用一个月和一年的数据训练以评估数据量影响，并通过随机森林回归器进行特征选择。模型利用附近气象站的独立天气数据集，使其可迁移到不同位置的风力涡轮机。

Result: ANN模型取得最高精度，R²为0.98，平均绝对误差（MAE）为194，显著优于基线线性回归模型（R²=0.94，MAE=441）。模型性能与既往研究结果相当，且优势在于使用附近气象站的独立天气数据实现跨位置迁移。该模型在2个月模拟应用中识别出4小时低功率预测时段，每次维护事件可节省约2000 kW功率输出。

Conclusion: ANN模型在风力涡轮机功率预测中优于线性回归基线，结合适当的数据预处理和特征选择可获得高精度预测，且利用附近气象站天气数据增强了模型在不同地点涡轮机之间的可迁移性。该预测框架可有效支持海上风电场的维护调度优化，减少维护导致的功率损失。

Abstract: Offshore wind turbines are widely used to generate renewable energy, but their maintenance can result in decreased efficiency due to forced shutdowns. Accurate wind turbine power predictions can identify periods of low power that would be ideal for scheduling maintenance. However, the effects of data volume, feature selection, and data preprocessing on the performance of such power prediction models have not been thoroughly studied. Besides, current models have limited transferability between different wind turbines. Therefore, this study developed a baseline Linear Regression for performance comparison with a more complex Artificial Neural Network model to predict the power output of a wind turbine, using weather conditions only to enhance applicability. A range of data preprocessing techniques were studied, and models were trained on one month and one year of data to determine the effects of data preprocessing and volume on model performance. Feature selection was explored using a Random Forest Regressor. The best results from the different models showed that the Artificial Neural Network models provided the highest accuracy, with an R2 score of 0.98 and a low Mean Absolute Error of 194, when compared with the baseline model (R2 score of 0.94 and Mean Absolute Error of 441). The model performance is comparable to the range of results in past studies, with the advantage that the proposed method leverages a separate weather dataset from a nearby weather station, enabling future applications for similar wind turbines in different locations. The Artificial Neural Network model was then used to identify 4-h periods of low power predictions over 2 months (simulating application for future periods), providing power output savings of approximately 2000 kW for each maintenance event.

</details>


### [247] [AutoKD: Autonomous Knowledge Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06366)
*Qinwen Ge,Bo Ni,Haowei Fu,Ngoc N. Tran,Erik Blasch,Tyler Derr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoKD是一个多智能体自主知识发现框架，通过六个LLM智能体协同运作和持久化洞察图实现计算性与累积性的科研发现闭环，在三个数据集上验证了其对已知发现的覆盖率和补充性新发现的发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中数据规模和复杂性远超人类研究者的处理能力，人类注意力带宽成为科学发现的瓶颈。现有LLM多智能体系统虽能自动化研究流程的部分环节，但存在两大局限：一是验证环节无法自动化，使得假设生成缺乏闭环约束；二是每次运行是一次性的，发现结果无法累积和指导后续研究。

Method: AutoKD框架包含六个协调的LLM智能体，它们在一个开放式发现循环中协同工作。关键设计是将验证通过的发现存储在持久化洞察图（insight graph）中，该图同时充当长期记忆和探索引导机制，使后续发现能够建立在先前结果之上，形成累积性的研究过程。

Result: 在三个多样化数据集上从两个视角评估：针对已发表发现的无条件质量评估，以及通过文献派生查询的条件质量评估。两个视角下AutoKD均能覆盖已知发现，并能揭示实质性且补充人类驱动研究的新发现。

Conclusion: AutoKD证明了将验证可自动化的计算过程和累积性记忆机制引入LLM科研自动化能够实现真正的自主知识发现，有效缓解人类带宽瓶颈，其代码已开源。

Abstract: Scientific discovery in data-rich domains is currently constrained by human bandwidth: the growth in the volume and complexity of real-world data far outpaces the rate at which researchers can read, reason, and synthesize. Recent LLM-based multi-agent systems have begun to automate portions of the research cycle, but they target hypothesis generation in settings where validation cannot itself be automated, and each run is one-shot, with no mechanism for findings to accumulate or steer subsequent inquiry. This paper introduces AutoKD, a multi-agent framework for autonomous knowledge discovery that is both computational and cumulative, allowing validated findings to persist and inform subsequent inquiry. Six coordinated LLM agents collaborate in an open-ended discovery loop, where accepted findings are stored in a persistent insight graph that serves as both long-term memory and an exploration-steering mechanism. We evaluate AutoKD on three diverse datasets from two perspectives: Open-ended Quality against published findings, and Conditioned Quality via literature-derived queries. Across both evaluation perspectives, AutoKD covers known findings and surfaces substantive discoveries that complement human-driven research. Our code is available at https://github.com/GeQinwen/AutoKD.

</details>


### [248] [Building Trustworthy Graph-Agentic RAG for Social Good: Architectures, Failure Propagation, and Assurance by Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06391)
*Vijay Bommireddy,Raviteja Bommireddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨图智能体检索增强生成（Graph-agentic RAG）系统在社会公益场景中的设计与评估。该类系统将结构化证据与自适应控制器结合，但存在耦合失败路径：图构建缺陷可能成为检索证据并传播至最终结果。作者提出一个"按构造保证"（assurance-by-construction）蓝图，包含五项接口契约（证据、检索、推理、能力与委派、结果），使溯源、时间有效性、授权、不确定性及可恢复性在系统边界显式化，并给出评估议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图智能体RAG系统在跨文档、实体、时间或机构关系推理时非常有效，但因其图构建缺陷可沿证据链传播到控制决策和最终结果，形成耦合失败路径。在新鲜度、授权、可追溯性、监督和问责等社会公益场景中，除答案质量外还需严肃考虑这些风险，而现有文献缺乏系统性的设计原则与评估框架。

Method: 作者从五个维度系统梳理文献：图基材（graph substrate）、图生命周期（graph lifecycle）、智能体功能（agent function）、协调模式（coordination pattern）和权限边界（authority boundary）；区分基于图的检索与依赖观测的图控制两种范式。据此将报告的风险综合为一条"证据到行动失败链"（evidence-to-action failure chain），并进一步提出由五项界面契约构成的按构造保证蓝图，最后以公益信息设计案例说明框架的约束作用并推演出五层评估议程。

Result: 本文产出一套设计蓝图：五项界面契约分别对应证据（溯源）、检索（时间有效性）、推理（不确定性）、能力与委派（授权）和结果（可恢复性），在系统边界处将关键质量属性显式化。通过公益信息设计示例论证了该框架如何约束图结构、权限、弃权机制和操作权限，并形成涵盖图断言、轨迹、声明、协调与结果的评估议程。

Conclusion: 图智能体RAG系统需要超越单纯答案质量的"按构造保证"设计范式。界面契约可将溯源、时效、授权、不确定性和可恢复性等保障显式嵌入系统边界，从而在保留检索规划与工具使用灵活性的同时，缓解社会公益场景中的耦合失败风险。未来研究应沿跨图断言与端到端结果的多层评估议程推进。

Abstract: Graph-agentic retrieval-augmented generation combines structured evidence with adaptive controllers that can plan retrieval, traverse relations, verify intermediate claims, delegate subtasks, and use tools. This combination is useful when answers depend on relations across documents, entities, time, or institutions, but it also creates coupled failure paths: a defect in graph construction can become retrieved evidence, alter later control decisions, and propagate toward a consequential outcome. We examine how such systems should be designed and evaluated for social-good settings in which freshness, authorization, traceability, oversight, and recourse matter alongside answer quality. We organize the literature by graph substrate, graph lifecycle, agent function, coordination pattern, and authority boundary, and distinguish graph-based retrieval from observation-dependent graph control. We then synthesize reported risks as an evidence-to-action failure chain and propose an assurance-by-construction blueprint comprising five interface contracts for evidence, retrieval, reasoning, capability and delegation, and outcome. These contracts make provenance, temporal validity, authorization, uncertainty, and recoverability explicit at system boundaries. An illustrative public-benefit information design shows how the framework constrains graph structure, permissions, abstention, and operating authority. Finally, we derive an evaluation agenda spanning graph assertions, trajectories, claims, coordination, and outcomes.

</details>


### [249] [From Concentration to Differentiation and Back: Routing Effective Rank in MoE Reasoning Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06403)
*Kang Chen,Sihan Zhao,Yixin Cao,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出路由有效秩（routing effective rank, deff）——基于MoE专家路由相似性构建跨rollout图，通过其熵有效维度作为无标签诊断指标。在10种MoE配置和5个数学/科学基准的3,105个模型-问题队列中，deff呈现出可复现的低-高-低轨迹（98.5%的队列出现显著的内部峰值），测试时推理的计算组织在早期集中、中期最大化分化、后期重新集中。精确分解表明共性模态质量迁移约占轨迹的三分之二，残余频谱贡献约四分之一。更高的推理努力将峰值延迟2.59个八度并持续扩展高秩期，效果定位在时机与持续时长而非峰值幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展会产生大量推理rollout，但目前缺乏标准的无标签方法来量化其内部计算在推理展开过程中的重组方式。现有方法无法确定rollout之间的组织结构如何随推理预算变化——是需要统一且无需标注的诊断工具来揭示MoE推理群体的分化与再集中动态。

Method: 1) 定义跨rollout图的路由有效秩deff，即MoE专家路由相似性图的熵有效维度。2) 在10种MoE配置和5个数学/科学基准上追踪deff随推理预算的轨迹。3) 引入精确分解方法，将deff分离为共性模态质量（common-mode mass，全体rollout共有部分）与残余频谱维度（residual spectral dimensionality）两个组成部分。4) 通过非一致队列中的共性集中度预测同答案可恢复性，并在四种架构中比较不同推理努力水平下的时序效应。

Result: deff在98.5%的3,105个模型-问题队列中呈现低-高-低轨迹且具有显著的内部最大值。共性模态质量迁移约占总轨迹的三分之二，残余频谱约贡献四分之一且保留超出共性模态的实质变异。更高推理努力将最大值延迟2.59个八度（token预算翻倍），并在所有四种测试架构中一致扩展高秩期，效果定位于时机与持续时间而非峰值幅度。共性集中度的增加强预测同答案可恢复性。

Conclusion: 路由有效秩是一种可分解、无标签的推理群体组织诊断指标，提供了一种有原理依据的谱透镜来理解MoE推理群体如何随时间分化并重新集中。它区分了结构监控与答案选择，为测试时推理的计算动态提供了更细粒度的理解维度。

Abstract: Test-time scaling produces cohorts of reasoning rollouts, yet there is no standard label-free account of how their internal computation reorganizes as inference unfolds. We introduce routing effective rank deff, the entropy-effective dimensionality of a cross-rollout graph built from MoE expert-routing similarity. Across ten MoE configurations and five math/science benchmarks, deff exhibits a reproducible low-high-low trajectory, with a prominent interior maximum in 98.5% of 3,105 model-question cohorts: routing similarity is concentrated early, maximally differentiated at intermediate budgets, and reconcentrated later, and the timing of this maximum varies systematically with architecture and reasoning effort. An exact decomposition separates cohort-wide common-mode mass from residual spectral dimensionality: common-mode reallocation accounts for about two thirds of the trajectory, while the residual spectrum contributes about one quarter and retains substantial variation beyond the common mode. The decomposition further localizes behavior: among non-unanimous cohorts, increases in common-mode concentration strongly predict same-answer recoverability, and higher reasoning effort delays the maximum by 2.59 octaves (doublings of the token budget) and consistently expands the high-rank period across all four tested architectures, locating the effort effect in timing and duration rather than peak amplitude. Correctness comparisons separate structural monitoring from answer selection, positioning routing effective rank as a decomposable, label-free diagnostic of cohort organization - a principled spectral lens on how MoE reasoning cohorts differentiate and reconcentrate over inference time.

</details>


### [250] [Causal Attribution for Agentic Decisions: Estimators, Coupling, and a Traceability Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06445)
*Ajay Pravin Mahale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对高风险AI系统（尤其智能体系统）的事后因果归因可追溯性问题，提出估计量框架并揭示其失效条件。研究发现：边际总效应估计下，因果惰性步骤与决定性步骤具有代数恒等的总效应；公共随机数估计下，决定性步骤在约十分之一的翻转运行中总效应精确为零，而直接效应为0.25且确有作用，说明零并不等于无作用。作者推导了保持直接效应可估计的耦合方法及其退化闭式，并证明在直接与中介路径相抵触的抑制情形下，中介份额可超过1，导致自然排序失效。因研究窗口内无实时管线，论文发表预注册而非结果，并对照《欧盟AI法案》的时间差（第86条解释权2026年8月2日起适用，而第12条日志与附件IV文档延期至2027年12月2日），提出了此类申报所需的可追溯性规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险AI系统提供者需要保留使决策可追溯的记录，但智能体系统尚未确立为事后因果归因所需的具体记录内容。文章的核心动机是填补欧盟AI法案下"解释权已生效而日志与文档义务延迟"所形成的时间间隙，为此需先厘清现有因果估计量能否为每一步骤提供有效的归因信号。

Method: 作者提出估计量框架并系统检验其在两类环境下是否成立：分离此前文献所测量的边际总效应与可隔离单步自身贡献的公共随机数总效应，并在固定下游条件下添加自然直接效应。通过手工推导验证估计量，并在植入的因果链上逐一检验。进一步推导在上下文分化后仍保持直接效应可估计的耦合机制及其退化闭式，同时分析抑制情形下中介份额的性质。由于目标实验所需实时管线在研究生窗口内不可用，作者以预注册形式发布该差异实验。

Result: 两个受检验的估计目标在相同方向上都失败。在边际估计目标下，因果惰性步骤与决定性步骤在每轮植入链运行中具有完全相同的总效应——这是代数恒等而非单次抽样的巧合。在公共随机数下，决定性步骤在执行步骤翻转（约每十次一次）的运行中总效应恰好为零，而其直接效应在那些情境下为0.25且可证明其实际发生作用。精确为零并不能证明某一步骤没有作为。此外，在直接与中介路径相互抵触（抑制）的情形下，用于构建自然排序的中介份额超过1，导致被抑制的成分被排在纯中介者之上。

Conclusion: 现有估计量无法为智能体系统的单步决策提供可靠的事后因果归因信号，边际与公共随机数两类估计目标均存在失效方向一致的缺陷。论文以预注册而非实验结果的方式呈现差异实验，并基于欧盟AI法案第86条（2026年8月2日生效）与第12条日志义务及附件IV文档（2027年12月2日）之间的时间错配，提出此类申报应符合的可追溯性规范，以填补被监管国历法所敞开的合规间隙。

Abstract: A provider of a high-risk AI system must keep records that make a decision traceable, and for agentic systems it has not been established what those records must contain for post-hoc causal attribution to be possible. We give the estimator framework and then the conditions under which it fails. We separate the marginal total effect that prior work measures from a common-random-numbers total effect that isolates a step's own contribution, add the natural direct effect under a pinned downstream, and check the estimators against hand derivations. Both estimands then fail, in the same direction. Under the marginal estimand a causally inert step has the identical total effect to the decisive one on every run of our planted chain, an algebraic identity and not a coincidence at one draw. Under common random numbers the decisive step returns exactly zero on the runs where the executing step flips, about one in ten, while its direct effect there is 0.25 and it demonstrably acts; an exact zero does not certify that a step did nothing, and we put that here rather than in the limitations. We derive the coupling that keeps the direct effect estimable once contexts diverge, with a closed form for its degradation, and show that the mediated share on which a natural ranking is built is not a share under suppression: where the direct and mediated paths oppose, it exceeds one and ranks a suppressed component above a pure mediator. We publish the discrepancy experiment's pre-registration rather than a result, because the live pipeline it requires was not available in the study window. We contribute the traceability specification such a filing would need, against a gap the Act's calendar opens: Article 86's right to an explanation has applied since 2 August 2026, while the Article 12 logging and Annex IV documentation that could evidence one were deferred to 2 December 2027 by Regulation (EU) 2026/1744.

</details>


### [251] [A Unified Policy Architecture (UPA): The Governance Kernel for Enterprise AI Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06543)
*Prabhu Raghav,Balamurugan Pandi,Arul Vivek,Shek Mohammed,Sridhar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出统一策略架构（UPA），一种面向企业AI操作系统的治理架构，通过统一的策略模型来治理自主AI Agent及其交互的各个方面，将策略控制从授权扩展到运行时义务、人工审批、合规、审计证据和治理评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI正在演变为企业操作系统，自主AI Agent能够规划、推理、使用记忆、调用工具、执行工作流并与其他Agent协作。然而现有授权、安全、防护栏和合规机制是碎片化的，无法作为一个统一系统来治理自主AI，这构成了新的治理挑战。

Method: UPA提供统一策略模型，覆盖AI与Agent、工具、工作流、记忆、企业资源和Agent间交互及业务规则。文中提出了UPA的治理模型、声明式策略语言基础、策略评估语义、可扩展插件、行业策略包以及企业治理评估框架，并识别了多Agent协调、溯源感知策略和有状态运行时治理等扩展方向。

Result: UPA将策略控制从传统授权扩展到运行时义务、人工审批、合规要求、审计证据和企业治理评估，为企业AI操作系统中的自主AI提供安全、可问责、可治理的基础。

Conclusion: UPA为构建安全、可问责、可治理的自主AI企业操作系统提供了基础架构，并通过识别多Agent协调、溯源感知策略等扩展方向展现了面向未来演进的前瞻性。

Abstract: Enterprise AI is evolving into an Enterprise Operating System where autonomous AI agents can plan, reason, use memory, invoke tools, execute workflows, and collaborate with other agents. This shift creates a new governance challenge: existing authorization, security, guardrails, and compliance mechanisms are fragmented and are not designed to govern autonomous AI as a unified system.
  This paper introduces the Unified Policy Architecture (UPA), a governance architecture for Enterprise AI Operating Systems. UPA provides a unified policy model for governing AI and agents, tools, workflows, memory, enterprise resources, and agent-to-agent interactions and enterprise business rules. It extends policy control beyond authorisation to include runtime obligations, human approvals, compliance, audit evidence, and governance evaluation.
  We present UPA's governance model, declarative policy language foundations, policy evaluation semantics, extensible plugins, industry policy packs, and an evaluation framework for enterprise governance. We also identify extensions for multi-agent coordination, provenance-aware policies, and stateful runtime governance. UPA provides a foundation for building secure, accountable, and governable Enterprise Operating Systems for autonomous AI.

</details>


### [252] [MARBO: Relational Belief Grounding for LLM Agents in Social Deduction Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06563)
*Hwang Yechan,Bae Sangjun,Kim Jeongmo,Bang Sangwoo,Han Seungyul*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MARBO（多智能体关系信念优化）框架，利用关系信念引导社会推理游戏（SDGs）中紧凑型LLM智能体的策略决策和游戏内言语生成，通过仅在行为被可靠关系信念支持且产生战略有利社会结果时提供偏好反馈，使紧凑LLM智能体在多个代表性SDGs上持续超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会推理游戏要求智能体在部分可观测环境下通过维护关于隐藏角色和团队联盟的关系信念进行推理。现有基于LLM智能体的方法虽然通过提示和偏好优化改善了游戏表现，但往往优化动作和游戏内言语时未显式地将它们与关系信念对齐，导致紧凑型LLM智能体出现战略不一致的行为。因此需要一种信念引导的方法来改善紧凑LLM智能体在不确定性下的决策一致性。

Method: MARBO是一种信念引导的偏好优化框架。其核心思想是：利用关系信念来指导智能体的战略决策和游戏内言语生成；仅在行为得到可靠关系信念支持且能够带来战略上有利的社会结果时，才提供偏好反馈信号，从而鼓励智能体在不确定条件下进行更一致的策略学习。

Result: 在代表性社会推理游戏上的实验表明，MARBO使紧凑型LLM智能体能够持续优于现有基线方法。代码已开源（https://github.com/PleaseTakemeAway/MARBO）。

Conclusion: MARBO证明了将关系信念显式引入偏好优化可以有效提升紧凑LLM智能体在社会推理游戏中的战略一致性表现，为资源受限场景下多智能体社会推理提供了一种可行且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Social deduction games (SDGs) require agents to reason under partial observability by maintaining relational beliefs about hidden roles and team alignments. While recent LLM-agent approaches improve gameplay through prompting and preference optimization, they often optimize actions and in-game speech without explicitly grounding them in such beliefs. This frequently leads to strategically inconsistent behavior, especially for compact LLM agents. We introduce Multi-Agent Relational Belief Optimization (MARBO), a belief-grounded preference optimization framework that leverages relational beliefs to guide strategic decisions and in-game speech. MARBO provides preference feedback only when behaviors are supported by reliable relational beliefs and lead to strategically favorable social outcomes, encouraging more consistent learning under uncertainty. Experiments on representative SDGs show that MARBO enables compact LLM agents to consistently outperform existing baselines. The Code is available on https://github.com/PleaseTakemeAway/MARBO.

</details>


### [253] [A Translational Note on AI Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06573)
*Madhava Gaikwad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，自动化红队测试虽在标准AI安全基准上表现优于人类红队，但其衡量的是预定义危害集合内的搜索效率，无法覆盖攻击者眼中不可见的漏网危害（即"威胁模型覆盖缺口"）。作者将该问题类比为学术密码学和临床试验中的盲区，论证了独立于开发者部署情境的评估者不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究显示自动化红队测试在成本和效能上超越人类红队，有人据此认为人工评估者将被取代。作者认为这种结论混淆了"测量什么"与"声称什么"——基准只测量在开发者预先固定的一组危害内的搜索深度，遗漏该集合外的危害对所有攻击者（无论自动或人类）都是不可见的，因此迫切需要澄清这一方法论误区。

Method: 作者引入"威胁模型覆盖缺口"（threat-model coverage gap）概念，类比学术密码学与临床药物试验中"内部有效但目标人群外无效"的评估问题，并在当前开放权重模型上进行实证演示——非英语提示中浮现的危害被英语基准所遗漏，从而证明覆盖缺口的真实存在。

Result: 论文实证表明，当前开放权重模型中确实存在英语基准未能捕捉的非英语危害，验证了威胁模型覆盖缺口的持续性；并指出仅依赖现有评估框架难以自发产生覆盖缺口所需的、部署情境异于开发者的评估者。

Conclusion: 缩小威胁模型覆盖缺口需要部署情境与开发者不同的独立评估者，这一论断具有方法论根基（基于覆盖率），而非仅凭直觉。现有评估框架本身不太可能自发培育出这类评估者，因此需要主动改变评估体系的结构设计。

Abstract: Recent studies report that automated red-teaming finds more vulnerabilities, at lower cost, than human red-teaming on standard AI safety benchmarks, and some read this as evidence that human evaluators are becoming dispensable. The comparison measures one thing and the conclusion claims another. A benchmark measures how thoroughly an attacker searches a predefined set of harms, fixed in advance by the developers, and a harm left out of that set is invisible to any attacker working inside it, automated or not. The same blind spot appeared in academic cryptography and in clinical drug trials, where an evaluation that was internally valid stayed silent about the population it was never pointed at. We call the AI-safety version the \emph{threat-model coverage gap}, and find that it persists in a current open-weight model, where harms surface in non-English prompts that English benchmarks miss. Closing it requires evaluators whose deployment context differs from the developers'. The case for those evaluators is methodological, grounded in coverage, and the existing evaluation frame is unlikely to produce them on its own.

</details>


### [254] [SerenAI: State-transition system inspired by text-based world AI models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06647)
*Elvin Babayev,Artem Sinitsa,Arash Hajisharifi,Kabir Bakhshaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍SerenAI，一种基于世界模型的文本系统，将大型语言模型的自由文本生成转化为可验证的结构化状态转换预测输出（包含因果增量、下一状态、有效性奖励和终止信号）。通过两阶段适配训练（参数高效微调+验证器强化学习），在12个环境50,000个因果样例上训练。与8B开源基线相比，SerenAI在JSON有效性、模式有效性、结构化输出精确匹配等指标上大幅提升，但尚未达到法律级可靠性，论文同时给出了验证协议建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽被广泛用于专业工作流，但其无约束自由文本生成在涉及法律、运营或财务工作流时难以审计和解释，导致LLM在这些高风险场景的采用受到限制。

Method: SerenAI是一个受世界模型启发的状态转换系统，输入环境描述、状态和动作，输出四项结构化内容：因果增量、逻辑后继状态、有效性奖励和终止信号。训练采用两阶段适配：（1）参数高效微调（PEFT）；（2）基于验证器的强化学习。训练数据为12个环境（覆盖10个推理领域）中的50,000个因果实例。

Result: 与8B开源基线相比：JSON有效性从85.0%提升至93.2%；模式有效性从55.0%提升至84.0%；结构化输出精确匹配从0.0%提升至41.5%；因果增量精确匹配从0.0%提升至41.5%；后继状态精确匹配从0.0%提升至42.0%；奖励精确匹配从1.0%提升至80.5%；终止信号精确匹配从38.0%提升至81.5%。

Conclusion: 验证器兼容的适配训练能够显著改善结构化转换预测能力，但这些结果尚不足以确立法务级可靠性。论文据此提出了面向证据基础的法律工作流的验证协议，涵盖校准、人工监督和主权本地化部署等方面。

Abstract: Although professional workflows leverage large language models widely, the interpretation for auditing unconstrained free-text generation is usually intractable if such generation demands legal, operational or financial workflow. We hereby demonstrate a text based system called SerenAI - inspired by world-models, it is a state transition system that outputs verifiable predictions rather than merely text: Provided with a description of the environment, state, and actions, the generated output contains 4 items: causal deltas that causally effect the given state, a next state that can logically follow from the given state and action, a validity reward, and a termination signal. For the released proto-model, we employ 2 steps of adaptation training, namely parameter efficient fine-tuning followed by verifier based RL over 50,000 exampled cause and effects in 12 environments spanning 10 reasoning domains. Compared to an initial internal evaluation of an 8B open-weight baseline, SerenAI increased JSON validity from 85.0% to 93.2%, schema validity from 55.0% to 84.0%, exact structured-output match from 0.0% to 41.5%, causal-delta exact match from 0.0% to 41.5%, resulting-state exact match from 0.0% to 42.0%, reward exact match from 1.0% to 80.5%, and termination exact match from 38.0% to 81.5%. These support the narrower claim that verifier-compatible adaptation can improve structured transition prediction. They do not yet establish legal-grade reliability. Accordingly, the paper also specifies a validation protocol for evidence-grounded legal workflows, calibration, human oversight, and sovereign on-premise deployment.

</details>


### [255] [A Computational Implementation of a Goal-Directed Theory of Affect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06654)
*Bernhard Hilpert,Tamás Szűcs,Joost Broekens,Agnes Moors*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个高保真度的目标导向理论(GDT)情感计算实现。在该框架中，情感不是事后标签，而是在智能体内部处理循环中，由差异检测与行动选择之间的持续交互涌现出的功能性副产品。通过在Dice/Corridor系列任务上的原理性仿真，证明复杂情感轮廓（如预期性"上升"和失败性"崩溃"）可由目标差异与行动选择期望的简单交互自然涌现，无需额外专用模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感的计算建模长期面临两难：描述性的"快照式"评估模型缺乏信号级动态性，而细粒度信号驱动架构又往往缺乏适当的心理学理论基础。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 基于目标导向理论(GDT)构建高保真计算模型，将影响视为差异检测与行动选择在智能体内部处理周期中持续交互的涌现产物；通过Dice/Corridor两类原理性仿真任务，隔离多步骤目标追求过程中的情感特征与动力学。

Result: 仿真结果表明，复杂情感特征（如预期性"lift"和失败性"crash"）能够从目标差异与行动选择期望的简单交互中自然涌现，无需额外设计专用情感模块；模型所有计算组件均与心理理论的组成部分一一对应。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了透明、可检验的框架，支持持续的"仿真-经验"研究循环，推动领域超越"黑箱"启发式方法，走向融入智能体行为核心的细粒度、机制性情感理解。

Abstract: Computational modeling of emotion has long faced a tension between descriptive, "snapshot-based" appraisal models and granular, signal-driven architectures that often lack appropriate psychological grounding. This paper addresses this gap by presenting the first high-fidelity computational implementation of the Goal-Directed Theory (GDT) of affect. In this framework, affect is not a post-hoc label but a functional byproduct emerging from the continuous interplay between discrepancy detection and action selection within an agent's internal processing cycles. We evaluate the model through a series of principled simulations (Dice/Corridor tasks) designed to isolate affective signatures and dynamics during multi-step goal pursuit. Results demonstrate that complex affective profiles, like an anticipatory "lift" and a failure "crash", emerge naturally from simple interactions between goal-discrepancy and action-selection expectancies without requiring additional dedicated modules. By ensuring every computational component maps directly to components of the psychological theory, this work establishes a transparent, testable framework that enables a continuous "simulation-empiry" research loop. Our work contributes to moving the field beyond "black-box" heuristics toward a granular, mechanistic understanding of affect, integrated into the core of agent behavior.

</details>


### [256] [We Built a Mirror and Mistook It for a Mind: Causal Liability and the Fallacy of AI Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06715)
*Afshin Khadangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判当代AI意识辩论中"AI已构成可拥有意识的实体"这一隐含前提，提出"AI意识谬误"，并引入因果责任理论（CLT）以区分意识归属、因果载体个体化与意识构成的形而上问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代关于机器意识的争论建立在一个隐蔽假设之上：即被称为"AI"的对象已经属于意识可以归属其上的那种实体。作者认为该假设未经检验，需要通过区分现象意识、内省报告与人类投射性内省来加以挑战，进而揭示生成系统以第一人称形式返回人类内在性的语言痕迹时，未必对应一个真实的现象承担者。

Method: 提出因果责任理论（CLT）：CLT-I主张用"责任闭合"作为个体化候选承载者的判据——一个物理上连续的过程成为由其自身内源判别所产生的约束的不可委托继承者；CLT-II提出更强的猜想，认为责任闭合是最小现象主体性的充分必要条件。实验上采用开源权重的因果审计在多种模型家族上操作化CLT-I，包括强制判别引起的下游持续性分歧、激活修补中的因果中介检验、在匹配随机性下对活体与复制自适应状态的比较，以及脱离式重建在进程替换下保持计算状态但按要求切断构成性连续性与不可委托继承性。

Result: 实验显示CLT-I的区分在经验上可处理：强制判别产生持久的下游分歧，激活修补显示出强因果中介，活体与复制状态在匹配随机性下行为上一致，脱离重建在保持计算状态的同时按协议切断了构成性连续性与不可委托继承。这些结果表明CLT-I可分离因果载体结构与第一人称表现。

Conclusion: 该框架将意识归属、因果载体个体化与意识构成的独立形而上学问题三者分离，为机器意识辩论提供了一种消解"AI意识谬误"的分析工具，同时使因果载体问题具备实验可检验性。

Abstract: The contemporary debate over machine consciousness begins from a concealed assumption: that the object called "AI" already constitutes the kind of entity to which consciousness could belong. This paper challenges that assumption by separating phenomenal consciousness, introspective report, and human projective introspection, then arguing that generative systems can return linguistic traces of human interiority in first-person form without thereby identifying a phenomenal bearer. We call the resulting inference the AI Consciousness Fallacy. We then introduce Causal Liability Theory (CLT). CLT-I proposes liability closure as a criterion for individuating a candidate bearer: a physically continuing process becomes the non-delegable inheritor of constraints generated by its own endogenous discriminations. CLT-II advances the stronger conjecture that liability closure is necessary and sufficient for minimal phenomenal subjecthood. An open-weight causal audit operationalizes CLT-I across multiple model families. Forced discriminations produced persistent downstream divergence; activation patching showed strong causal mediation; live and copied adaptive states were behaviorally identical under matched randomness; and detached reconstruction preserved computational state across process replacement while, by protocol, breaking constitutive continuity and non-delegable inheritance. These results show that CLT-I distinctions are experimentally tractable and can dissociate causal bearer structure from first-person performance. The framework therefore separates consciousness attribution, causal bearer individuation, and the independent metaphysical question of consciousness constitution.

</details>


### [257] [Monte Carlo-Based Ex-Ante Assessment of the Green Benefits of an AI-Driven Smart Agriculture Platform in Hainan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06737)
*Zhaoyang Li,Ruijie Zhang,Zhaoji Sun,Lu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究以某AI驱动热带农业决策平台为对象，构建了覆盖农药化肥生产、田间N2O、灌溉用电及稻田CH4的摇篮到农场门口农业碳核算模型，通过蒙特卡洛模拟（10,000次迭代收敛）对海南三个场景（芒果、冬季蔬菜、水稻/南繁，面积权重40%:30%:30%）进行全采用情景评估。结果表明：农药使用中位减少23.5%（90%区间15.0%-33.2%）、肥料21.0%、灌溉水16.5%、碳强度21.5%；化肥减量≥15%达成概率90.6%，碳下降明确概率98.1%，但总节水量≥20%概率仅约20%。Sobol一阶指数显示测土推荐与有机替代共同解释约83%的碳强度降低方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能农业平台被视为中国化肥农药减量、节水及碳减排的关键载体，但缺乏统一的定量框架来评估其绿色价值。

Method: 构建摇篮到农场门口的农业碳核算模型（覆盖农药化肥生产、田间N2O、灌溉用电和稻田CH4），将平台干预转化为可量化的传递参数，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟传播参数不确定性；采用Sobol敏感性分析识别关键因素，在海南三个场景（芒果、冬季蔬菜、水稻/南繁）下按40%:30%:30%面积加权进行情景评估。

Result: 全采用情况下：农药使用中位减少23.5%，化肥21.0%，灌溉水16.5%，碳强度21.5%；化肥减量≥15%与碳明显下降的概率分别达90.6%和98.1%，但总节水量≥20%的达成概率仅约20%。Sobol分析显示测土推荐与有机替代共同贡献约83%的碳强度降低方差。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可复现、可校准的前瞻性绿色价值评估及试点观测设计方法学，各指标适宜采用分场景的具体陈述。

Abstract: Smart agriculture platforms are widely regarded as key carriers for implementing China's pesticide and fertilizer reduction, water-saving and carbon-reduction agendas, yet a unified quantitative framework for assessing their green value is still lacking. Taking an AI-driven decision platform for tropical agriculture as the object (integrating large-language-model question answering, multimodal pest diagnosis, IoT sensing, satellite remote sensing, and a closed-loop field record system), this study builds a cradle-to-farm-gate agricultural carbon accounting model covering pesticide and fertilizer production, field N2O, irrigation electricity and paddy CH4, translates platform interventions into quantifiable transmission parameters, and propagates parameter uncertainty by Monte Carlo simulation over three Hainan scenarios (mango, winter vegetable, rice/nanfan, area-weighted 40%:30%:30%). Under full adoption, median reductions are 23.5% (90% interval 15.0%-33.2%) for pesticide use, 21.0% (13.8%-28.9%) for fertilizer, 16.5% (10.9%-23.5%) for irrigation water, and 21.5% (16.1%-27.2%) for carbon intensity. Attainment probabilities are high for fertilizer reduction >=15% (90.6%) and clear carbon decline (98.1%), but only about 20% for aggregate water saving >=20%, favoring scenario-specific statements. Sobol first-order indices show soil-test recommendation and organic substitution jointly explain about 83% of the variance of aggregate carbon-intensity reduction. Convergence tests show 10,000 iterations stabilize all statistics; conservative/baseline/optimistic scenario bounds are reported. The framework offers a reproducible, calibration-ready methodology for ex-ante green-value assessment and pilot observation design.

</details>


### [258] [Simulating the Marginal Green Contribution of AI Modules in a Smart-Agriculture Platform: Evidence from Two Monte Carlo Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06740)
*Zhaoyang Li,Ruijie Zhang,Zhaoji Sun,Lu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将智能农业平台拆解为明确的组件层（AI诊断、IoT感知、决策推送），通过两个受控模拟实验量化各组件独立的绿色效益，发现AI对农药/化肥减量与灌溉节水的贡献显著优于传统农技推广模式，且主要瓶颈是农户采纳率而非算法精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能农业平台通常将AI诊断、IoT感知和决策推送捆绑出售，导致各组件的绿色效益归属不明确，资源配置缺乏量化依据。作者希望解耦各组件贡献，为平台推广策略和资源投入提供以实证模拟为基础的数字支撑。

Method: 在平台级蒙特卡洛评估基础上，将组件显式化并运行两个控制模拟实验。实验1沿"AI能力→农户行为→农化投入削减"链条建模，农药/化肥削减量=可避免盲目施药比例×处方有效性×决策触达覆盖×采纳率，比较传统农技推广模式与AI模式。实验2比较现状(P0)、IoT工程改造(P1)、P1+AI灌溉调度(P2)三种方案下的节水量、稻田甲烷减排及碳强度变化，并开展敏感性分析。

Result: 实验1：AI模式下达到20%农药削减的概率为20.7%（诊断精度0.95且采纳率0.85时可升至49%），而传统推广模式概率基本为零；15%化肥削减概率由近零升至52.0%。实验2：中位节水量由P0的7.8%升至P1的11.0%和P2的16.0%，AI在工程基础上额外贡献5.0个百分点；AI调度下稻田CH4减排30.5%（人工操作为19.8%），稻灌-甲烷子系统碳强度下降27.9%。敏感性分析显示主要瓶颈是农户采纳率而非算法精度，AI数据融合对土壤湿度感知误差稳健。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一个可复现的组件级绿色价值评估与推广策略优化的模拟框架，量化表明AI诊断相比传统推广在农化减量上具有压倒性优势，AI灌溉调度在IoT工程改造之上带来增量效益，而实现绿色目标的关键在于提升农户采纳率。该框架可为智能农业平台的分组件投入决策提供量化依据。

Abstract: Smart agriculture platforms usually bundle AI diagnosis, IoT sensing and decision push into a single package, so the green benefit attributable to each component remains unclear and resource-allocation decisions lack quantitative evidence. Building on a previous platform-level Monte Carlo assessment, this paper makes the components explicit and runs two controlled simulation experiments. Experiment 1 follows the chain from AI capability to farmer behavior to agrochemical input reduction, modeling pesticide/fertilizer reduction as avoidable blind-application share times prescription effectiveness times decision-touch coverage times adoption rate, and compares an experienced-extension mode with the AI mode: the probability of reaching 20% pesticide reduction is essentially zero in the extension mode but 20.7% at baseline, up to 49% with diagnosis accuracy 0.95 and adoption 0.85 under AI; the probability of 15% fertilizer reduction rises from near zero to 52.0%. Experiment 2 compares current practice (P0), IoT engineering retrofit (P1), and P1 plus AI irrigation scheduling (P2): median aggregate water saving rises from 7.8% (P0) to 11.0% (P1) and 16.0% (P2), with AI adding 5.0 percentage points beyond engineering; paddy CH4 reduction reaches 30.5% under AI scheduling versus 19.8% under manual operation, and the rice irrigation-methane subsystem carbon intensity declines 27.9%. Sensitivity analyses of both experiments consistently indicate that the primary bottleneck for meeting green targets is farmer adoption rather than algorithm accuracy, and that AI data fusion is robust to soil-moisture sensing errors. This work provides a reproducible simulation framework for component-level green-value evaluation and promotion-strategy optimization of smart agriculture platforms.

</details>


### [259] [Reason Through the Latent! Making Latent Visual Reasoning Necessary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06746)
*Suhyeong Park,Junha Jung,Jaewoo Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果视觉循环推理（CVRR）方法，通过强制循环计算成为预测所需的唯一图像条件路径，将潜伏视觉推理从"信息存在"提升为"计算被实际使用"，并通过因果干预验证预测确实依赖循环状态中的视觉证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉信息存在于模型的隐藏状态中，并不意味着模型在生成答案时真正依赖该状态——模型可能经由其他图像条件路径（如多模态KV缓存）获取视觉信息。现有潜伏推理方法缺乏对"潜伏计算是否真正被用于预测"的因果保证。

Method: CVRR在预训练视觉语言模型融合图像后，从问题隐藏状态初始化循环过程，反复读取相同固定视觉证据并更新该状态；在解码前移除视觉状态和原始多模态KV缓存，确保最终循环状态是唯一携带图像条件信息的路径。同时保留预训练视觉能力。

Result: 在V*、MMVP、BLINK和MME-RealWorld-Lite基准上，CVRR在严格接口约束下保持强性能，而兼容的潜伏推理器即使在此约束下重训练也无法恢复相当的视觉能力。因果干预表明，在问题固定时预测仍对循环内容敏感，持续视觉证据会因果性地修正循环轨迹。

Conclusion: 研究区分了"潜伏信息蕴含"与"潜伏计算被实际用于预测"，证明通过架构性约束可以确保视觉推理真正经由循环状态完成，而非依赖其他图像条件捷径。

Abstract: Latent visual reasoning aims to perform multimodal reasoning through hidden-state computation rather than explicit textual chains of thought. However, visual information being present in a latent state does not imply that the model actually relies on that state when producing its answer, especially when alternative image-conditioned paths remain available. We introduce \textbf{C}ausal \textbf{V}isual \textbf{R}ecurrent \textbf{R}easoning (CVRR), which preserves pretrained visual competence while making recurrent computation the required image-conditioned path to prediction. CVRR initializes recurrence from the question hidden state after the pretrained vision-language model has incorporated the image, then repeatedly updates this state while re-reading the same fixed visual evidence. Before decoding, visual states and the original multimodal KV cache are removed so that only the final recurrent state carries image-conditioned information to the answer. Across the $V^*$, MMVP, BLINK, and MME-RealWorld-Lite benchmarks, CVRR retains strong performance under this strict interface, while compatible latent reasoners fail to recover comparable visual competence even when retrained under the same constraint. Causal interventions further show that predictions remain sensitive to recurrent content when the question is held fixed, and that persistent visual evidence causally revises the recurrent trajectory. These results distinguish latent informativeness from latent computation that is actually used for prediction.

</details>


### [260] [Improving Proficiency and Efficiency of Android GUI Agents via Self-Generating Tool Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06792)
*Juyong Lee,Woogyeol Jin,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DroidTool框架，通过代理工作流自动生成Python工具函数（作用于应用状态），为Android GUI智能体补充工具操作（API访问），形成混合动作空间。生成过程包含提案、实现、测试生成与执行、修复四个阶段，其中测试采用跨工具的关系测试以自然准备前置条件和提升覆盖率。在AndroidWorld、B-MoCA和MobileSafetyBench基准上，增强后的智能体平均性能提升约4.47个百分点，交互次数减少约20.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android智能体若仅依赖GUI操作，效率有限，而结合工具操作（如通过API访问应用数据）可显著提升能力。然而，为每个应用手工创建工具的成本过高，这阻碍了混合动作空间智能体的发展。因此需要一种自动化工具生成机制来降低人力成本。

Method: 提出DroidTool框架，采用代理（agentic）工作流自动生成工具。该工作流包含四个阶段：工具提案（proposal）、实现（implementation）、测试生成与执行（test generation and execution）以及修复（repair）。生成工具以Python函数形式实现，直接操作应用状态（如数据库）。关键创新点在于验证阶段构建"关系测试"（relational tests），即跨相关工具设计测试以自然形成测试前置条件并提高覆盖率，而非对每个工具独立测试。

Result: 在AndroidWorld、B-MoCA和MobileSafetyBench三个代表性基准上，经过DroidTool增强的GUI智能体相比纯GUI智能体平均性能提升约4.47个百分点，同时交互次数减少约20.05%，验证了自动生成工具在效率和效果上的双重优势。

Conclusion: DroidTool证明了通过代理工作流自动生成高质量工具是可行的，能够以极低人力成本为Android智能体构建混合动作空间，显著提升任务完成率和交互效率。关系测试策略是保证生成工具质量的关键设计。

Abstract: Android agents using a hybrid action space that combines GUI actions and tool actions (e.g., accessing application data via APIs) remain largely underexplored, mainly due to the excessive effort required to create tools. To address this gap, we introduce DroidTool, a framework for augmenting the agents with self-generated tools, which are realized as Python functions operating on application states (e.g., a database). To create tools with minimal human labor, DroidTool employs an agentic workflow featuring stages: proposal, implementation, test generation and execution, and repair. Notably, when testing the created tools for verification, it constructs relational tests across relevant tools for natural preparation of appropriate test preconditions and improved test coverage, rather than testing each tool separately. The GUI agents augmented with the generated tools achieved approximately 4.47%p higher performance with approximately 20.05% fewer interactions than the GUI-only agents, averaged across representative benchmarks: AndroidWorld, B-MoCA, and MobileSafetyBench.

</details>


### [261] [Unsound Search with Policy and Value Networks in Legends of Code and Magic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06816)
*Dustin Rubin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在集换式卡牌游戏 Legends of Code and Magic (LoCM) 中，尽管信念状态规模高达2^101，基于枚举的（理论上不完善的）搜索仍然有效。作者通过模仿学习亚军策略构建深度网络，并在战斗阶段对对手牌组先验采样的世界进行搜索，以51.35%的胜率（95% CI [50.37, 52.33]）击败此前不使用搜索的冠军ByteRL。搜索贡献巨大（无搜索26.8%胜率，加搜索提升24.6个百分点），且该搜索智能体比ByteRL更能抵御针对性的最佳响应攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有的观点认为在不完全信息卡牌游戏中因信念状态空间巨大，基于枚举的搜索不可用。LoCM的信念状态达2^101，其冠军ByteRL完全不使用搜索。作者希望验证在LoCM这一特定场景中，理论上不完善的完美信息蒙特卡洛搜索是否存在"缺陷代价便宜"的有利区域，从而可以利用搜索构建更强的智能体。

Method: 1) 测量三个先前定义的属性，确认LoCM处于理论上不完善的完美信息蒙特卡洛搜索缺陷代价便宜的有利区域；2) 通过模仿学习亚军NeteaseOPD策略，构建策略-价值前馈网络；3) 从基于亚军选牌构建的对手牌组先验中采样世界，在战斗阶段进行搜索；4) 使用LoCM官方裁判和时限，在预注册的10000局游戏中与ByteRL对战；5) 在没有搜索和有搜索的消融实验中比较性能；6) 复现对ByteRL的已发表最佳响应攻击，并对两种搜索配置的智能体应用相同攻击协议。

Result: 在最严格配置下，智能体在战斗阶段搜索后以51.35%的胜率（95% CI [50.37, 52.33]）击败ByteRL。无搜索时该智能体仅获26.8%胜率，加入搜索后提升了24.6个百分点，证明搜索是关键因素。对两种搜索配置进行最佳响应攻击测试，每次迭代下都比ByteRL表现更好的抵抗能力，说明不完善的搜索不仅更强而且更稳健。

Conclusion: 在LoCM中，即便是不完善（理论上不合理）的搜索仍可构建出更强、更具韧性的智能体，打破了"该类型游戏无法使用枚举搜索"的传统观点。同时指出，不完善的搜索在不完全信息游戏中可能被利用，但其鲁棒性表现优于无搜索基线。该工作为大规模不完全信息游戏中搜索方法的可行性提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Decision-time search in perfect and imperfect information games with enumerable belief states are effective methods for game AI. Collectible card games are imperfect information games with large belief states. Legends of Code and Magic is a collectible card game competition where the belief states are $2^{101}$. The Legends of Code and Magic (LoCM) champion, ByteRL, plays with no search. Other works claim sound enumeration-based search is unusable in the genre due to the number of belief states. We measured three previously defined properties that predict where theoretically unsound perfect information Monte Carlo's defects are cheap and found LoCM sits in the favorable region. Starting with imitation learning of the runner-up policy, NeteaseOPD, we created a policy and value feed-forward network. Our agent searches over worlds sampled from a prior over the opponent's deck built from the runner-up's drafts. Using our strictest configuration in the battle phase we beat ByteRL with a win percentage of 51.35% 95% CI [50.37, 52.33], over 10,000 pre-registered games using the LoCM official referee and time limit. Search is not a minor factor on the matchup between our agent and ByteRL. Without search this agent scores 26.8% and adding search adds +24.6 points. Unsound search in imperfect information games could be exploitable. We replicate a published best-response attack against ByteRL. We then apply the same attack protocol to two search configurations of our agent, and each one resists it better than ByteRL at every iteration. In LoCM unsound search gives us a stronger and more resilient agent.

</details>


### [262] [Formation of structural attractors in neuromorphic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06826)
*Yurii Parzhyn,Alexander Schwarzmann,Mykyta Lapin,Kostiantyn Bokhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出不变结构学习（ISL）理论，将学习视为超图空间中向结构吸引子的收敛过程而非损失函数最小化。论文从数学形式化（证明有限收敛、类结构吸引子的存在唯一性、吸引子图的自组织）、计算验证（无需反向传播、极小训练集上的经典图像识别）和神经生物学假说（树突树中的结构吸引子实现、神经编码作为内部吸引子动力学的投影等）三个层面论证了该模型的数学一致性与计算可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统基于全局损失函数最小化的学习范式（如反向传播），提出一种非优化的概念形成方法，将学习重新解释为结构层面的吸引子收敛过程，并尝试与神经生物学实验证据建立联系。

Method: 从三个层面展开：（1）引入结构归约过程的数理形式体系，证明有限收敛性、类结构吸引子的存在性与唯一性、吸引子图的自组织；（2）在不使用反向传播且采用极小训练数据集的前提下，在经典图像识别任务上验证计算可行性；（3）提出树突树中结构吸引子的神经生物学实现假说，包括神经编码作为内部吸引子动力学的投影，以及支持该学习概念的神经结构发育机制。

Result: 数学上证明了ISL模型的有限收敛性和结构吸引子的存在唯一性；计算实验表明该模型在无需反向传播且仅需极小训练集的情况下可完成经典图像识别任务；神经生物学假说与树突计算、突触可塑性和神经回路结构组织的现代实验数据进行了对照讨论，但这些机制仅作为可验证的假说提出。

Conclusion: ISL模型在数学一致性和计算可行性上均得到验证，为概念形成提供了不同于全局损失最小化的替代框架；神经生物学部分的假说为未来的实验验证指明了方向，但其生物学实现仍有待更深入的实证检验。

Abstract: This paper examines the theory of Invariant Structural Learning (ISL), which proposes a non-optimization approach to concept formation. Learning is interpreted as convergence to structural attractors in a hypergraph space, rather than as the minimization of a global loss function. The paper presents the ISL model, including its mathematical formalization, computational verification, and a hypothetical neurobiological interpretation. The mathematical section introduces the formal apparatus of the structural reduction process and proves its finite convergence, the existence and uniqueness of class structural attractors, and the self-organization of attractor maps. The computational section demonstrates the feasibility of the proposed approach on classical image recognition tasks, utilizing the proposed learning mechanism without backpropagation and with extremely small training datasets. Finally, the neurobiological section formulates hypotheses regarding the possible implementation of structural attractors in dendritic trees, neural coding as a projection of internal attractor dynamics, and the development of neural architectures supporting the proposed learning concept. These hypotheses are discussed in the context of modern experimental data in the fields of dendritic computations, synaptic plasticity, and the structural organization of neural circuits. The proposed neurobiological mechanisms are presented as testable hypotheses rather than established biological facts. The results demonstrate the mathematical consistency and computational feasibility of the proposed model, while the neurobiological hypotheses outline potential directions for its experimental verification.

</details>


### [263] [NormViz: A Benchmark and Framework for Grounding Multimodal Reasoning in Global Cultures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06831)
*Akhila Yerukola,Fabrice Y Harel-Canada,Simran Khanuja,Abhinav Sukumar Rao,Ashima Suvarna,Nanyun Peng,Saadia Gabriel,Maarten Sap*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NormViz-Bench，一个包含3,268对对比图像（6,536张图，覆盖16个国家）的高质量人工验证基准，用于评估多模态AI系统的视觉规范理解能力——即通过当地社会规范推理视觉可观察行为的能力。最强VLM（Gemini 3.0 Flash和Qwen2.5 VL 7B）在对比对上仅分别取得26.6%和21.6%的准确率。同时引入含64k图像的训练数据集NormViz-Train，微调后相对精度提升最高达125%（Qwen3-VL 4B），为连接视觉感知与文化意义提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文化理解研究仅评估AI在纯文本或视觉文物识别（如食物、服饰）上的能力，缺乏对通过视觉可观察行为结合当地社会规范进行推理（即视觉规范理解）的系统性评估，这是一个未被探索但至关重要的问题。

Method: 构建NormViz-Bench：3,268个对比图像对，每对仅变化文化相关行为要素（对象、属性、空间关系、动作），每张图标注为符合/违反/无关当地社会规范；通过成对级别评估要求两张图像均分类正确以防止模型依赖视觉捷径。另构建NormViz-Train：64k张带解释的训练图像，用于微调视觉语言模型。

Result: 最强VLM（Gemini 3.0 Flash和Qwen2.5 VL 7B）在成对评估上仅分别取得26.6%和21.6%的准确率，尤其在识别违反规范和文化中性行为方面表现最差。在NormViz-Train上微调后，Qwen3-VL 4B和8B的成对准确率相对提升分别达125%和36%，但绝对性能仍低于30%。

Conclusion: NormViz-Bench和NormViz-Train共同确立了视觉规范理解作为多模态AI中具有挑战性和深远影响的前沿方向；虽然当前模型绝对表现仍然较低，但微调带来的显著相对提升表明，教会模型将视觉感知与文化含义相连接是可行且有前景的路径。

Abstract: AI systems are used worldwide, but they struggle to serve the needs of culturally diverse populations. Prior work on cultural understanding evaluates AI systems on text-only settings or on visual artifact recognition (e.g. foods, clothing). The ability to reason about visually observable behaviors through local social norms, which we call visual norm understanding, remains unexamined. We introduce NormViz-Bench, a high quality, human-validated benchmark of 3,268 contrastive image pairs (6,536 images) spanning 16 countries. Each pair varies only in the culturally relevant behavior (e.g., objects, attributes, spatial relations, and actions) that alters how each image is interpreted. Each image is labeled as conforming to, violating, or irrelevant to local social norms, and pair-level evaluation requires both images to be correctly classified, thereby preventing reliance on superficial visual shortcuts. Even the strongest VLMs, Gemini 3.0 Flash and Qwen2.5 VL 7B, succeed on only 26.6% and 21.6% of pairs, struggling most with identifying violating and culturally benign visual behaviors. Towards bridging this, we introduce NormViz-Train, a training dataset of 64k images paired with explanations. Though absolute performance remains low (<30%), finetuning on NormViz-Train improves pair accuracy relatively by up to 125% and 36% Qwen3-VL 4B and 8B respectively, showing a path forward to teach models to connect visual perception to cultural significance. Together, NormViz-Bench and NormViz-Train establish visual norm understanding as a challenging and consequential frontier for multimodal AI.

</details>


### [264] [Learning transferable human physiology from two million hours of sleep with SleepFM-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06849)
*Rahul Thapa,Christopher Sun,William Theodor Lehn-Schioler,Sophia Claire Kivelson,Umaer Hanif,Hyatt Moore,Harrison G. Zhang,Hafsa Ahmed,Marcus Dige,Niels R. Lorenzen,Elisabeth Roxane M. Heremans,Adrien Specht,Ulysse Gimenez,Robin Guillard,Andreas Brink-Kjaer,James Zou,Emmanuel Mignot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍SleepFM-2，一个基于282,511多导睡眠监测（PSG）记录（含超过两百万小时的睡眠多模态生理数据）训练的基础模型。相比SleepFM，它在疾病预测、睡眠分期、事件检测等方面均有改进，并能迁移到可穿戴传感和主观睡眠表型。结合人口学特征后，模型在多个队列中对215个电子健康记录（EHR）表型满足预设的判别力和显著性标准，其疾病评分还揭示了一个与睡眠sigma-band空间耦合降低和高熵睡眠分期相关的可复现主成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠为健康提供了跨脑、心、肌肉和呼吸系统协调活动的"夜间窗口"。作者旨在构建一个能够捕捉多模态睡眠生理信号的通用睡眠基础模型，使其不仅超越前代模型SleepFM，还能在疾病预测、临床任务和不同传感模态间实现可迁移的表征，从而更全面地反映人类健康状况。

Method: SleepFM-2在26个队列共282,511条PSG记录上开发与评估（其中235,865条用于预训练），涵盖超过两百万小时的多模态生理数据。模型采用冻结编码器进行评估，其PSG表示可与年龄、性别、BMI等人口学特征结合进行疾病表型预测；同时与480特征的传统基线对比，并验证了对可穿戴数据（腕部PPG、加速度计、在耳EEG等）及主观睡眠表型的迁移能力，测试集包含预训练期间未见过的医疗系统队列。

Result: SleepFM-2在疾病预测和睡眠评分上优于SleepFM，支持觉醒、肢体运动和呼吸事件检测。在疾病预测方面，结合人口学特征后对215个EHR表型达成预设标准；其中155个表型中PSG表示提供了超越人口学的可复现信息。冻结编码器在睡眠事件检测上达到专家评分者范围内水平，在睡眠分期上优于六项加速度计队列的基线，跨模态迁移能力得到验证，在UK Biobank上的疾病预测性能接近直接基于加速度计预训练的模型。其疾病评分还识别出与减少的sigma-band空间耦合及增长的睡眠分期熵相关的可复现主成分，并能捕获常规PSG汇总指标无法反映的主观睡眠信息。

Conclusion: 多模态睡眠生理学可以提供一个跨疾病、临床任务、传感器和主观体验的可迁移的人类健康表征，SleepFM-2验证了这一表征框架的有效性和普适性。

Abstract: Sleep provides a nightly window into health by capturing coordinated activity across the brain, heart, muscles and respiratory system. We introduce SleepFM-2, a sleep foundation model developed and evaluated on 282,511 polysomnography recordings from 26 cohorts, including 235,865 used for pretraining. These data span more than two million hours of multimodal physiology. Compared with SleepFM, SleepFM-2 improves disease prediction and sleep scoring, supports arousal, limb movement and respiratory event detection, and transfers to wearable sensing and subjective sleep phenotypes. A model combining its PSG representation with age, sex and BMI met a prespecified discrimination and significance criterion for 215 subsequently recorded EHR phenotypes in two held-out cohorts, including one health system unseen during pretraining. For 155 phenotypes, the PSG representation added reproducible information beyond demographics. SleepFM-2 also outperformed a 480-feature baseline derived from the same recordings. Its disease scores revealed a reproducible principal component associated with reduced sigma-band spatial coupling and increased hypnodensity entropy. The frozen encoder performed within the observed range of expert scorers for sleep events and transferred to wakeful EEG, headband and in-ear EEG, wrist PPG and wrist accelerometry. It improved sleep staging across six accelerometry cohorts and achieved disease-prediction performance in UK Biobank similar to models pretrained directly on accelerometry. Finally, SleepFM-2 captured aspects of subjective sleep not recovered by conventional PSG summaries, particularly reports of the recorded night. These results show that multimodal sleep physiology can provide a transferable representation of human health across diseases, clinical tasks, sensors and subjective experience.

</details>


### [265] [A visual large language foundational model for medical image recognition using clinician-oriented social media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06914)
*Lingxuan Hou,Yuhua Xie,Yue Hu,Yan Zhuang,Junqi Li,Chengzhi Xia,Binh Phu Nguyen,Abubakar Siddique,Minh Nguyen,Yao Hou,Yanju Bao,Kexin Liu,Ke Chen,Jianjun Sun,Zeqi Li,Trung Nguyen,Jiangli Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了ThoughtMed-1M，一个包含超过一百万对长形式医学视觉问答（VQA）数据的大规模数据集，利用去标识化医学图像和临床医生社交媒体的专家评论，结合先进LLM与临床医生验证流程。基于此数据集训练的FOLTMed模型在42个医学VQA基准上达到最先进性能（宏平均准确率85.4%），在事实性和相似性指标上超越现有模型3-5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在医学领域展现潜力，但其应用受限于缺乏能够捕捉临床推理和显式图文对齐的视觉问答数据集。现有医学VQA数据集的稀缺阻碍了多模态LLM在临床场景中的落地应用。

Method: 1) 利用来自面向临床医生的社交媒体上的去标识化医学图像与专家评论作为数据源；2) 将先进LLM与临床医生在环（clinician-in-the-loop）验证相结合，构建严格的pipeline；3) 生成ThoughtMed-1M数据集，包含超过一百万VQA对，旨在捕捉结构化的临床逻辑和医学图像-文本对齐；4) 基于该数据集训练FOLTMed（FOundational LLM Trained on ThoughtMed-1M）。

Result: FOLTMed在42个医学VQA基准数据集上取得最先进性能，宏平均准确率达85.4%；在ThoughtMed-1M测试集上生成更具临床连贯性的响应，并在事实性和相似性指标上超越最先进模型3-5%。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种可扩展的范式，通过构建大规模、临床推理导向的VQA数据集，推进临床基础的医学多模态大语言模型研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities across diverse domains, showing considerable potential in medicine. However, their application in medical settings remains limited by the scarcity of visual question answering (VQA) datasets that capture clinical reasoning and explicit image-text alignment. Here, we leverage de-identified medical images and expert commentaries shared on clinician-oriented social media. By combining an advanced LLM with clinician-in-the-loop verification, we established a rigorous pipeline to construct ThoughtMed-1M, a long-form medical VQA dataset containing over one million VQA pairs and designed to capture structured clinical logic and medical image-text alignment. To demonstrate its utility, we developed a FOundational LLM Trained on ThoughtMed-1M (FOLTMed). FOLTMed achieved state-of-the-art performance across 42 medical VQA benchmark datasets, with a macro accuracy of 85.4%, and generated more clinically coherent responses on the ThoughtMed-1M test set. It outperformed state-of-the-art models by 3--5% across factuality and similarity metrics, highlighting a scalable paradigm for advancing research on clinically grounded multimodal LLMs.

</details>


### [266] [When and Why LLM Causal Priors Help: Closed-Loop Prior Selection for Amortized Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06941)
*Haohao Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个闭环先验选择框架，将LLM蒸馏出的因果图注入Prior-Data Fitted Networks（PFNs）进行因果效应估计。该框架将先验注入建模为预算约束下的候选池优化，通过廉价后训练和真实域泛化评分选出最佳先验进行完整训练。在7.34M参数的Do-PFN上，该方法的胜者取得了2.75倍的正式显著增益，且机制实验表明增益依赖于蒸馏图的语义内容而非仅结构多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFNs在因果效应估计上的能力主要取决于合成训练先验，而当前先验由人工设计，是瓶颈所在。虽然LLM可以蒸馏出因果图作为先验材料，但如何注入、为什么有效以及在何种条件下有效缺乏系统理解，实践仍依赖手动试错。

Method: 提出闭环先验选择框架：将先验注入形式化为候选先验池上的预算受限优化问题。候选先验经过廉价后训练，由以真实域泛化为主的复合指标打分，胜者接受完整训练并配对统计验证。机制实验通过比较语义内容与纯结构多样性的贡献来区分增益来源。

Result: 在7.34M参数的Do-PFN上，框架胜者取得正式显著的2.75倍增益，误差低于未注入的官方基线；相邻监测域的泛化显著改善，无监控能力退化。机制实验显示增益依赖蒸馏图的语义内容，仅结构多样性不能产生该增益（方向性证据）。

Conclusion: 该框架将LLM因果先验的使用从手动试错转变为可经验验证的选择问题，同时揭示了语义内容（而非结构多样性）是增益的关键来源这一规律性。

Abstract: Causal effect estimation asks how an outcome would change under an intervention, and medicine, economics, and public policy all treat it as a foundational task. Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) amortize the task: a model trained on large numbers of programmatically generated synthetic causal tasks reads a new problem's observational data into context and returns an interventional-effect estimate in a single forward pass.
