The capability of such models is largely determined by the synthetic training prior, which is currently designed by hand, a bottleneck acknowledged by both Do-PFN and CausalPFN. Large language models (LLMs) can now ``draw'' plausible causal graphs for a given domain, suggesting that LLM-distilled graphs could serve as prior material. Whether injecting such graphs helps at all, where any gain comes from, and when injection helps. Practice has so far relied on manual trial and error. We propose a \emph{closed-loop prior selection framework} that casts prior injection as a budget-constrained optimization over a candidate prior pool. Candidates undergo cheap post-training and are scored by a composite metric dominated by real-domain generalization; the winner then receives full training and paired statistical validation. On a 7.34M-parameter Do-PFN, the framework's winner attains a formally significant $2.75\times$ gain on the primary evaluation domain, and its error falls below that of the uninjected official base. Generalization on an adjacent monitoring domain improves significantly, and no monitored capability degrades. Mechanism experiments show that the gain depends on the semantic content of the distilled graph rather than its structural diversity alone does not produce it (directional evidence). With this framework and this regularity in hand, the use of LLM causal priors stops being manual trial and error and becomes an empirically verifiable selection problem.

</details>


### [267] [iBrain: A Unified Foundation Model Reading the Brain from Surface to Spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06960)
*Ying Chen,Tiou Wang,Zhifeng Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出iBrain，一个统一的神经基础模型，能够在iEEG和尖峰活动（spiking activity）两种异质侵入性神经信号上进行联合预训练。通过信号特异性编码器适配不同信号特征，以及共享的时空Transformer骨干网络建模跨通道和跨时间的依赖关系，在超过7,000小时的多模态神经记录上利用掩码信号重建和通道视角对齐进行预训练，在多个基准上超越单信号预训练基线，达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经基础模型大多针对单一的侵入性记录范式独立开发，缺乏在异质侵入性信号（如iEEG和皮层内尖峰活动）上的联合预训练探索。由于这两种信号在空间和时间尺度上各有独特的神经动力学表征，如何统一建模以支持可扩展的神经表征学习是一个尚未解决的问题。

Method: iBrain采用双模块架构：一是针对不同信号特征设计的信号特异性编码器（signal-specific encoders），用于适配iEEG和尖峰活动的不同特性；二是共享的时空Transformer骨干网络，用于建模跨记录通道和时间维度的依赖关系。预训练策略包括掩码信号重建（masked signal reconstruction）和通道视角对齐（channel-view alignment），在超过7,000小时的异质神经记录数据上进行联合训练。

Result: iBrain在多个基准测试上一致优于单信号预训练基线，达到最先进性能。进一步的实验表明，iBrain在不同记录设置下展现出良好的可迁移性和数据效率，验证了在异质侵入性神经记录上联合预训练的有效性。

Conclusion: 异质侵入性神经信号的联合预训练能够支持可扩展的神经建模，并在不同记录设置和下游任务上产生可迁移的表征，为构建统一的神经基础模型提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Invasive neural recordings provide high-fidelity measurements of brain activity, with signals such as intracranial EEG (iEEG) and intracortical spiking activity capturing neural dynamics at different spatial and temporal scales. Yet existing neural foundation models have largely been developed independently for different invasive recording paradigms, leaving joint pretraining across heterogeneous invasive signals underexplored. In this work, we introduce iBrain, a unified foundation model that jointly learns from iEEG and spiking activity. iBrain employs signal-specific encoders to accommodate their distinct signal characteristics and a shared spatiotemporal Transformer backbone to model dependencies across recording channels and time. We pretrain iBrain on over 7,000 hours of heterogeneous neural recordings using masked signal reconstruction and channel-view alignment, promoting contextual modeling of neural dynamics and robustness across different channels. iBrain consistently outperforms single-signal pretraining baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks. Further experiments demonstrate that iBrain exhibits transferability and data efficiency across diverse recording settings. These results highlight the potential of joint pretraining on heterogeneous invasive neural recordings to support scalable neural modeling and transferable representations across recording settings and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [268] [SSP-DMGTimeNet: Physics-Constrained Learning for Spatiotemporal Trajectory Prediction of Vehicle Platoons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06961)
*Yuhang Wang,Kailang Ma,Zirui Li,Mingfeng Fan,Kitae Jang,Changju Lee,Heye Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SSP-DMGTimeNet，一种物理约束的车辆队列时空轨迹预测框架，通过传播延迟感知的因果注意力机制和时域/频域弦稳定性损失，在保持轨迹预测精度的同时确保扰动沿车队传播的真实性与稳定性。在HighD、NGSIM US-101和I-80数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跟车预测方法主要优化轨迹精度，很少考虑预测扰动是否沿车队真实传播（即弦不稳定性问题），导致预测轨迹虽准确但传播特性失真。作者希望同时兼顾轨迹预测准确性与扰动传播稳定性。

Method: 1) 多尺度时间表示与跨车交互特征融合以捕获复杂时变车队动力学；2) 提出传播延迟感知的因果注意力机制，学习相邻车辆间响应延迟并沿队列累积，建模上游到下游的扰动传播；3) 引入时域与频域的弦稳定性损失，在训练期间抑制相邻车辆及任意子车队的扰动放大。整体框架属于物理约束的学习方法。

Result: 在HighD数据集上，五车队列的不稳定窗口率为0.65%，真实激励子集上最大头尾放大系数为0.898；零样本迁移到NGSIM US-101和I-80时，速度MAE分别为1.316 m/s和1.252 m/s，不稳定窗口率分别为3.90%和4.10%。轨迹预测性能保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 将车队层级物理约束（弦稳定性）引入轨迹预测学习框架，可以有效平衡预测精度与扰动传播稳定性，验证了物理约束在智能交通预测中的价值。

Abstract: Existing car-following prediction methods mainly optimize trajectory accuracy, while rarely considering whether predicted disturbances propagate realistically along a vehicle platoon. This limitation may lead to accurate but string-unstable predictions. We propose SSP-DMGTimeNet, a physics-constrained learning framework for spatiotemporal trajectory prediction of vehicle platoons. The model combines multi-scale temporal representations with cross-vehicle interaction features to capture complex and time-varying platoon dynamics. A propagation-delay-aware causal attention mechanism explicitly models upstream-to-downstream disturbance propagation by learning response delays between adjacent vehicles and accumulating them along the platoon. In addition, time- and frequency-domain string-stability losses relieve disturbance amplification across both adjacent vehicles and arbitrary sub-platoons during training. Experiments on HighD show that SSP-DMGTimeNet achieves an unstable-window rate of 0.65\% for five-vehicle platoons and a maximum head-to-tail amplification of 0.898 on the ground-truth excitation subset, while maintaining competitive trajectory prediction performance. In zero-shot evaluation on NGSIM US-101 and I-80, the model achieves velocity MAEs of 1.316~m/s and 1.252~m/s, with unstable-window rates of 3.90\% and 4.10\%, respectively. These results demonstrate that incorporating platoon-level physical constraints can effectively balance trajectory prediction accuracy and disturbance propagation stability.

</details>


### [269] [RedKnot-MLA: Multi-Head Offline-Online Reuse for DeepSeek-V4 Long-Context Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07008)
*Yang Liu,Zhaokai Luo,Huayi Jin,Ruozhou He,Chenchen Hong,Mingxiao Ma,Biao Zhang,Zhiyong Wang,Boyu Wang,Guanjie Chen,Yifei Liu,Tao Xie,Junhao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍DeepSeek-V4中实现RedKnot包头感知复用原理的系统：通过MLA-Off与MLA-Online双路径拆分，文档离线在位置零处理并保留Local头贡献，服务时通过RoPE重定位恢复请求位置并重算Global头与受保护的Local token行，两者合并后再经单一共享输出投影，全程不分隔打包的MLA潜在表示。Flash配置在37层/56-8 Local-Global分区下实现75.29%的逻辑头行分析上限，Pro-0813配置在55层/112-16下达78.89%。冻结Flash运行点显示热工件TTFT加速2.02-3.84倍，256K归档三数据集研究聚合F1提升+3.24个百分点、EM提升+4.16点、主算子算术节省78.7-79.5%（一个数据集F1下降2.81点）；另有未经完整追踪记录的约2.0x QPS初步测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头潜在注意力（MLA）通过单一打包的潜在KV流暴露多个逻辑查询头，这种表示虽然内存高效，但移除了传统逐头缓存复用所依赖的物理每头缓存边界。系统需要在保持MLA内存效率的同时，恢复头感知的缓存复用能力。

Method: 每个不可变文档在离线状态下于规范位置零进行预处理，经过认证的Local头贡献保留为MLA-Off。服务时，查询侧RoPE重定位恢复文档的请求位置，小规模的Global头集合与受保护的Local token行以MLA-Online方式重算，两条路径在单一共享输出投影前合并。打包的MLA潜在表示从不被分割。系统还包含因子分解、位置修复、token行闭包、稀疏MoE支持与TP8集成。

Result: DeepSeek-V4-Flash使用37个可复用层与56/8的Local/Global分区，达到75.29%的分析逻辑头行上限；Pro-0813配置使用55层与112/16头，达到78.89%。冻结Flash运行点在热工件TTFT上加速2.02-3.84倍。256K归档三数据集研究显示聚合F1变化+3.24个百分点、EM变化+4.16点、分析主算子算术节省78.7-79.5%，但一个数据集F1下降2.81点。另有作者报告的约2.0x QPS初步测量因未包含原始并发追踪而被标记为初步证据而非归档证据。

Conclusion: 系统在保持打包MLA潜在表示完整的前提下实现头感知复用，并通过双路径设计在高加速率与可接受质量变化之间取得平衡。归档验证中聚合质量指标改善，节省显著，但部分数据集出现退化；单独的QPS报告因缺乏原始追踪仅作初步参考。论文阐明了因子分解、位置修复与token行闭包等关键机制及测量边界。

Abstract: Multi-head latent attention (MLA) exposes many logical query heads through one packed latent KV stream. This representation is memory efficient, but it removes the physical per-head cache boundary assumed by conventional head-wise reuse. We present our system, a DeepSeek-V4 realization of RedKnot's head-aware reuse principle. Each immutable document is processed offline at canonical position zero; certified Local-head contributions are retained as MLA-Off. At serving time, query-side RoPE relocation restores the document's request position, a small Global-head set and protected Local token rows are recomputed as MLA-Online, and the two paths are merged before a single shared output projection. The packed MLA latent is never split. DeepSeek-V4-Flash uses 37 reusable layers and a 56/8 Local/Global partition, giving a 75.29% analytic logical head-row ceiling; the Pro-0813 profile uses 55 layers and 112/16 heads, giving 78.89%. Frozen Flash operating points show hot-artifact TTFT speedups of 2.02-3.84x. At 256K, the archived three-dataset study reports an aggregate F1 change of +3.24 percentage points, an EM change of +4.16 points, and a 78.7-79.5% analytic major-operator arithmetic saving, while one dataset decreases by 2.81 F1 points. A separate author-reported 256K hot-artifact QPS measurement is approximately 2.0x; because its raw concurrency trace is not included in this bundle, we mark it as preliminary rather than archived evidence. We describe the factorization, position repair, token-row closure, sparse-MoE support, TP8 integration, and the measurement boundaries needed to interpret these results.

</details>


### [270] [Beyond One-Shot Expansion: Contrastive Evidence Exploration for Multi-Hop Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07050)
*JungMin Yun,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的迭代式多跳检索框架，通过证据条件化探索、段落特定对比细化与覆盖率感知排序，提升多跳问答中的检索质量与下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索方法依赖单一检索意图或单次查询扩展，难以适应多跳QA中新检索证据的动态更新，且容易引入噪声或冗余的检索信号。

Method: 提出一个无需训练的（training-free）多跳检索框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 离线阶段构建段落特定的对比特性（passage-specific contrastive facets），通过与语义相似邻居的对比提供细粒度区分信号；(2) 推理阶段迭代检索证据，生成针对未解决信息需求的探针（probes）；(3) 通过对比特性细化候选相关性，并选择互补性段落以整体覆盖多样化的证据寻求意图（coverage-aware final ranking）。

Result: 在MuSiQue、HotpotQA和2WikiMultihopQA三个多跳QA基准上，所提方法在检索质量和下游问答性能上均持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过迭代式证据条件探索与对比式细化，无需额外训练即可有效应对多跳检索中证据链逐步展开的需求，验证了该框架在多跳QA场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) critically depends on retrieving the evidence necessary for effective reasoning. However, this remains particularly challenging in multi-hop question answering (QA), where supporting passages are often linked through intermediate entities and relations that must be progressively uncovered. Existing retrieval approaches typically rely on a single retrieval intent or one-shot query expansion, limiting their ability to adapt to newly retrieved evidence and potentially introducing noisy or redundant retrieval signals. To address these limitations, we propose a training-free multi-hop retrieval framework that integrates evidence-conditioned exploration, passage-specific contrastive refinement, and coverage-aware final ranking. During offline indexing, the framework constructs passage-specific contrastive facets that characterize each passage relative to its semantically similar neighbors, providing fine-grained signals to distinguish closely related candidates. At inference time, the framework iteratively retrieves evidence, generates probes targeting unresolved information needs, refines candidate relevance using the contrastive facets, and selects a complementary set of passages that collectively cover diverse evidence-seeking intents. Experiments on MuSiQue, HotpotQA, and 2WikiMultihopQA demonstrate consistent improvements in retrieval quality and downstream QA performance over baselines.

</details>


### [271] [VST: Verifiable Structured Transport for Auditable Agent-to-Agent Alpha Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07065)
*Yuqi Li,Siyuan Liu,Bingjun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM多智能体系统中A2A（agent-to-agent）阿尔法发现效率低的问题，提出将智能体间自由文本通信重构为"类型化、因果可寻址、单播记录"的结构化协议，形成可回放的因果轨迹，并在此基础上引入单一预测器（四个类型化头）加"验证-跳跃"事务控制器。结构本身是核心贡献（提供可审计性、确定性回放、防泄漏），而非提升精度——消融实验表明同等信息的自由文本通道达到相同预测命中率。在CSI 1000样本外数据上，该方法是八种方法中唯一在因子层面保持正的中位年化收益和Sharpe的方法，但相对基准超额收益仍为负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在当代LLM多智能体系统中，Mining与Evaluation两类智能体间的协作依赖自由格式自然语言消息进行迭代反馈，这种通信缺乏稳定契约、无法回放重放，缺乏因果可追溯性和可审计性，拖慢了A2A阿尔法发现的过程。

Method: 将传统自由文本通信重构为结构化A2A协议，通信由类型化、因果可寻址的单播记录构成，提交的流形成因果轨迹。在该轨迹上，一个具有四个类型化头的单一预测器预测若干周期后两个miner将累积到的指导；随后一个事务性的"验证-跳跃"控制器仅在通过四级门控时才提交多周期推测性结果，否则回滚到确切的前一状态。

Result: 在CSI 1000样本外holdout上，该方法是八种方法（七个基线+本文方法）中唯一在因子层面维持正的中位年化收益和Sharpe的；开发期选出的Top-20组合在优化期内的一个划分上达到0.71的中位holdout Sharpe。但所有方法（包括本文）相对基准的中位超额收益仍为负。消融显示同等信息的自由文本通道预测命中率相同，说明类型化不提升精度。

Conclusion: 结构（type化协议）是使能性贡献，其价值在于可审计性、确定性回放和结构性防泄漏，而非预测精度。研究明确承认局限：单次运行、描述性报告、未计成本，且未将leap机制的效果与继承的搜索底层分离，留待未来工作。

Abstract: Agent-to-agent (A2A) alpha discovery is slowed by repeated feedback cycles between mining and evaluation agents, whose hand-offs, in contemporary LLM multi-agent systems, are free-form natural-language messages that carry no stable contract and cannot be replayed. We first restructure this communication as a structured agent-to-agent protocol of \emph{typed, causally addressable, unicast records}, so that the committed stream forms a causal trajectory. On that trajectory a single predictor with four typed heads forecasts the accumulated guidance the two miners would receive several cycles ahead; a transactional verify--leap controller then commits a multi-cycle speculative outcome only when it passes a four-level gate, and otherwise rolls back to the exact prior state. Structure is the enabling contribution, and its value is not accuracy. A controlled ablation shows an equal-information free-text channel reaches the same predictor hit rate. What typing provides is a state that can be schema-checked, replayed deterministically, and prevented by construction from leaking a forecast to an evaluator: auditability by construction, not an empirically stress-tested guarantee. On a CSI~1000 out-of-sample holdout, our single run is the only one among eight methods (seven baselines and ours) to hold a positive median annualized return and Sharpe at the factor level, though the median return \emph{in excess} of the benchmark stays negative for every method including ours; its development-selected top-20 portfolios reach a $0.71$ median holdout Sharpe, selected on a split inside the optimization horizon. We report these single-run results descriptively, gross of costs, and are explicit about their limits throughout; in particular we do not isolate the effect of the leap machinery from the inherited search substrate, which we leave to future work.

</details>


### [272] [A Hierarchical Consistency Framework for Auditing Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07075)
*Ramon Gonzalez,Antonio Diaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出层次一致性框架（HCF），一种后验的、模型无关的RAG审计方法，从知识语料库、最终检索上下文和生成答案三个层次检测与诊断RAG过程中的矛盾证据，并通过可控实验证明三个诊断层次可以解耦，能够在答案匹配真实答案的情况下识别出检索证据中的直接矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG评测仅关注最终答案是否正确，但一个答案即使与参考答案匹配，其生成所依赖的上下文中可能包含直接矛盾，这些争议性证据在仅看答案的评测和检索相关性评分中不可见，因此需要对RAG全过程进行更细粒度的审计。

Method: HCF是一种后验的、模型无关的审计框架，包含三个诊断层次：（1）知识语料库层面：将语料库冲突表示为具有来源链接的原子事实，识别负责的文档；（2）最终检索上下文层面：评估检索上下文中支持与矛盾的证据；（3）生成答案层面：输出答案一致性评分（ACS），并附带支持性与矛盾性上下文语句的解释。在五个领域100个查询-语料库实例的可控语料库上评估，并由人工评估者将每个生成回复与提供的真实回复进行比较。

Result: 三个诊断层次可以解耦：具有最高平均检索相似度的语料库反而具有最低的平均ACS；而结构退化（受损）的语料库在语料库层面表现更差，但在答案层面表现更好。最重要的是，在多个案例中，即使答案与真实答案匹配，HCF仍能识别出检索证据中的直接矛盾。

Conclusion: HCF并不认证事实真理性，它的价值在于使支持和质疑某个答案的证据可被检查、可被归因，从而为RAG系统提供超越传统答案匹配评测的更全面的证据级审计能力。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is commonly evaluated by whether the final answer is correct. That test is insufficient: an answer can match its reference while the context that produced it contains a direct contradiction, leaving the contested evidence invisible to answer-only review and retrieval relevance scores. This paper presents the Hierarchical Consistency Framework (HCF), a post-hoc, model-agnostic audit of three distinct levels of a RAG process: the knowledge corpus, the final retrieved context, and the generated answer. HCF represents corpus conflicts as source-linked atomic facts, thereby identifying the documents responsible, and returns each Answer Consistency Score (ACS) with an explanation of supporting and contradictory contextual statements. We evaluate HCF on several controlled corpora spanning five domains and 100 query-corpus instances. A human evaluator compares every generated response with its supplied ground-truth response. The results show that the three diagnostic levels can dissociate: the corpus with the highest mean retrieval similarity has the lowest mean ACS, while a structurally degraded corpus performs worse at corpus level but better at answer level. Most importantly, HCF identifies contradictory retrieved evidence in several cases where the answer still matches the ground truth. HCF does not certify factual truth; it makes the evidence supporting and challenging an answer inspectable and attributable.

</details>


### [273] [Risk Is Not Review Value: Wrong-Answer Exposure Under Bounded Review Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07095)
*SangJin Park,Myungsub Choi,Jineok Kim,Minseung Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在有限审查预算下，LLM助手的答案审查应优先考虑"暴露减少"而非单纯的风险排序。通过结合错误可能性、干预可修正性、影响力和成本四个因素，设计审查队列的排序方法，并引入WAER和PRRE两个评估指标。在TAT-QA/SciFact基准上，该方法在保持WAER不变的同时将PRRE从0.881降至0.716。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM助手生成的答案数量远超人类审查能力，但现有评估方法只判断答案是否正确、是否有依据或置信度高低，未考虑有限审查预算下"先检查哪个答案"的优先级问题。同时仅靠风险排序不够，因为高风险答案可能难以修复，而中等风险答案可能可以直接通过已有证据修正，因此需要更全面的审查优先级模型。

Method: 将审查优先级建模为"暴露减少"问题，提出审查价值函数，综合四个维度：估计错误率（estimated wrongness）、干预可及性（intervention affordance，即答案是否容易修复）、影响力（impact）和成本（cost）。同时定义两个评估指标：WAER（错误答案未审查率，衡量审查队列中未被覆盖的错误答案比例）和PRRE（修复后残余暴露率），其中PRRE使用基准支撑的确定性修复规则计算，不用与排序阶段相同的数值分数，避免评估偏差。

Result: 在包含720个条目的TAT-QA/SciFact压力测试基准上，审查价值排序方法在20%预算下保持答案级WAER几乎不变（0.605对比0.600），但将PRRE从0.881显著降至0.716，表明该方法能更有效地利用有限审查能力减少用户实际接触的错误答案。

Conclusion: 可信任的LLM评估不应只衡量错误检测能力，还应衡量有限审查容量如何降低暴露给用户的错误答案。审查优先级需要考虑答案的可修复性而不能仅依赖风险排序，PRRE为审查系统提供了更有意义的评估目标。

Abstract: LLM assistants often produce more answers than humans can review before users see them. Most evaluations ask whether an answer is wrong, unsupported, or low-confidence. Bounded review budgets instead ask which answers should be checked first under a fixed review budget. Risk alone is not enough: a high-risk answer may be hard to repair, while a moderately risky answer may be directly correctable from available evidence. For generated-answer evaluation, we model review prioritization as exposure reduction, where review value combines estimated wrongness, intervention affordance, impact, and cost. We evaluate review queues with Wrong-Answer Exposure Ratio (WAER), the fraction of wrong answers left unreviewed, and post-repair residual exposure (PRRE), the fraction still exposed after deterministic benchmark-supported repairs. PRRE uses repairability rules that do not numerically reuse the affordance scores used for ranking. On a 720-item TAT-QA/SciFact stress benchmark, review-value ranking keeps answer-level WAER nearly unchanged at 20% budget (0.605 vs. 0.600) but lowers PRRE from 0.881 to 0.716. These results show that trustworthy LLM evaluation should measure not only error detection, but also how limited review capacity reduces exposed wrong answers.

</details>


### [274] [Beyond Sparse Rewards: A New Benchmark and Structure-Aware Graph Alignment for Micro-Drama Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07107)
*Yixin Qin,Shi-Zhe Chen,Zhiqi Yu,Siyuan Cheng,Tao Cheng,Jinwen Luo,Zheng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出M-Drama，首个大规模中英双语微短剧理解基准（含9,138个片段、超35K实例），并设计了SAGA（结构感知图对齐）奖励函数，将叙事建模为异构图，通过解耦语义三元组与结构时序匹配提供稠密奖励，在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微短剧具有超短时长和高密度情节的特点，传统视频理解基准无法应对其独特挑战；此外，现有强化学习奖励指标存在稀疏、表面的问题，难以捕捉复杂的人物身份与时序结构。

Method: 1) 构建M-Drama双语大规模基准，涵盖超过35K实例与9,138个视频片段；2) 提出SAGA（Structure-Aware Graph Alignment）奖励函数，将叙事建模为异构图，通过解耦的语义三元组匹配与结构时序匹配计算稠密且严格的奖励信号。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上的大量实验表明，SAGA在开放式回答准确率和摘要质量上显著优于现有基线，同时保持具有竞争力的域外泛化性能。

Conclusion: SAGA与M-Drama共同填补了微短剧视频理解基准缺失与奖励信号稀疏两大空白，证明了图匹配式稠密奖励在复杂叙事视频理解中的有效性。

Abstract: Micro-dramas, characterized by ultra-short durations and hyper-dense storylines, pose unique challenges for video understanding that conventional benchmarks fail to address. To bridge this gap, we introduce M-Drama, the first large-scale bilingual benchmark for micro-drama comprehension, featuring over 35K instances across 9,138 clips. Furthermore, while reinforcement learning can enhance VLMs on complex narratives, existing reward metrics often suffer from sparse and superficial signals, failing to capture intricate character identities and temporal structures. We propose SAGA (Structure-Aware Graph Alignment), a novel graph-matching reward function that models narratives as heterogeneous graphs. SAGA computes dense, rigorous rewards via decoupled semantic triplet and structural temporal matching. Extensive experiments on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct demonstrate that SAGA outperforms existing baselines, delivering substantial improvements in open-ended accuracy and summary quality, while maintaining competitive out-of-domain generalization. Code is available at https://github.com/qyx1121/MDrama_SAGA.

</details>


### [275] [EEG-Driven Decoding Framework for Passenger Hazard Perception in Highly Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07128)
*Yingkai Yang,Ashton Yu Xuan Tan,Bowen Li,Xiaorong Gao,Sifa Zheng,Jianqiang Wang,Xinyu Gu,Yang Zhao,Yuxin Zhang,Sharon X. Huang,Tania Stathaki,Jun Li,Hong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于脑电图（EEG）的脑机接口（BCI）框架，用于解码自动驾驶车辆（AV）乘客的神经信号，实现风险预测（RP）和危险识别（DI），以乘客认知信号作为辅助监督提升AV的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆的风险评估仍是核心挑战，现有的自动化技术存在客观与主观安全性的局限。乘客的认知信号作为一种非侵入性的辅助信号，无需主动人工干预即可提升主客观安全性，但目前缺乏将乘客作为真实使用场景主体的建模与解码研究。

Method: 提出了三个核心组件：乘客认知模型（PCM）用于匹配真实乘车场景的认知过程；风险感知序列标注（RSL）用于改进危险识别标注；乘客EEG解码策略（PEDS）集成一个3D卷积循环神经网络（3D-CRNN）进行联合EEG解码。实验涵盖事件级分析、跨会话和跨主体的泛化评估。

Result: 3D-CRNN在风险预测中达到95.3%±2.7%的平衡准确率（BA），在危险识别中通过RSL将单主体BA从80.9%±3.9%提升至85.0%±3.2%。跨会话DI达到77.0%±5.3% BA，跨主体中已知主体77.4%±1.1% BA，未知主体64.9%±8.5% BA，展示了良好的泛化与迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了面向AV乘客风险感知的EEG解码框架，证明乘客认知信号可作为AV决策制定和预期功能安全（SOTIF）支持的辅助监督信号。

Abstract: Reliable risk assessment remains a central challenge for Autonomous Vehicles (AVs). Despite advances in automation, passenger cognition provides a non-intrusive auxiliary signal that improves both objective and perceived safety without requiring active human intervention. We introduce an Electroencephalogram (EEG)-based Brain-Computer Interface (BCI) that decodes passenger neural responses for both Risk Prediction (RP) and Danger Identification (DI), explicitly modeling humans as passengers to match real-world AV use. To achieve this, we propose the Passenger Cognitive Model (PCM), Risk-aware Sequential Labeling (RSL), and the Passenger EEG Decoding Strategy (PEDS), which integrates a 3D Convolutional Recurrent Neural Network (3D-CRNN) model for joint EEG decoding. Experimental results show that 3D-CRNN achieves a Balanced Accuracy (BA) of $95.3\% \pm 2.7\%$ in RP and improves single-subject DI from $80.9\% \pm 3.9\%$ to $85.0\% \pm 3.2\%$ with RSL. Event-wise analyses further show that 3D-CRNN consistently outperforms other models across different event types in RP and DI. In generalization experiments, 3D-CRNN achieves $77.0\% \pm 5.3\%$ BA in cross-session DI and $77.4\% \pm 1.1\%$ BA on seen subjects in cross-subject evaluation, while maintaining a $64.9\% \pm 8.5\%$ BA on unseen subjects, demonstrating promising generalizability and transferability across both intra-subject and inter-subject variability. These findings establish an Electroencephalogram (EEG) decoding framework for AV passenger hazard perception and suggest that passenger cognitive signals can provide auxiliary supervision for future AV decision-making and Safety of the Intended Functionality (SOTIF) support.

</details>


### [276] [Encoded Early, Used Late: Where Transformers Begin to Act on an Inferred Partner's Expertise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07139)
*Mika Okamoto,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过ExpertCollab语料（多轮研究规划对话，包含四个专业水平的人设）发现，对话伙伴的专业水平这一"推断关系属性"在Transformer网络的浅层即可线性解码，但在网络中点之前就已降至接近随机水平。反事实修补实验表明，在峰值解码层注入专业水平差异几乎不影响后期层的固定读出器，而在网络中点之后注入则几乎完全传播，差距超过一个数量级。这表明推断属性在因果上尚未激活之前就已得到表征，界定了任何针对伙伴条件行为的读出或操控必须介入的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，直接陈述在输入中的属性会在残差流中提前可解码（早于其影响输出的深度）。本文提出新问题：对于需要在对话中逐步推断的属性（如对话伙伴的专业水平），这一"可读性-使用性之间的鸿沟"是否依然成立，从而理解模型中信息表征与因果使用的时序关系。

Method: 构建ExpertCollab语料（多轮研究规划对话，模型扮演四个专业等级的人设）；对残差流中的伙伴专业水平做线性探针测试其可解码性；使用反事实修补（counterfactual patching）在峰值解码层与网络中点之后分别注入专业水平差异，比较对固定后期层读出器的影响；辅以内容匹配随机对照和探针无关的诊断方法，并纳入静态指定控制属性作为对比基准。

Result: 伙伴专业水平在早期层达到最高可解码性，在网络中点前降至接近随机；峰值解码层的反事实注入对后期读出影响极小，而中点以后注入则几乎完全传播（差异超一个数量级）；随机控制和探针无关诊断均将该转变定位于同一早期层；静态指定的控制属性则全程保持可解码。

Conclusion: 推断出的关系属性（伙伴专业水平）在神经网络中先于其因果激活而被充分表征，即"先可读、后使用"的鸿沟对推断属性同样成立。该发现界定了对伙伴条件行为进行读出或干预性操控时必须作用的网络位置（早期层）。本工作采用单一模型与合成语料，作为初步演示。

Abstract: A transformer can make an attribute linearly decodable in its residual stream at a depth where that attribute does not yet influence the output. This gap between where information is readable and where it is used has been shown for attributes stated directly in the input. We ask whether it also holds for an attribute the model must infer gradually over a conversation, namely how expert its dialogue partner is. Using ExpertCollab, a corpus of multi-turn research-planning dialogues between model-played personas at four expertise levels, we find that partner expertise is most decodable in the early layers and falls to near chance before the midpoint of the network. Counterfactual patching shows that injecting the expertise difference at the layer of peak decodability barely changes a fixed late-layer readout, whereas the same difference injected past the midpoint propagates almost completely, a separation of more than an order of magnitude. A content-matched random control and a probe-free diagnostic place the transition at the same early layer, and a statically specified control attribute stays decodable throughout. An inferred relational attribute is therefore represented well before it becomes causally active, which bounds where any attempt to read out or steer partner-conditioned behavior must intervene. We use one model on a synthetic corpus as an initial demonstration.

</details>


### [277] [An Auditable Symbolic-RAG-Generative AI Architecture for Goal-Oriented Conversation Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07152)
*Ramon Gonzalez,Antonio Diaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Symbolic-RAG-Generative架构的目标导向对话系统，核心组件GRACE将业务目标编译为不可变目标集，并通过受约束的强化策略实现目标进展与用户体验的平衡。在房地产和清洁服务两个领域的评估中，GRACE实现了84.9%的状态转换准确率、91.6%证据精确率和89.6%证据召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标导向的对话系统需要同时满足三个挑战性需求：回答事实性问题、理解访客提供的信息、推动业务目标达成，同时又不能退化为僵硬的问卷式对话流程。现有方法难以在这三者之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出Symbolic-RAG-Generative三层架构。其中，Business Goal Compiler通过指令约束将业务意图转化为不可变目标集、归一化优先级向量、规范问题和初始状态向量。运行时，GRACE接收完整对话历史、最新访客消息、当前状态以及独立RAG组件生成的基于检索的答案，仅依据访客撰写的证据更新完成度，并选择一个受上下文调整的追问。核心策略在满足最小访客效用的约束下，最大化预期的业务进展。论文对状态、单调转换、源分离、问题调制和约束策略进行了形式化定义。

Result: 评估涵盖24个英语房地产对话和10个西班牙语专业清洁对话，共119个协议定义的访客轮次。GRACE在两个领域取得84.9%的精确状态转换准确率、91.6%的证据精确率、89.6%的证据召回率、100%的单调性和94.1%的终态准确率，在标准、多目标、RAG绕行、验证、拒绝和鲁棒性六类场景中均表现稳定。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明这种符号驱动的状态管理方法在目标导向对话系统中具有令人信服的表现，为在标准及多种复杂场景中保持一致的符号状态追踪提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Goal-oriented conversational systems must answer factual questions, understand visitor-provided information, and advance business objectives without becoming rigid questionnaires. This paper proposes a Symbolic-RAG-Generative architecture centered on the Goal-oriented Retrieval-Augmented Conversation Engine (GRACE). An instruction-constrained Business Goal Compiler transforms business intent into an immutable objective set, normalized priority vector, canonical questions, and initial state vector. At runtime, GRACE receives the complete conversation history, latest visitor message, current state, and grounded answer generated by a separate RAG component. It updates completion only from visitor-authored evidence and selects one contextually modulated follow-up. The core policy maximizes expected business progress subject to a minimum visitor-utility constraint. We formalize the state, monotonic transitions, source separation, question modulation, and constrained policy; present the reference architecture; and define an evaluation comprising 24 English real-estate and 10 Spanish professional-cleaning conversations, totaling 119 protocol-defined visitor turns. Across both domains, GRACE achieves 84.9% exact state-transition accuracy, 91.6% evidence precision, 89.6% evidence recall, 100% monotonicity, and 94.1% terminal-state accuracy. The evaluation establishes compelling symbolic-state performance across standard, multi-goal, RAG-detour, validation, refusal, and robustness scenarios.

</details>


### [278] [PhysMAS: Physics-Grounded Multi-Agent Synthesis of Compositional 4D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07174)
*Jiang Qin,Chunji Lv,Yangguang Wei,Yang Gao,Ming Liu,Lizhong Ding,Ye Yuan,Yinjie Lei,Changsheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了PhysMAS，一个基于物理的多智能体框架，用于高效、全自动且物理合理的4D高斯动态场景生成。它通过对象-部件场景智能体建立持久身份、材料推理智能体分配材质属性，并使用求解器感知的技能将材质绑定到粒子级MPM场中执行仿真，无需逐场景扩散分数反向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理驱动4D高斯方法基于MPM，但难以处理异构多部件物体和交互式多物体场景。主要挑战包括：物体级物理分配会将不同部件合并为单一材料状态；基于LLM/VLM/代理的一次性预测既不能可靠地将不同材料绑定到识别出的部件，也无法验证MPM配置的可执行性；而基于SDS的参数优化需要大量每场景分数评估和梯度反向传播，导致耗时且可能产生次优或不稳定解。

Method: PhysMAS是一个物理引导的多智能体框架，流程如下：从运动提示和四个场景视角出发，(1) 命名为Object-Part Scene Agent（对象-部件场景智能体）建立持久部件身份并调用Material Reasoning Agent（材料推理智能体）生成部件级材料参数；(2) 该框架调用solver-aware skills（求解器感知技能）将这些身份和材料绑定到逐粒子的MPM场中，并在共享域中执行所有物体的仿真；(3) 框架筛选候选前向仿真结果，最终输出动态场景。

Result: 大量实验表明，与依赖SDS的近期基于物理的4D高斯基线方法相比，PhysMAS实现了更好的语义对齐和感知物理合理性，同时所需的运行时间更少。

Conclusion: PhysMAS有效支持异构多部件物体和交互式多物体场景的4D高斯动态生成，无需逐个场景的扩散分数反向传播，兼顾了物理合理性、语义一致性和计算效率。

Abstract: Efficient, fully automatic, and physically plausible 4D Gaussian synthesis is an important goal for dynamic scene generation. Recent physics-based methods couple 3D Gaussians with the Material Point Method (MPM) to generate physically driven motion, but extending this paradigm to heterogeneous multi-part objects and interacting multi-object scenes remains challenging. Object-level physical assignment collapses distinct parts into a single material state, while one-shot predictions from large language models, vision-language models, or agents neither reliably bind different materials to identified parts nor verify that the resulting MPM configuration is executable. Score Distillation Sampling (SDS)-based parameter optimization, meanwhile, requires repeated per-scene score evaluations and gradient backpropagation, incurring lengthy optimization and potentially yielding suboptimal or unstable solutions. We therefore present PhysMAS, a physics-grounded multi-agent framework. From a motion prompt and four scene views, an Object-Part Scene Agent establishes persistent identities and calls a Material Reasoning Agent for part-wise profiles. It invokes solver-aware skills to bind these identities and profiles to per-particle MPM fields and execute all objects in a shared domain; the framework then screens candidate forward-simulation results. This supports heterogeneous multi-part and interacting multi-object scenes without per-scene diffusion-score backpropagation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, compared with recent physics-based 4D Gaussian baselines that rely on SDS, PhysMAS achieves better semantic alignment and perceived physical plausibility while requiring less runtime.

</details>


### [279] [EmoMed: An Emotionally-Aware Agent for Multimodal Medical Support with Real-Time Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07194)
*Ivan Nasonov,Nikita Glazkov,Ivan Makovetskiy,Mikhail Mozikov,Daniil Sukhorukov,Andrey Savchenko,Ilya Makarov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EmoMed是一个多模态医疗咨询代理，能够根据用户情绪状态（焦虑、困惑、紧急程度）自适应调整回应风格，同时通过双重检索机制（网络事实核查+持续更新的医学知识库API）保持临床准确性。在七个LLM上评估表明，情绪自适应回应在各项指标上优于中性基线且不牺牲临床准确性，用户研究验证了感知共情和沟通清晰度的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗咨询系统通常只关注医学内容的准确性，忽视了用户在咨询过程中的情绪状态，导致交流体验不佳。作者希望构建一个既能识别并回应用户情绪、又不会因情绪适应而损害医学事实可靠性的医疗咨询代理。

Method: EmoMed采用多模态架构处理文本和医学图像输入，从中检测情绪指标（焦虑、困惑、紧急程度），据此动态调整回应的语气、结构和详细程度。为确保事实可靠性，系统通过双重检索机制接地临床信息：基于网络的实时事实核查和使用API连接的持续更新医学知识库。评估涉及七个LLM（GPT-4/5、Qwen3、Llama 4、Gemini 2.5、Grok4、Claude3），使用LLM-as-judge、MedQA风格准确率测试、BERT Score、安全/有用性评分及多模态医学基准等综合指标，并辅以受控用户研究。

Result: 实验表明情绪自适应回应在所有评估维度上均优于中性基线，且不损害临床准确性。用户研究参与者也报告了更高的感知共情和沟通清晰度，同时维持对事实准确性的信任。

Conclusion: 情绪感知的情感适应能力可以在医疗咨询代理中实现，且能与临床准确性兼容共存。EmoMed验证了"情感智能+医学知识"相结合的设计路径的有效性，为未来更具人文关怀的医疗AI系统提供了实证基础。

Abstract: We present EmoMed - a multimodal medical consultation agent that adapts its responses based on users' emotional states while maintaining clinical accuracy. The system processes text and medical images, detects affect indicators (anxiety, confusion, urgency) from user input, and adjusts response tone, structure, and detail level accordingly. To ensure factual reliability, the agent grounds clinical information through a dual retrieval mechanism: web-based fact-checking and an API-connected, continuously updated medical knowledge base. We evaluate our approach across seven state-of-the-art language models (GPT-4/5, Qwen3, Llama 4, Gemini 2.5, Grok4, Claude3) using comprehensive metrics including LLM-as-judge assessments, MedQA style accuracy tests, BERT Score, safety/helpfulness ratings, and multimodal medical benchmarks. The results demonstrate that emotionally adaptive responses consistently outperform neutral baseline across evaluation dimensions, without compromising clinical accuracy. A controlled user study validated these findings, with participants reporting improved perceived empathy and communication clarity, while maintaining trust in factual accuracy. Source code: https://github.com/NasonovIvan/EmoMed-Agent

</details>


### [280] [Agentic Algorithm Engineering: Improving Shared-Memory Exact Minimum Cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07204)
*David A. Bader,Adil Chhabra,Ernestine Großmann,Monika Henzinger,Alexander Noe,Christian Schulz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种称为"智能体算法工程"(AAE)的新方法，通过自主大语言模型代理在现有代码库上运行算法工程周期（提出假设→实现→基准测试→保留或丢弃），用于改进最小割问题的求解算法。该方法在VieCut开源包的基础上，找到了显著优化：在真实世界k-core实例上提升1.28倍（顺序）和1.63倍（32线程），在DIMACS核心实例上提升6.26倍和127倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管作者已经手工精心调优了最小割算法，但仍有进一步优化的空间。传统的手工算法工程耗时且受限于人的经验，作者希望探索一种新的自动化方法，让AI代理能够自主执行完整的算法工程循环，从而在已高度优化的代码基础上发现人类遗漏的改进机会。

Method: 作者引入智能体算法工程（AAE）方法论：使用自主的大语言模型代理在现有代码库上运行算法工程周期。代理首先形成关于运行时瓶颈的假设，然后实现相应的改动，在固定的实例集上进行基准测试，最后根据结果决定保留或丢弃该改动。该此前算法本身结合了不精确算法以获取更好的界、依赖此界的归约、改进的数据结构以及并行收缩例程。

Result: AAE方法在已高度手工调优的算法上仍找到了显著优化。在真实世界k-core实例上，顺序版本提速1.28倍，32线程版本提速1.63倍；在DIMACS核心实例上，顺序版本提速6.26倍，并行版本（32线程）提速127倍。

Conclusion: 智能体算法工程（AAE）是一种有效的自动化算法优化方法，即使在代码已经过大量手工调优的情况下，自主LLM代理仍能发现显著的性能改进机会，展示了AI驱动的算法工程在替代和辅助人类专家方面的潜力。

Abstract: The minimum cut problem for an undirected edge-weighted graph asks us to divide its set of nodes into two blocks while minimizing the weighted sum of the cut edges. Over the last years, we engineered a range of fast algorithms for this problem. Our fastest exact algorithm uses an inexact algorithm to obtain a better bound for the problem, reductions that depend on this bound, improved data structures and parallel contraction routines. It is available in the open-source package VieCut and, on real-world instances, outperformed the previously fastest solvers by a factor of up to 2.5 sequentially and up to 12.9 when run in parallel. We improve this algorithm using agentic algorithm engineering (AAE), a methodology that we introduce here, in which autonomous large language model agents run the algorithm engineering cycle on an existing code base: they form hypotheses about where running time is lost, implement them, benchmark the result on a fixed instance set and keep or discard the change. Even though we had already tuned our algorithm by hand extensively, the agent finds significant optimizations, in particular on the DIMACS core instances: factors of 1.28 (sequential) and 1.63 (32 threads) on real-world k-cores, and 6.26 and 127 on the DIMACS core instances.

</details>


### [281] [Unraveling the Real Working Mechanism and Inherent Flaws of GAE: A Method for Interpreting Transformer Processes from an Economic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07213)
*Yongjin Cui,Xiaohui Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文属于XXAI（XAI方法本身的解释性研究）系列工作。作者指出当前XAI领域被代理指标导向的研究范式所主导，导致XAI方法本身缺乏可解释性。论文以Generic Attention-model Explainability (GAE)方法为研究对象，揭示其真实工作机制与缺陷，并提出了一种基于经济零和博弈视角、融合过程和特征两种思想的Transformer解释方法——Cumulative Asset Holdings (CAH)。该方法适用于含特殊token的模型，这是现有方法的局限场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XAI研究过度追求代理指标的优化，但这些指标本身有缺陷，且指标导向的研究范式导致了对方法本身理性与可解释性的忽视。因此需要对XAI方法本身进行解释性研究（即XXAI），以揭示主流XAI方法的真实工作机制与缺陷。

Method: 以GAE这一代表性XAI方法为研究对象，通过模型简化研究方法与加性操作分析，揭示其实际工作机制和内在缺陷。在此基础上，从经济学的零和博弈视角出发，提出Cumulative Asset Holdings (CAH)——一种融合基于过程与基于特征两种思想的Transformer解释方法。

Result: 指出GAE是一种聚焦于注意力过程的解释方法，并揭露了其工作机制中的缺陷。提出了CAH方法，能够弥补现有方法在含特殊token的模型场景下的局限性。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，可能需要对GAE相关方法及其领域应用进行重新审视或验证。所提出的模型简化研究方法和加性操作分析为其他XAI研究提供了方法论启示。

Abstract: We observe a phenomenon that current algorithmic research in the field of explainable artificial intelligence primarily pursues better performance on several proxy metrics. On the one hand, these proxy metrics themselves are more or less flawed and cannot properly measure the quality of methods. On the other hand, metric-oriented research approaches often lead to the neglect of the rationality and interpretability of the methods themselves. Explainable artificial intelligence is abbreviated as XAI. The metric-driven research paradigm has resulted in a lack of interpretability of the relevant XAI methods themselves. Accordingly, there is a need for interpretability research on XAI methods, which can be playfully referred to as XXAI. This paper is one of our works on XXAI. This paper takes Generic Attention-model Explainability (GAE), a widely influential model interpretation method , or rather, XAI method that represents an important technical route, as the research object, and explores the real working mechanism and flaws of this method as well as the technical route it represents. Based on the conclusions of this study, it may be necessary to re-examine or verify GAE-related methods and their domain applications. We argue that GAE is an interpretation method that focuses on the attention process. After pointing out the working mechanism and flaws of GAE, we propose Cumulative Asset Holdings (CAH), a more reasonable Transformer interpretation method integrating both process-based and feature-based ideas from an economic zero-sum games perspective. In addition, it is worth noting that our method is applicable to models with special tokens, where existing methods may suffer from limitations. The model simplification research method and the analysis of additive operations adopted in this study may provide inspiration for other research works in XAI.

</details>


### [282] [Distance-Aware Attention and Wall-Distance Expert Routing for Transformer-Based 3D Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07222)
*Sanghyeon Kim,Sunwoong Yang,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于壁面物理信号的Transformer条件化机制——距离感知交叉注意力（DA-CA）和表面-体积混合专家（SVMoE），分别用于3D流场预测中Transformer的检索步骤和前馈层，使其感知查询点相对于壁面的位置。两者可无缝应用于AB-UPT和Transolver-3等架构，在DrivAerML和DrivAerNet++基准上显著降低了体积压力和速度误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer代理模型将工业网格压缩为token后，检索步骤（cross-attention）和前馈层都是几何盲的，即不感知查询点相对壁面的物理位置（如边界层内与外流区域的差异）。这限制了对边界层等关键区域流场特征的建模精度，尤其是在训练数据有限的场景下。

Method: 1) DA-CA（距离感知交叉注意力）：在交叉注意力检索前，根据查询点到壁面的距离重塑体积查询token，使边界层深部与外流区域的点获得不同的几何信息。2) SVMoE（表面-体积混合专家）：将共享前馈层替换为少量专家，体积点根据壁面距离路由，表面点根据局部几何路由。两种机制均与具体架构解耦，可直接应用于AB-UPT和Transolver-3。

Result: DrivAerML上50个训练样本时：DA-CA使体积压力误差降低10.1%，DA-CA+SVMoE共同降低12.5%；DA-CA提升近壁区精度但牺牲远场精度，SVMoE恢复远场精度。体积专家无需监督即可自发聚为近壁、过渡和自由流三个条带。300个样本重训后：AB-UPT的体积压力/速度误差分别降低33.1%/18.6%，Transolver-3降低21.4%/21.3%。DrivAerNet++上的留一主体测试对未见主体类型的体积压力误差降低达14.2%。

Conclusion: 将基于壁面距离的物理先验注入Transformer的两个关键操作（检索和前馈）能显著提升3D流场预测的精度与泛化能力，且该方法与具体网络架构无关，具有通用性。

Abstract: Transformer surrogates for 3D flow prediction compress an industrial mesh into a small set of tokens from which every prediction point reads. Two operations follow: the retrieval step in which a point gathers information from the compressed representation, and the feed-forward layer that transforms what it retrieved. In current backbones both are blind to where the point sits in the flow. We condition both on wall-related physical signals. Distance-aware cross-attention (DA-CA) reshapes each volume query by its wall distance before retrieval, so that a point deep in the boundary layer draws different geometric information than one in the outer flow. Surface-volume mixture-of-experts (SVMoE) replaces the shared feed-forward layer with a small set of experts, routed by wall distance for volume points and by local geometry for surface points. Neither mechanism is tied to one architecture, so we apply both unchanged to AB-UPT and Transolver-3. On DrivAerML with 50 training cases, DA-CA reduces the volume pressure error by 10.1%, and DA-CA and SVMoE together reduce it by 12.5%; DA-CA improves the near-wall region at some cost in the far region, which SVMoE recovers, and the volume experts settle into near-wall, transition, and free-stream bands without routing supervision. Retrained on 300 cases, the conditioning improves every field quantity, reducing volume pressure and velocity errors by 33.1% and 18.6% on AB-UPT and by 21.4% and 21.3% on Transolver-3. Under Leave-One-Body-Out evaluation on DrivAerNet++, it reduces the volume pressure error on unseen body types by up to 14.2%.

</details>


### [283] [Elastic Horizon: Discovering the Effective Interaction Frontier in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07247)
*Gangyi Zhang,Junjie Meng,Letian Zhang,Wei Wu,Yang Zheng,Dong Wang,Yang Liu,Guanjun Jiang,Chongming Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"有效交互前沿假说"——存在一个动态边界，超过该边界后增加智能体交互次数（扩展horizon）带来的收益递减而成本线性增长。基于此，作者设计了Elastic Horizon，一种闭环控制器，通过成功轨迹长度的90百分位数来动态追踪该边界，在AppWorld和BFCL基准上使模型从过高或过低的初始容量都能稳定在饱和区间内，在7B和14B主干模型上取得最优成功率，并节省高达25%的每步轨迹token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩展交互horizon的方法使用开环调度策略，即单调增加交互上限直到人为设定的最大值，无法感知"何时继续扩展不再带来收益"。这导致资源浪费和在饱和点之后的无效训练，需要一种能动态检测最佳交互边界的闭环机制。

Method: 1) 提出"有效交互前沿假说"：存在一个最优交互边界，超过后增量收益递减而成本线性增长。2) 设计Elastic Horizon闭环控制器，通过监控成功轨迹（成功完成任务的episode）长度的90百分位数作为代理指标，动态调整交互horizon，使其收敛到有效交互前沿附近。3) 在AppWorld和BFCL两个基准上，先用固定horizon的扫描实验验证饱和平台的存在，再测试Elastic Horizon从欠容量和过容量两种初始化状态下的表现。

Result: 固定horizon扫描显示存在清晰的饱和平台期；Elastic Horizon能从不充足和过度的初始容量出发都将horizon稳定在饱和区间内，在7B和14B的不同backbone上均达到最优成功率，并节省最多25%的每步轨迹token开销。

Conclusion: 本文将研究范式从"如何扩展交互horizon"转向"何时停止扩展"，通过闭环控制实现训练资源的自适应分配，既保证了长程任务的性能，又避免了超出收益边界的无效成本开销。

Abstract: Scaling the interaction horizon-the maximum number of environment interactions per episode-improves LLM agents on long-horizon tasks, and curriculum-based methods that progressively expand the horizon outperform fixed-horizon alternatives. However, existing schedules are open-loop: they monotonically increase the horizon until a manually specified maximum, with no mechanism to detect when further expansion stops helping. We propose the effective interaction frontier hypothesis: a dynamic boundary beyond which additional interactions yield diminishing returns while cost grows linearly. We then introduce Elastic Horizon, a closed-loop controller that tracks this boundary via the 90th percentile of successful trajectory lengths. On AppWorld and BFCL, fixed-horizon sweeps reveal clear saturation plateaus; Elastic Horizon stabilizes the horizon inside the saturation band from both under- and over-capacity initializations, attains the best success rates across 7B and 14B backbones, and saves up to 25% of per-step trajectory tokens. Our work shifts the paradigm from how to scale interaction horizons to when to stop scaling.

</details>


### [284] [SkillAlign: Aligning Skill Interfaces for LLM-based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07255)
*Shuo Ren,Xiaomian Kang,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillAlign框架，强调技能暴露方式（接口形式）对语言模型智能体效能的影响，通过多种暴露界面（完整指令、提示、压缩摘要、工作流、无暴露）进行反事实评估，证明暴露形式显著影响任务成功率和上下文成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究聚焦于技能的获取、检索、压缩和组合，但普遍假设技能一旦被选中，其与智能体的接口（暴露方式）是固定的。作者认为这忽略了技能效用的一个关键来源：同一技能在不同暴露方式下可能产生帮助、干扰或误导的不同效果。

Method: 提出SkillAlign框架，将候选技能表示为多视角程序卡片（multi-view procedural cards），并通过备选暴露接口（完整指令、提示、压缩摘要、工作流或零暴露）进行渲染，从而实现反事实评估——固定任务、智能体和候选技能，仅变化暴露接口。

Result: 在ALFWorld和SkillsBench上的实验表明：暴露形式对任务成功率和渲染上下文成本有显著影响；紧凑的top-k暴露可优于完整库注入；在ALFWorld上进行的基于复放（replay）的策略学习分析显示，自适应暴露包含可学习的信号，但仍远未达到oracle（最优）选择水平。

Conclusion: 技能增强型智能体不仅应优化选择哪些技能，还应优化这些技能如何被呈现（presentation）。暴露方式本身是一个重要的可优化维度。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly rely on skills: reusable procedural knowledge for reasoning, tool use, and interaction. Existing work studies how skills are acquired, retrieved, compressed, or composed, but often assumes that once a skill is selected, its interface to the agent is fixed. We argue that this overlooks a key source of skill utility: the same skill can help, distract, or mislead depending on how it is exposed. We propose SkillAlign, a provider-agnostic framework that represents candidate skills as multi-view procedural cards and renders them through alternative exposure interfaces, including full instructions, hints, compressed summaries, workflows, or no exposure. This enables counterfactual evaluation where the task, agent, and candidate skills are fixed while only the exposure interface varies. Across ALFWorld and SkillsBench, we show that exposure form substantially affects task success and rendered context cost, and that compact top-k exposure can outperform full-library injection. We further conduct a replay-based policy-learning analysis on ALFWorld, showing that adaptive exposure contains learnable signal but remains far from oracle selection. Our results suggest that skill-augmented agents should optimize not only which skills to use, but also how those skills are presented.

</details>


### [285] [World Models Under Asynchronous Sensor Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07299)
*Akash Anand,Abhay Anand,Yash Vishe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究异步物理观测下的世界模型，提出使用零阶保持（zero-order-hold）最近读取值，并通过"陈旧度"（staleness）和"刷新时间"（time-to-refresh）两个特征向模型提供采样调度信息。通过在三个递增因果耦合层级（开环滚动预测、闭环模型预测规划、线性锁存执行器系统）的实验，发现time-to-refresh的有效性取决于采样调度的因果角色——即刷新事件是影响系统本身还是仅报告系统状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习的世界模型通常假设观测同步到达（每个环境步骤返回完整状态向量），但物理传感以异构速率运行，绝大多数感知通道在任意时刻都是过期的。插值过期通道会引入从未观测到的测量值，而降采样到最慢传感器则丢弃有效测量值，需要一种更自然的替代方案来处理异步观测。

Method: 采用零阶保持策略保留最近读数，并通过两个额外特征（staleness和时间到刷新time-to-refresh）将已知采样调度信息编码到模型中。使用transformer世界模型，在三个因果耦合渐增的场景中进行测试：(1) 连续控制运动领域的开环滚动预测；(2) 闭环模型预测规划（每个学习模型作为规划器动力学）；(3) 线性锁存执行器系统（刷新事件将零阶保持命令施加到被控对象）。

Result: time-to-refresh特征的有效性取决于采样调度的因果角色：当刷新事件影响系统本身（而非仅仅报告系统状态）时，该特征提供有用信息；当刷新事件仅报告状态时其作用有限。

Conclusion: 这些结果明确了在异步物理观测条件下，采样调度信息何时能为预测性世界模型提供有用信号，为物理传感场景下世界模型的设计提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Learned world models typically assume that observations arrive synchronously, an abstraction inherited from simulators that return a complete state vector at each environment step. Physical sensing instead operates at heterogeneous rates, leaving most observation channels stale at any given instant. Interpolating stale channels introduces measurements that were never observed, while downsampling to the slowest sensor discards valid measurements. A natural alternative is to zero-order-hold the most recent reading and provide the known sampling schedule to the model through two features, staleness and time-to-refresh. We test this prediction using transformer world models across three regimes of increasing causal coupling: open-loop rollouts in continuous-control locomotion, closed-loop model-predictive planning in which each learned model serves as the planner dynamics, and a linear latched-actuator system in which refresh events apply a zero-order-held command to the plant. Our findings show that the effectiveness of time-to-refresh depends on the causal role of the sampling schedule, specifically when refresh events affect the system rather than merely report its state. These results establish when sampling schedules provide useful information for predictive world models operating under asynchronous physical observations.

</details>


### [286] [Weakly supervised neural network: segmentation of complex structures in X-ray microCT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07313)
*Daniele Rusconi,Michela Ascolese,Stephanie Fest-Santini,Alberto Bravin,Maurizio Santini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了X射线断层扫描数据中复杂结构分割的弱监督学习方法，基于nnU-Net框架采用点状稀疏标注结合少量全分割图像，在大鼠肾脏microCT图像中对肾小球进行分割，结果表明弱监督能提供有效学习信号，结合少量高质量标注即可接近全监督性能，是一种标注高效的分割策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线断层扫描数据中复杂结构（如肾小球）的分割是生物医学研究的基础任务，但全监督方法需要大量精确标注数据，成本高且难以规模化。为降低标注负担同时保持分割精度，研究者探索弱监督深度学习策略。

Method: 采用基于nnU-Net框架的二维卷积神经网络，将其适配到弱监督设置中。使用基于稀疏点的标注（点状标注）代替密集标注，并补充少量完全分割的图像作为训练数据。方法在大鼠肾脏高分辨率microCT切片上进行评估，目标是分割小而低对比度的肾小球结构。

Result: 弱监督提供了有意义的学习信号，即使在缺乏密集标签的情况下也能可靠定位肾小球。加入少量高质量全分割标注后，分割性能显著提升，接近全监督模型的表现。

Conclusion: 弱监督学习是分析X射线断层扫描数据中复杂结构的标注高效策略，具有广泛应用潜力。针对稀疏标注定制的替代损失函数可能进一步提升分割性能。

Abstract: Segmentation of complex structures in X-ray tomographic data is a fundamental task in biomedical research, but it often requires large amounts of precisely annotated data, making fully supervised approaches costly and difficult to scale. In this study, weakly supervised deep learning is investigated as a strategy to reduce annotation effort while maintaining accurate segmentation. A two-dimensional convolutional neural network based on the nnU-Net framework was adapted to a weak supervision setting using sparse dot-based annotations, complemented by a limited number of fully segmented images. The approach was evaluated on high-resolution microCT slices of rat kidneys, targeting the segmentation of renal glomeruli, which are small, low-contrast anatomical structures. Results indicate that weak supervision provides a meaningful learning signal, enabling reliable localization of glomeruli even in the absence of dense labels. Incorporating a small set of high-quality annotations substantially improves segmentation performance, approaching that of a fully supervised model. These findings highlight the potential of weakly supervised learning as an annotation-efficient strategy for the analysis of complex structures in X-ray tomographic data, and suggest that alternative loss formulations tailored to sparse annotations may further enhance performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [287] [ECHO: Dyadic 3D Facial Motion Generation with Asymmetric Deterministic Articulation and Stochastic Reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05506)
*Zhuoqiang Cai,Yujie Sun,Chaoyue Niu,Hongyun Yu,Zhiwen Chen,Chengfei Lv,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了ECHO模型，用于严格双流纯音频条件下的二元3D面部运动生成，将问题建模为非对称任务，通过将运动分解为确定性锚点（捕捉语音相关结构）和随机残差（建模一对多的听者反应），并引入Motion Memory作为训练期正则化器和语义组缩放控制残差注入，实现了说话侧发音保真与听者侧多样性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格纯音频双流（说话人和听者）条件下生成3D面部运动具有内在不对称性：说话人的面部运动受语音约束（确定性），而听者的反应存在一对多的不确定性。现有方法往往对整体运动施加均匀随机性，未区分确定性语音结构和不确定性交互动态，导致发音保真度或反应多样性不足。

Method: 1) 将3D面部运动分解为确定性锚点（稳定、与语音相关的结构）和随机残差（一对多交互动态）；2) Motion Memory作为仅训练期正则化器，在短时后期微调阶段为弱条件的听者窗口提供局部先验；3) 语义组缩放（semantic-group scaling）控制残差在表情、下颌和颈部不同区域上的注入强度。整个流程在单一生成过程中平衡发言方与听者两侧的需求。

Result: 统一评估、状态级评估和消融实验结果均表明，将稳定与不确定的组件进行分离优于对所有运动成分均匀施加随机性的方法，对话式3D运动生成获得了更好的效果。

Conclusion: ECHO提供了一种实用的建模框架和技术基础，使得在严格纯音频条件下部署可交互的对话数字人成为可能。其核心洞见是：分解确定性锚点与随机残差而非均匀施加随机性，是对对话式3D面部运动生成更有效的建模方式。

Abstract: We propose ECHO for dyadic 3D facial motion generation under a strict dual-stream audio-only setting, formulating the problem as an asymmetric task involving speech-constrained articulation and one-to-many listener reactions. To address this asymmetry, ECHO decomposes motion into a deterministic anchor that captures stable speech-correlated structure and a stochastic residual that models the remaining one-to-many interaction dynamics. On top of this backbone, Motion Memory acts as a training-only regularizer during brief late-stage fine-tuning to provide local priors for weakly conditioned listening windows, while semantic-group scaling controls residual injection across expression, jaw, and neck. This design balances speaking-side articulatory fidelity with listening-side realism and diversity in a single generation process. Results from unified, state-wise, and ablation evaluations show that conversational 3D motion benefits from decomposing stable and uncertain components rather than applying stochasticity uniformly. ECHO provides a practical formulation and technical basis for deployable conversational digital humans under strict audio-only conditions.

</details>


### [288] [RBF Your SDF: Radial Basis Function Interpolation of Signed Distance Fields with Implied Tangent Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06209)
*Yong Cheng,Yotam Gingold*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的隐式曲面重建方法，结合符号距离场（SDF）的切球结构与径向基函数（RBF）插值，利用所有样本数据（而非仅表面附近点）进行重建，并通过检测约束切点球来保留尖锐特征，同时采用分区统一分解实现大规模网格的高效扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SDF重建方法（如marching cubes、dual contouring）忽略了远离表面的样本携带的几何信息。近期基于切球结构的方法（Sellán et al. 2023等）虽然利用了所有样本，但最终通过表面重建提取零水平集，仅考虑表面上的点和法线，仍忽略了其余样本的信息。此外，这类方法在尖锐特征处会系统性圆角化。

Method: 将SDF切球观察与径向基函数（RBF）插值相结合，同时利用隐含的表面点和原始数据。通过检测具有极端约束切点的球（这种几何配置在尖锐表面特征处被强制出现），识别并保留表面重建方法系统性圆角的角点。采用分区统一（partition-of-unity）分解使方法能高效扩展到大的网格分辨率。

Result: 在测试的每个分辨率下，重建结果在Chamfer距离和Hausdorff距离精度上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过融合切球结构与RBF插值，并显式处理尖锐特征，所提出的方法在各分辨率下均能实现更精确的SDF曲面重建。

Abstract: Signed distance fields (SDFs) are a popular implicit representation of geometry. Converting a discrete set of SDF samples into an explicit surface is a fundamental problem in geometry processing. Traditional reconstruction methods such as marching cubes and dual contouring ignore the geometric information carried by samples far from the surface. Recently, Sellán et al.[2023] and several follow-up works leveraged the tangent-sphere structure of SDFs; every sample implies a point on a sphere tangent to the surface. However, these approaches extract the zero-level set via surface reconstruction, which considers only points and normals on the surface and ignores the remaining samples. We propose an approach that marries the tangent-sphere observation with radial basis function interpolation of all data, the implied surface points and the original data. By detecting spheres with extremely constrained tangent points, a configuration geometrically forced at sharp surface features, we identify and preserve surface corners that surface reconstruction-based methods systematically round. A partition-of-unity decomposition allows our method to scale efficiently to large grid resolutions. Our reconstructions improve both Chamfer and Hausdorff accuracy at every tested resolution.

</details>


### [289] [Skinned Motion Retargeting via Artifact-driven Kinematic Prior Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06517)
*Seokhyeon Hong,Chaelin Kim,Inseo Jang,Soojin Choi,Junyong Noh*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出一个几何感知的运动重定向框架，通过显式连接角色几何中观察到的伪影（如自穿透）与运动精修，同时保持骨架无关的神经重定向灵活性，在固定和任意骨架结构设置下均提升了运动精度并减少了几何伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经运动重定向方法虽提高了跨骨架的灵活性，但难以解决目标侧的几何伪影（如自穿透）。已有几何感知方法依赖固定骨架模板或隐式几何条件预测，要求单一网络同时处理目标几何变形、检测伪影并预测修正，难以泛化到多样的骨架结构和体型。

Method: 首先使用基于Transformer的重定向自编码器学习跨不同骨架共享的运动嵌入，实现任意源-目标骨架对的运动迁移；然后引入伪影驱动精修模块，通过运动到顶点雅可比矩阵将目标网格上观察到的自穿透转化为矫正信号；最后利用基于蒙皮权重的关节对齐几何特征（来自静止姿态网格）对运动解码进行几何条件化，统一了显式伪影推理与灵活的几何感知解码。

Result: 在固定和任意骨架结构设置下，该方法均提升了运动学重定向精度并减少了几何伪影，能在已见和未见的目标角色上产生合理的运动。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了将显式几何伪影推理与几何感知运动解码结合的统一框架，既保持了骨架无关神经重定向的灵活性，又能够有效解决目标侧的几何伪影问题，在多样骨架结构和体型上具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Motion retargeting aims to transfer a source motion to target characters with different skeletal structures, proportions, and body shapes. Although recent neural retargeting methods have improved flexibility across diverse skeletons, target-side geometric artifacts such as self-penetration remain difficult to resolve. Specifically, existing geometry-aware approaches often rely on fixed skeleton templates or implicit geometry-conditioned prediction, requiring a single network to account for target geometry deformation, detect target-side artifacts, and predict the corresponding correction from target geometry alone, which limits their ability to generalize across diverse skeleton structures and body shapes. In this paper, we present a geometry-aware motion retargeting framework that explicitly connects artifacts observed in the posed character geometry to motion refinement while preserving the flexibility of skeleton-agnostic neural retargeting. Our method first learns a motion embedding shared across different skeletons using a transformer-based retargeting autoencoder that transfers motion across arbitrary source--target skeleton pairs. Building on this kinematic motion prior, we introduce an artifact-driven refinement module that observes self-penetration on the posed target mesh and converts it into a corrective cue through a motion-to-vertex Jacobian. We further condition motion decoding on target geometry using skinning weight-based joint-aligned geometry features derived from the rest pose mesh. This design combines explicit target-side artifact reasoning with flexible geometry-aware decoding in a unified framework. Experiments on both fixed and arbitrary skeleton structure settings show that our method improves kinematic retargeting accuracy and reduces geometric artifacts, producing plausible motions across seen and unseen target characters.

</details>


### [290] [CT2Yarn: Yarn-Level Reconstruction of Crochet from Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06950)
*Chang Luo,Nobuyuki Umetani*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出CT2Yarn，一个人机协同框架，从真实钩针编织物的微计算机断层扫描中恢复单一连续纱线路径。通过Gabor滤波估计纤维方向、各向异性均值平移聚合纱线级方向、自动拓扑骨架化提取路径片段，并结合交互式草图界面处理歧义，支持物理仿真、绒毛级渲染和针法提取等下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钩针编织由单根纱线互锁环构成复杂三维形状，从外部观测难以恢复底层纱线路径，因为存在严重的自遮挡。虽然micro-CT能揭示内部完整结构，但体扫描本身不显式编码纱线的走向。难点源于纱线的层次结构：纱线由多股捻合而成，每股又由多根纤维捻成，因此局部纤维方向与整体纱线方向不一致。

Method: 首先使用Gabor滤波估计局部纤维方向并将体数据转换为带方向的点云；随后提出各向异性均值平移过程，在纱线半径邻域内将局部纤维方向聚合成纱线级方向估计；结合自动拓扑骨架化策略提取纱线路径片段；再通过片段连接、节点清理和环检测将骨架树合并成少量长曲线；对自动重建仍有歧义之处，提供基于草图的用户界面让用户交互式完成单一连续纱线路径。

Result: 恢复的纱线路径支持多种下游应用，包括基于物理的仿真、绒毛级（ply-level）渲染以及针法图案提取。

Conclusion: CT2Yarn通过结合自动计算与人工交互，有效解决了从micro-CT扫描中恢复钩针编织物单一连续纱线路径的难题，为真实编织物的数字化重建和分析提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce CT2Yarn, a human-in-the-loop framework for recovering a single continuous yarn path from micro-computed tomography (micro-CT) scans of real crochet objects. Crochet is a craft that creates complex three-dimensional shapes by interlocking loops formed from a single yarn. Recovering the underlying yarn path from external observations is challenging because of severe self-occlusion. While micro-CT reveals the full internal structure of a crochet object, the volumetric scan alone does not explicitly encode how the yarn traverses the object. This challenge stems from the hierarchical structure of yarn: a yarn consists of multiple twisted plies, and each ply itself consists of twisted fibers. Consequently, local fiber orientations observed in micro-CT scans are not aligned with the overall yarn direction. To recover yarn-level orientations from ply-level fiber orientations, we first estimate local fiber directions using Gabor filtering and convert the volume into an oriented point cloud. We then introduce an anisotropic mean-shift procedure that aggregates local fiber orientations within a neighborhood of the yarn radius into yarn-level orientation estimates. Combined with an automatic topology skeletonization strategy, our method extracts yarn-path fragments. Subsequent fragment linking, junction cleaning, and loop detection merge these trees into a small number of long curves. Where the automatic reconstruction remains ambiguous, a sketch-based user interface enables users to interactively complete the single continuous yarn path. The recovered yarn path enables downstream applications including physically based simulation, ply-level rendering, and stitch-pattern extraction.

</details>


### [291] [Neural Centroidal Voronoi Tessellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08497)
*Jiacheng Xu,Bo Pang,Rui Xu,Xiaocheng Zhang,Yang Liu,Fei Zhu,Guoping Wang,Peng-Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出Neural CVT，一种基于学习的曲面CVT求解器，通过图神经网络编码器和轻量级循环优化器替代传统昂贵的受限Voronoi图构造与积分计算，将CVT优化加速1-2个数量级，同时保持几何保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统曲面CVT求解器依赖反复构造受限Voronoi图（RVD）并在曲面单元上进行数值积分，这些几何计算代价高昂，成为高质量表面采样和各向同性重网格化的性能瓶颈。

Method: 首先在输入曲面上采样密集点云，用图神经网络编码器提取多尺度几何特征；随后一个轻量级循环优化器在少量迭代中细化种子位置，通过聚合插值后的表面特征与优化历史来预测每个种子的位移。该框架以自监督方式训练，使用促进均匀分布（及可选的特征对齐）的CVT目标函数。

Result: 在多种有机和CAD类形状的基准上，Neural CVT对未见过的几何形状、初始化策略和种子密度均展现出良好的泛化能力，相比最先进的离线优化方法，计算成本大幅度降低，同时产生可比的各向同性、特征保持的网格。

Conclusion: Neural CVT证明深度学习可以有效地替代传统曲面CVT中的昂贵几何计算，在保持几何保真度的同时实现数量级的加速，为表面重网格化提供了一条新的高效途径。

Abstract: Centroidal Voronoi tessellation (CVT) is a fundamental primitive for high-quality surface sampling and isotropic remeshing in computer graphics. However, computing surface CVTs with classical solvers remains expensive: each optimization step repeatedly constructs restricted Voronoi diagrams (RVDs) and integrates quantities over their surface cells. We introduce Neural CVT, a learning-based surface-CVT solver that replaces these costly geometric computations with a recurrent neural optimizer, accelerating CVT optimization by one to two orders of magnitude in our benchmarks while preserving geometric fidelity. Given an input surface, we sample a dense point cloud and extract multi-scale geometric descriptors with a graph neural encoder. A lightweight recurrent optimizer then refines seed positions over a small number of iterations, aggregating interpolated surface features and optimization history to predict per-seed displacements. The framework is trained self-supervised using CVT objectives that promote uniform distributions and, when desired, feature alignment. Across diverse organic and CAD-like shapes, Neural CVT generalizes to unseen geometry, initialization strategies, and seed densities, producing isotropic, feature-preserving remeshes comparable to state-of-the-art offline optimization methods at a fraction of the computational cost. Code and trained models will be released.

</details>


### [292] [Inverse Digital Marbling: Recovering Gesture Programs with a Replay Adjoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08722)
*Tianqi Liu,Yushan Han,Hang Liu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对数字大理石花纹（纸染/纸墨）沉积模型的可执行程序恢复方法：给定目标图像，优化一系列有序的胶囊插入操作，使其回放近似目标图案。该方法在2000个手势、1024²分辨率下比检查点自动微分方案节省8.7倍内存，单GPU约4分钟即可完成程序拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纸墨大理石纹中的颜料沉积会位移已有的花纹图案，使得每一次操作的视觉呈现都与后续动作耦合。这种复杂的耦合关系使得逆向恢复生成程序变得困难——但可编辑程序是数字媒介创作中重要的能力，因此作者希望从目标图像反向恢复出可执行的生成程序。

Method: 核心贡献包括：(1) 胶囊插入作为基本原语，连续连接圆形墨滴与拉伸沉积物；(2) 传输严格面积守恒，且在沉积区域外部具有闭式逆变换；(3) 提出replay adjoint方法重建中间状态，保留沉积内部坐标损失并通过周期性位置检查点来稳定优化；(4) 在PyTorch中实现了高效的回放，并与检查点自动微分方案在内存和速度上对比；(5) 对恢复的程序进行多种评估，包括与禁用传输的拟合、单遍几何补偿、已发表基于笔画的方法进行对比。

Result: PyTorch回放实现在2000手势、1024²像素下比检查点自动梯度配置节省8.7倍内存且步进时间相近；融合实现在单工作站GPU上约4分钟完成程序拟合。在五张大理石花纹纸上评估了图像重建质量。恢复的程序可在4倍线性分辨率范围内回放。按程序顺序或调色板空间指定的编辑可以正确回放，但按图像内容指定的编辑（如移动图案主体）则不行。在已知生成程序的合成目标上，恢复的程序虽匹配图像，但在位置匹配统计上不匹配原始生成手势。

Conclusion: 本文成功实现了对数字大理石花纹生成过程的程序级逆向恢复，输出为可在原始数字媒介中编辑和回放的可执行程序。恢复的程序保持了媒介的可编辑性（程序序/调色板编辑有效）但非所有图像内容级编辑都可行，且恢复的手势与原生成手势在位置统计上不同——表明这是一个功能等价而非精确恢复的逆问题求解。

Abstract: Pigment deposition in paper marbling displaces the pattern already present, coupling the appearance of each gesture to later actions. We recover executable programs for a deposition-based digital marbling model: given a target image, we optimise an ordered program of capsule insertions whose replay approximates it. The capsule primitive continuously joins circular drops to elongated deposits. Its transport is exactly area-preserving and has a closed-form inverse on the exterior of the deposited region. A replay adjoint reconstructs intermediate states, retaining coordinates lost inside deposits and periodic position checkpoints. At 2000 gestures and 1024^2 pixels, the PyTorch replay implementation uses 8.7x less memory than the tested checkpointed-autograd configuration at comparable step time; the fused implementation fits a program in about four minutes on one workstation GPU. We evaluate image reconstruction on five marbled sheets, compare against transport-disabled fitting, one-pass geometric compensation and a published stroke-based fitter at matched stroke count, and measure sensitivity to an alternative ordered-drop transport. Recovered programs replay across a 4x range of linear resolution. Edits specified in program order or in palette space -- inserting a gesture, recolouring a stage, translating a stage -- replay correctly under the same model; edits specified by image content, such as moving a motif, do not. On synthetic targets with known generating programs, the recovered programs match the images but not the generating gestures under a positional matching statistic. The output is an editable program in the specified digital medium.

</details>
